Build/restart verified; served asset `/deadcity-seed.glb` returns 200 and served staging bundle contains the `dead-city-glb` path. Headless visual/runtime proof remains unreliable for the heavy GLB path; use device check for the look.
- **Original cloud shell beauty pass + scan clearing (Jul 8)**: Staging `/tinyworld-staging?phantom=cloud` keeps the original continuous voxel cloud shell as the default (CloudPack lump buildings remain opt-in via `?cb=1`). The shell shader now borrows the best CloudPack techniques without replacing the massing: stronger CloudPack-style height gradient (`uGrad=0.7`, `uGradBase=-4`, `uGradH=48`), soft fuzzy rim/edge glow (`uRim=0.22`), and gentler erosion (`uErode=0.22`, `uDetail=0.12`) applied mostly at face rims via the quad-corner edge field so the mass stays continuous instead of hollowing out. Live tuning: `__tw.tiles.cloud({ rim, grad, gradBase, gradH, erode, detail, edge, edgeMin, opacity, ... })`. Cloud-only scan clearing now removes phantom cloud columns in a halo around the scan carve footprint so the reclaimed/scanned area has readable open air; default `?cc=3.5` meters, disable with `?cc=0`, tune by reloading with another `?cc=` value. Build passed, zo-space restarted, served bundle `tinyworld-staging-D4sw0uBw.js` contains `uGradBase`, `uRim`, and `cloudClear`; `get_space_errors` clean. Visual/device check still needed because headless cloud screenshots are unreliable.
- **Cloud buildings experiment is opt-in (Jul 7/8)**: KJ supplied a real extracted Unity asset pack under `assets/clouds/CloudPack Voxel` (not the earlier Unity Asset Store webpage dump). `Clouds_V.fbx` was converted to GLB; the runtime asset served by zo.space is `/cloud-pieces.glb`, sourced from `assets/clouds/cloud-pieces-lo.glb` (79KB GLB, 9 pre-authored cloud lump meshes). The first CloudPack cloud-building pass looked worse than the original soft voxel cloud shell, so staging now defaults back to the original cloud layer: `/tinyworld-staging?phantom=cloud` does **not** load or drop in cloud-building pieces unless `?cb=1` is present. The experiment remains available for side-by-side tuning: `?cb=1`, `?cbb=` massing cell meters, `?cbh=` min height, `?cbmax=` instance budget. `__tw.tiles.info()` includes `cloudBuildings` when the experiment runs. Best follow-up direction: keep the original voxel shell silhouette/continuity and selectively borrow CloudPack techniques (height gradient, fuzzy rim, gentler erosion), rather than replacing city masses with separate GLB lumps.
- **Two-tier leaf glow — regular weak / super-tree beacon (Jul 6)**: KJ: "make all trees glow — but very weak glow for regular trees" (super strong implied; the beacon that "pushes back the void"). All leaves already glowed uniformly; now two-tier in ONE shared leaves InstancedMesh via a per-instance `aSuper` flag (0=weak, 1=strong), not a layer split (avoids settle/shadow/save edge cases; `addLayer` already clones geometry + wires per-instance attrs like `treeShade`). **Detection** (`src/tw-staging-app.tsx`, in `addLayer`'s leaf branch): saved worlds merge all leaves into one blob and don't preserve which came from the super tree, and leaves are stride-subsampled (TREE_BUDGET) so direct 6-neighbor adjacency is shattered — so re-derive the super canopy by **coarse-cell (3-voxel) union-find** over leaf positions, promote the single dominant cluster, guarded by a **dominance test (≥300 leaves AND ≥1.8× the runner-up)** so an ambiguous world degrades safely to all-weak rather than crowning a random grove. **Shader** (the AO onBeforeCompile wrapper, which composes with the leaf foliage shader via `_prevCompile`): adds `vSuper` varying + uniforms `uLeafNight` (0 day / 1 night, driven by the existing render-loop `applyLeafGlow` toggle) / `uLeafGlowWeak=0.30` / `uLeafGlowStrong=1.8`; the emissive line `gl_FragColor.rgb += vec3(0.184,0.365,0.227) * uLeafNight * mix(uLeafGlowWeak,uLeafGlowStrong,vSuper)` is appended AFTER the AO/treeShade multiply so the beacon bypasses AO darkening. Grown-canopy mats (`makeGrowable`, plain MeshStandardMaterial, no per-instance data) take the plain-emissive weak path in `applyLeafGlow` (`isShaderLeaf` false → emissive 0x16281b). Levers: `__tw.setLeafGlow(weak, strong)` (live retune, no rebuild — pass undefined to hold a tier), `__tw.superReport()` (`{leaves, superLeaves, dominant, second, promoted}` — which canopy was crowned). Built + restarted; served chain verified: index `index-DjHXYdTv.js` → `tinyworld-staging-CYbztTuD.js`, literals `aSuper`/`uLeafGlowStrong`/`setLeafGlow` present, `get_space_errors` clean. **Detection premise (largest connected canopy = super strangler-fig) is NOT offline-verified against KJ's real world data** (233K wedges headless) — dominance guard mitigates; the super beacon vs weak-grove contrast needs KJ's on-device night check. **Design (Part B, captured not wired):** KJ's follow-on ruling — "when glow matter is attached to a void ship it blinks, dissipates, and scatters matter; bigger ship needs more light material" — recorded in `docs/void-motes-ship-and-moon.md` v11 §4.1 (glow matter = the world's counter-force; revises §4's "ship can't be destroyed" to a non-violent light path; scales with ship mass) + open calls #38 (glow-matter harvest/delivery verb), #39 (is the super tree a progression gate?), #40 (does the void island breathe with the nightly tug-of-war?).
- **Repo**: https://github.com/kevinjohanwong/project-tinyworld (this folder)
- **Docs**: README, NYC temperate biome spec, persistence/access spec — done
- **Ownership protocol (Jun 26)**: Before touching Tinyworld, declare one work zone from `docs/ownership-map.md` (Scan Pipeline; Terrain Rendering; Player/Sentinel/Drone; Characters/Villagers; Geo/Access/Persistence; Game Systems). Edit only that zone in a pass. If another zone is implicated, stop and split the work. This prevents scan-pipeline, terrain, character, geolocation, and Sentinel changes from overwriting each other inside the huge route files.
- **Staging route persistence (Jun 27)**: The `/tinyworld-staging` route lives in `/__substrate/space/.routes.json` (the zo.space route DB), NOT just on disk. A disk-only edit gets wiped to a 4-line `TinyworldStagingStub` on every space sync (`/__substrate/space/.sync-done` timestamp marks syncs). To make staging edits persist: (1) edit the disk file at `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx`, (2) register the code in `.routes.json` (strip the `// CODE GENERATED BY SPACE TOOLS` header line, set `{path, type:"page", code, public:false, meta:null}`), (3) `bun run build` + `supervisorctl -c /etc/zo/supervisor.conf restart zo-space`. The `reapply-staging.sh` script does all three from the latest verified-good backup. The production `/tinyworld` route is already in `.routes.json` (~397KB) and survives syncs without this step. A space sync that ran at 23:02 UTC wiped a full day's beauty-pass work (shadows/bloom/fog/veg/1.5× density/Progressive Scan regex) because staging was only on disk — all recovered from the 22:28 backup and re-registered.
- **Staging route persistence — PERMANENT FIX (Jun 28)**: Root cause of the recurring stub-wipe is a SIZE LIMIT in the backend route version store. On reboot/sync the hostagent feeds the backend store's route array to `space-sync.ts`, which clears `routes/pages` + `routes/api` and rewrites every route from that array (it does NOT touch `src/`, `lib/`, `components/`). When a route's stored code exceeds the store's per-route cap it is replaced by a 4-line `…Stub` returning null. Production `/tinyworld` (~395K chars) is under the cap and persists; staging grew to ~423K and kept getting stubbed. **Fix:** the `/tinyworld-staging` route is now a 2-line re-export shell (`import App from "@/tw-staging-app"; export default App;`) pushed to the store via `write_space_route`. The real code lives in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx` (the component) + `src/tw-staging-core.tsx` (module-level consts/types/helpers/Joystick), imported via the `@` → `./src` vite/tsconfig alias. `src/` is never cleared by sync, so it survives reboot; the tiny shell is permanently under the cap so it can never be stubbed again. Future staging growth must go into the `src/` modules, never back into the route file. The old `reapply-staging.sh` workaround (re-register full code in `.routes.json` after each wipe) is now superseded.
- **Production route — SHELL-ROUTE PATTERN (Jun 28)**: `/tinyworld` is now the SAME re-export shell as staging. Earlier notes saying "production is in `.routes.json` (~397KB) and survives syncs" are OUTDATED — that was only true until a reboot. The authoritative route store is REMOTE (`entrypoint.sh` boots with `curl https://api.zo.computer/space/routes | bun run /__substrate/space-sync.ts`); local `.routes.json` is just a cache. A disk-only edit to `routes/pages/tinyworld.tsx` survives a `supervisorctl restart` but a full REBOOT rewrites it from the remote store and reverts. **Fix applied:** the remote store entry for `/tinyworld` is now the 2-line shell `import App from "@/tinyworld-app"; export default App;` (pushed via `write_space_route` → `Synced 46 routes`; public preserved). The real component lives in `src/tinyworld-app.tsx` (the full former route body, GENERATED banner stripped, `export default function TinyWorld()`), imported via the `@`→`./src` alias. `src/` is never cleared by sync and persists across reboot. **DEPLOY CONTRACT GOING FORWARD:** edit `src/tinyworld-app.tsx` (NEVER `routes/pages/tinyworld.tsx` — it's a generated shell that gets overwritten), then `bun run build` + `supervisorctl -c /etc/zo/supervisor.conf restart zo-space`. This is now reboot-durable WITHOUT re-pushing the store, because the stored shell is stable and forever points at `src/`. Only re-run `write_space_route` if the shell/import path itself changes. Verified: store now returns 55-char shell (was 403K), bundle hash unchanged (`tinyworld-28bKAbHy.js`, byte-identical → behavior preserved), route serves 200.
- **Debug world (Jul 4, expanded 4x Jul 5)**: `/tinyworld-staging?debugWorld=1` loads a synthetic 96×96 (~28k blocks) world with no GPS/network/scan dependency and a landing-screen `DEBUG WORLD` button. It is the DEFAULT headless-verification target (KJ, Jul 5) — saved scan worlds (200k+ blocks) wedge software GL and are device-test only. Jul 5 additions reproduce the scan-world bug classes headless: a broad pillared gray ROOF (y=14, x 56–80 / z 8–28) over walkable grass with a tree underneath (the colTop/overhang fingerprint — worker vanish, roof-snapped destinations), and a walkable cone HILL (peak +4 at (73,71), slope ≤1/cell) with 3 slope trees + 1 flat-ground tree (A*/surface lookups on non-flat terrain). The debug world intentionally includes floating wall/tree components so `groundSavedLayers()` proves load-time grounding; verified console marker: `load grounding: 5 comps, 2 floating, 2 settled (22 vox), 0 left in place`. It also exposes enough nearby terrain/table blocks to verify build-mode PICK/PLACE/ERASE via `__tw.build` without device input.
- **Golden browser playtest harness (Jul 12)**: `/tinyworld-staging?playtest=full` builds a deterministic staging-only **369,177-block, 112 m-wide world at 0.5 m/voxel** without GPS, persistence, or saved scans. It contains multiple building shells, a large supported overhang, rolling terrain + a ridge, pond, deliberately unsupported debris, 13 regular trees, and one dominant super-tree canopy. A visible `GOLDEN PLAYTEST` panel provides named checkpoints (`spawn`, `buildings`, `overhang`, `hill`, `pond`, `debris`, `superTree`), `RUN SMOKE`, `RUN FULL`, reset, and an explicit `GRASS STRESS` test; decorative grass is off during normal runs because ~494k animated blades made Zo Browser unresponsive, but all grass voxels remain. URL automation avoids unreliable AI clicking on busy WebGL pages: append `&autorun=smoke` or `&autorun=full`. Runtime API: `__tw.playtest.{checkpoints,goto,snapshot,run,reset,last}`. The report records scale, FPS/jitter, loop error, workers, conservation ledger/actual-world drift, super-tree detection, grass, assertions, visited checkpoints, and pass/fail. **Zo Browser validation:** smoke auto-run completed across spawn→overhang→superTree; representative scale, 0.5 m scale, renderer-alive, no-loop-error, ledger-balanced/zero-drift, and super-tree-detected all passed. Zo Browser's remote WebGL tab reported 1 FPS even while correctness checks completed, so FPS is a separate performance warning/device metric, not a core pass gate. Keep `?debugWorld=1` for fast logic iteration; use golden playtest as the browser release gate.
- **Build mode verified (Jul 4)**: Staging build mode now guards Sentinel pointer-lock/render ownership while `buildModeRef.current` is true, preventing late pointer-lock `lock` events from re-enabling normal walking and dragging the build bot anchor. Verified on `/tinyworld-staging?debugWorld=1`: `__tw.build.pick(22,2,23)` + `pick(22,2,24)` drained to stock (`grass:2`), a ghost at `(24,3,22)` added then erased cleanly, and `add(24,3,23)` + `add(25,3,23)` drained the place queue and placed both blocks with `loopError:false`.
- **Worker readability (Jul 4)**: KJ reported the worker + mech appeared briefly then disappeared/read too small. Staging now uses `WORKER_VISUAL_SCALE = 1.5` in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`: mecha01 target height is 3.6 voxels (was 2.4), mote/spirit scale and locator base scale follow the same multiplier, and worker A* requires 4 clear headroom cells to avoid clipping with the larger suit. Runtime debug-world GLB scale log increased from ~0.027 to ~0.038.
- **Worker vanish ROOT CAUSE — overhang teleport (Jul 5)**: The 1.5× scale did NOT fix KJ's "mech + emote appear briefly then disappear" — the worker was being TELEPORTED. The idle ground-resync pinned `w.vy = colTop(w.vx, w.vz)` (absolute column max). The spawn cell is protected (skips cells with structure above), but `aStarOnGround` legitimately walks workers UNDER overhangs (any cell with 4 clear voxels above), and KJ's saved scan world has a huge gray voxel overhang over walkable grass. First idle tick after a harvest under it → worker (+docked spirit + speech bubble) snapped to the overhang ROOF, out of frame. Fix in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`: new `colSurfaceNear(vx, vz, vyRef)` (nearest y in column with air above, closest to vyRef; degrades to exactly `colTop` on solid columns) replaces `colTop` in the worker flow: idle resync + neighbor-step escape, MoveTo/Place/Build/Interact dest selection, placing arrival (`placeVy`), build `baseTops` anchor, and built-structure `topY`. Void-creature colTop sites (dark-mote drop, void ship) intentionally untouched. Verify on KJ's world `?world=w_mr6ve9z6_xd17e4` — 233K blocks wedges headless, so device confirmation required. **CONFIRMED by KJ (Jul 5 screenshot): mech visible and grounded on the grass beside the overhang structure in the loaded scan world.**
- **ONE-GRID RULE (Jul 5, KJ design invariant)**: a worker may only ever move ONE grid cell per tick — NEVER teleport, under any circumstance. Enforced in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`: (1) idle resync walks vertical corrections 1 cell/tick (down = controlled fall any depth; up only when actually buried, i.e. block at `w.vy+1`; a floating worker under a far roof never levitates to it — it neighbor-steps or uses the escape-block); (2) neighbor-step escape requires the target surface within ±1 vertical; (3) the old "teleport-rescue" (warp up to 12 cells to nearest `topAt` after 4 failed wanders — could land ON the overhang roof) is now an inch-rescue: one validated grid step per tick toward the nearest open cell with relaxed 1-cell headroom, `wanderFails` stays ≥4 so it keeps inching until a real path succeeds. Initial spawn/save-restore placement is exempt (placement, not movement). If no legal one-cell step exists the worker stays put and retries — stuck beats teleporting. Any future code writing `w.vx/vy/vz` outside A* path steps must respect this.
- **Live grass rebuild + load-time floater sweep (Jul 5)**: Two fixes in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. **(1) Grass tufts now follow voxel state.** The billboard-grass field was a load-time snapshot fully decoupled from grass voxels — a fallen grass voxel left its tufts floating, placed grass grew no tufts, mined grass kept lingering blades. `buildBillboardGrass` was refactored into reusable pieces: hoisted pure noise (`grassVNoise`) + a per-top **seeded** RNG (`makeGrand`, seeded from x,z,dry,density so a chunk regenerates byte-identical blades regardless of order — the invariant that makes single-chunk rebuild seamless), `collectGrassTops`, `genBladesForTop`, `grassChunkIndexWorld` (buckets each grass voxel to ONE chunk by its top center), `buildGrassChunkMesh`, `disposeGrassChunk`. Field-wide params (perTop, floorY/highY, grid bounds, shared material) are cached in `grassParams`. New `markGrassDirty(vx,vz)` + `flushGrassRebuild()` do a **throttled (150 ms debounce), per-chunk** rebuild — only the affected chunk(s) regenerate, NOT the whole field (KJ approved "throttled chunk rebuild"). Hooked into `spawnBlockInto` (place), `removeBlockFrom` (mine), and the settle **landing** in `animateFallingBlocks` (a grass block that falls rebuilds its chunk when it lands, so tufts re-seat at the new height rather than mid-drop). `setGrass(false)` clears the pending timer/dirty set. **(2) Load-time floater sweep.** Root cause of "floating blocks in the edge": the support-settle only fires on a block REMOVAL, so scan-time photogrammetry floaters never drop. Offline census on KJ's world `w_mr6ve9z6_xd17e4` (233K blocks): **5,917 floating blocks in 3,488 disconnected clusters** — 2,557 singles + 790 clumps(2–4), 678 near edges / 2,810 interior, median 56 vox above ground; larger floaters (>8 vox) include real ceiling(69)/wall(37), so blanket ≤320 deletion would be destructive. New `surveyFloaters(cap)` (full uncapped flood per component, classify grounded/edge) + `settleFloaters({deleteMax,cap})` run once after `groundRef` is built. **UPDATED default (KJ 2026-07-05: "on build needs to clear the floating blocks — afterwards it's an updating ledger"): the build-time sweep now clears EVERY unsupported floater up to the settle cap (`deleteMax = SETTLE_COMP_CAP = 320`), not just ≤4 specks.** Trees/water still exempt; masses >cap are grounded by the float rule and kept. The earlier conservative ≤4 default is SUPERSEDED — KJ wants the world clean on load and accepts that mid-size disconnected clusters (incl. possibly-real scanned ceiling/wall <320 vox) get cleared. Deletes are conservation-safe world→void ledger moves, but the sweep runs BEFORE the authoritative conservation-ledger recount (`~line 8287`, same overwrite path that folds catch-up erosion), so cleared floaters simply drop out of the baseline — the world starts balanced-clean and the ledger updates live from there (exactly the "afterwards it's an updating ledger" model). Autosaves (one-time, idempotent). Disable via `?floaters=0`. Levers: `__tw.floaterReport()` (survey only, deletes nothing), `__tw.settleFloaters(deleteMax=320, cap?)` (manual re-run / dial-back after edits). Census logged as `[tinyworld] floater sweep: {...}`. Built + restarted; served chunk `tinyworld-staging-FHRL7p0N.js` (verified served chain, and minified `deleteMax:ei` with `ei=320`). Both need device confirmation on the loaded scan world (233K wedges headless): grass follows a mined/placed/fallen voxel; on load the world should be visibly free of floating blocks and console shows the floater census + `deleted` count.
- **Worker inert near overhangs — topAt dest fix (Jul 5)**: KJ's voice test: chirps fired but the mote never moved. Root cause is the same roof-vs-surface defect in every `topAt` path DESTINATION: (1) `greetPlayer` probe cells around the player resolved to the overhang roof → all aStar probes failed → silent "waves from afar" fallback, no walk; (2) WANDER random targets (R=6) under the roof always failed → worker sat inert and racked up wanderFails; (3) night walk-to-doorway failed for a hut under the roof → worker went dormant instead of resting; (4) dawn arrivals would spawn on the roof. All four now use `colSurfaceNear(x, z, refY)` (ref = worker's vy; dawn arrivals use an occupant's vy stashed on the homes entry). `topAt` remains only in the player block-aim fallback, which is intentional. Deployed bundle `tinyworld-staging-Bf-ekekX.js`.
- **Energy-as-currency + personality burn policy (Jul 5)**: Wired the locked energy/drive/personality design (`/home/workspace/TINYWORLD_ENERGY_DRIVE_DESIGN.md`) into `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. The worker AI was already a utility-AI drive stack (`makeMotivationPlan` scores each candidate action's `u:`, highest wins), with MBTI traits `{ei,sn,tf,jp}` already deterministic per worker — but energy *drained and nothing read it* and the `u:` values were fixed constants (switches, not gradients). Three-layer model now: **(a) Energy is a currency, not a survival panic switch** — two jobs: a tempo-governor (`paceMul`, already consumed in every action-duration calc at ~7150–7320) AND a per-action cost. New `ACTION_COST={walking:.8,picking:.7,mining:1.0,placing:1.4}`; the working-mode drain (in `tickWorkers`, the day branch) is now `dt*W_DRAIN_PER_MS*ACTION_COST[mode]*burnRate(w)` (was a flat `dt*W_DRAIN_PER_MS`), and a new daytime `idle` breather trickles energy back (`W_REST_PER_MS*0.16*(1.35-0.7*sprintiness)`) so recovery is a mid-day investment too, not night-only. **(b) Two personality knobs** (helpers next to `paceMul` at ~6534): `workEthic(w)=0.5+0.5*jp` (lazy↔driven — output ceiling + how low it runs reserve) and `sprintiness(w)=0.5+0.5*ei` (marathon↔sprint — spend rate + drawdown depth); `burnRate(w)=(0.7+0.6*sprintiness)*(0.9+0.2*workEthic)`. `paceMul` is now personality-aware: `lo=0.95-0.35*sprintiness; 1.6-(1.6-lo)*fillFrac` (sprinters keep a brisker floor pace when full, everyone → 1.6× when empty). **(c) Drives became pressure×fit gradients** — hunger was a binary `hungry` bool (fixed u4 eat/u3.5 graze); now a smooth ramp `hungerP=clamp((sinceMeal/W_MEAL_INTERVAL_MS-0.6)/0.9, 0, 1.4)` with `u=1.6+2.6*hungerP` (eat) / `1.3+2.2*hungerP` (graze), so eating gradually outranks work and *overshoots* everything when starving instead of flipping on. Growth drives gated by ambition×energy: homestead expansions scale by `(0.6+0.8*workEthic)*buildEnergyF` where `buildEnergyF=min(1, fillFrac/0.35 + 0.6*sprintiness)` (tired-lazy won't launch an expensive build; sprinters who mean to dip deep still build), larder + homestead restock scale by `(0.7+0.6*workEthic)`. **Shelter drive stays UNGATED (survival non-negotiable); conservation ledger + executor untouched; NO LLM added to the tick loop** (stays a demoted occasional goal-setter/reflector, per design). Debug lever `__tw.shelterReport()` per-worker rows extended with `workEthic`/`sprintiness`/`burn`. TDZ-safe: `makeMotivationPlan` (~5400) forward-references the energy helpers (~6534) but only at tick time, after init. Built + restarted; served chain verified — index `index-BZPZQHUk.js` matches disk, index chunk imports `tinyworld-staging-DuhTfOTH.js`, served staging chunk contains the new `sprintiness` report key. Deterministic layer is headless-observable via `__tw.shelterReport()`/`setEnergy()`, but the *feel* (personalities diverging over a day, tired workers slowing, hunger ramping) needs KJ's on-device check (233K worlds wedge headless).
- **Per-worker perception scales with tier + higher base (Jul 5)**: KJ: "radius and memory by tier with a higher base tier — so better decisions can be made and they can see what they are building." The per-worker spatial memory (a COARSE chunk-keyed overlay `Map<chunkKey,MapCell>`, `MapCell={s,f,g,d}`, chunk=8 voxels — NOT a second voxel grid, v1 data-only, no planner/LLM consumption yet) previously revealed exactly ONE chunk per step (sight=1 cell) and capped memory at a flat 96 chunks with NO tier scaling. Now three module-scope tables + helpers in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx` (placed at module scope so BOTH the component-scope `saveCurrentWorld` path AND the scene-effect discovery hooks can read them): `MAP_SIGHT_R_BY_TIER=[0,3,4,6,8]`, `MAP_MEM_CAP_BY_TIER=[0,96,128,192,320]`, `clampTier`, `mapSightR(tier)`, `mapMemCap(tier)`. **Base Mote (spawn tier 2): sight radius 4 voxels (9×9 patch), memory cap 128 chunks. Elder (tier 4): radius 8 (17×17), cap 320.** `recordDiscovery`'s `"visit"`/seen branch now stamps a bounding-box PATCH of chunks over `mapSightR(w.tier)` voxels (was single-cell); resource sightings (fruit/grass/dirt) stay SINGLE-cell (harvest ground-truth). `pruneWorkerMap` + the save-path `serializeWorkerMap(w.map, mapMemCap(w.tier))` both use the tier cap. `__tw.workerMap(id?)`/`mapReport()` levers now report `tier`/`sightR`/`cap`. **Design call: spawn tier stays 2/Mote — did NOT raise it, because tier also drives energy cap (`W_TIER_CAP`) + sprite scale (`W_TIER_SCALE`); "higher base" is interpreted as a generous perception FLOOR, not a bigger/hungrier worker.** This is perception plumbing only — the follow-up that turns it into "better decisions" is hooking the map into `makeMotivationPlan` (currently the planner doesn't read `w.map`). Built + restarted; served chain verified — index `index-DM0FJjN4.js` matches disk, index chunk imports `tinyworld-staging-BrEdLtt-.js`, served staging chunk contains the `sightR` literal. Deterministic + headless-inspectable via `__tw.mapReport()`/`workerMap()`, but the felt effect (workers revealing/remembering a wider patch, tier divergence) needs KJ's on-device check (233K worlds wedge headless).
- **Tree baked AO / pre-shading (Jul 4)**: Staging tree layers now use a real baked-AO shader path: the Minecraft-style per-vertex AO multiply was moved after Three's `output_fragment` so it is not overwritten, and `trunks` / `leaves` get a deterministic `treeShade` instanced attribute for subtle root/interior/canopy darkening. Applies to regular trees and the super tree because both render through the shared tree voxel layers. Verified on `/tinyworld-staging?debugWorld=1` with served chunk `tinyworld-staging-wDqPc4P5.js`, `loopError:null`, `window.__twTreeAoStats.leaves = { count: 38232, min: 0.714, max: 1.04, avg: 0.931 }`, `trunks = { count: 1768, min: 0.74, max: 0.996, avg: 0.911 }`.
- **Voice-to-spirits / talk-to-worker (Jul 4)**: hold-to-talk mic → Gemini STT → worker suggestion system, live on `/tinyworld-staging`. Desktop "Hold to talk" HUD pill + mobile "Talk" button (next to Jump) record via MediaRecorder (`audio/webm;codecs=opus` → `audio/webm` → `audio/mp4` for iOS) and POST base64 audio to the new API route `/api/tinyworld-stt` (Gemini `gemini-2.5-flash-lite` audio + intent extraction in one call; returns `{transcript, intent:{kind: gather|build|come|plant|eat|unknown, layer?, structure?, near?}}`; keyword fallback `parseIntentLocal` if the route fails). Reaction beats in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`: (1) on mic-release all awake workers chirp "heard" + focused eyes, halo pulses with live mic amplitude (`AnalyserNode` → `__tw.voiceAmp`); (2) nearest awake worker enters a questioning phase — "?" bubble (moteSay now anchors to the drifting spirit, not the parked suit), head-tilt eyes, rising chirp — masking STT latency; (3) it unconditionally greets: pathfinds to a walkable cell radius 2–4 around the player and WAVES (new single-frame arm sprite with shoulder-pivot rotation; resting workers wave in place, night rest is sacred); (4) tier-scaled comprehension `W_COMPREHEND=[0,.15,.4,.7,.95]` — understood commands become `w.suggestion` (120 s TTL) which both injects u=3.4 options into `makeMotivationPlan` (voice-gather/build/plant/eat/graze) and appends a "THE PLAYER SPOKE TO YOU" line to the LLM goal; compat>0.2 housemates catch it via contagion (60 %). Greet plans are protected from in-flight LLM plan-fetch races. Levers: `__tw.voice(text, intent?)` (bypass mic), `voiceThinking()`, `voiceMic(on)`, `voiceAmp(a)`, `voiceLayers()`, `voiceReport()`; dead `setGoal` lever removed. Verified headless: TTS-generated speech "go gather leaves near the big tree" → route → `{kind:"gather", layer:"leaves"}`; Elder understood → plan `gatherLeavesAndBuildHut`; Speck → confused beat; wave arm confirmed in pixels. NOT pixel-confirmed headless: the "?" bubble over a resting spirit (anchor fix deployed; eyeball on device). Deferred: `near` landmark resolution only flows through the LLM goal text, no per-worker earshot radius.
- **Prototype backend**: live on kj.zo.space as API routes (NOT in this repo — see "Live prototype" below)
- **DB**: `data/tinyworld.db` (SQLite v0) — tables: worlds, block_events, bridges, structures, world_blocks. **Wiped clean Jun 22, 2026** (0 worlds, 0 blocks) to clear 25 stale test worlds and resolve the orphaned-worker 502 storm. Full pre-wipe backup preserved at `data/tinyworld.db.wipe-backup-20260622T193040Z`. Schema unchanged; reseed from real camera captures going forward (no more synthetic test worlds).
- **`src/` and `ios/`**: empty and **intentionally sunset** — the Rust input module and iOS LiDAR capture app are abandoned (see decision #3 amendment). Ingest is phone video via the `/tinyworld` page, finalized by the OpenMVS/GLB path (`tools/old_path_from_capture.py` -> `tools/glb_to_world_payload.py`). The Depth/RGB-D TSDF script is removed from the active project and must not be reintroduced as fallback/debug. The live route + API routes live on kj.zo.space (edit via space tools, not these folders).
- **`tools/server_worker.ts`**: legacy standalone/offline prototype, not the live worker loop. It still has an `inventory` column model and should not be used as design truth; the live staging route now uses the conserved `CARRIED` pool + physical stockpile model.
- **Active focus now (Jun 23)**: first playable onboarding loop. The priority is: scan/camera capture -> island rises -> orb gives only 1-2 lines -> player pilots the small flying body -> first block pick/place/snap works -> player is prompted to scan additional space -> the second scan triggers the first Void consequence. Fun/BotW-style freedom is the primary layer; melancholy tiny survivors are the emotional layer; the Void is tension, not the first-experience dominant tone. The world should lightly simulate in the player's absence so villagers keep living/building while the Sentinel sleeps.
- **Drone full collision (Jun 25)**: The drone previously had ZERO collision ("unlimited flight by design") — both the walk horizontal block (`if (!isDrone && !wasStuck)`) and the gravity/ground-clamp block (`if (!isDrone && !recoveringFromStuck)`) skip drone mode, so it flew straight through terrain. Per KJ, drone now gets FULL collision while keeping free, gravity-less flight. In the walk/drone movement loop (`/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld.tsx`, ~line 7124+) added `oldY` to the pre-move position capture, then after the walk horizontal block added a drone-only block that resolves each axis independently against `bodyBlockedAtY`: if moving to (newX, droneY, oldZ) embeds the body cylinder → revert X; same for Z; then revert Y (thrust/bob) if the resolved column is blocked. Net: the drone can fly freely through open air and the void, but cannot pass through walls/cliffs/floors/ceilings, and rests ~2.4 voxel above a surface (the forgiven body-cylinder bottom). No gravity — it hovers where left. Per-frame steps (~0.23 voxel) are < body radius so no tunneling. Walk/sentinel physics untouched (sentinel already grounded + wall-collided since Jun 24). Deployed: edit on disk → `bun run build` (clean, 4.74s, bundle `tinyworld-BoF0KSju.js`) → `supervisorctl -c /etc/zo/supervisor.conf restart zo-space`; page serves 200, `.errors` empty. No `/tinyworld-staging` route exists, so this is live on production `/tinyworld`. Runtime drone-vs-terrain behavior NOT verified headless (needs a loaded world + drone mode) — confirm on device. Backups: pre `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260625T223440Z-pre-drone-collision.tsx`, post `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260625T223955Z-drone-fullcollision-live.tsx`.
- **Walk physics (Jun 11)**: Minosoft-inspired. Spawn picks the real groundMap cell nearest centroid + sweeps Y for body clearance (no more independent-median spawn bug). Per-frame stuck-recovery: vertical push-up first, then corner-sample horizontal pushout fallback if ceiling blocks the lift. Diagonal speed normalized (W+D no longer √2× faster). Smooth step-up over ~80ms via dt-lerp when grounded; hard snap on jump landings keeps jumps crisp.
- **Tiny Worker v1 (Jun 11)**: one NPC per world, spawns at floor centroid. Pink cube (~0.7 voxel) with white edge outline + small white indicator cube above head when carrying. State machine: idle → walk → pickup (2.5s) → walk → place (1s) → idle. Paths via A* on `groundMap` (4-conn, step-up ≤ 1). 250ms decision tick; per-frame interp for smooth motion (~500ms/cell — ~10× slower than player on foot). Conservation-respecting — uses `freeSlots` like the player. Only picks up from `SOFT_WORKER_LAYERS` = {grass, dryGrass, leaves, fruit, dirt, snow}; ignores stone/wall/water/trunks. Behavior bias 60% pickup-and-move / 40% wander. Won't eat fruit (player-only).
- **Villagers v1 (Embodied AI / GOAP) (Jun 13)**: Upgraded the Tiny Worker to full Embodied AI. The `/api/tinyworld-plan` now accepts the worker's local perception (`resources`, `walkable`, `stockpile`, `structures`) and identity (`name`, `archetype`, `goal`). The Gemini 2.5 LLM acts as the villager's brain, returning a sequence of primitive GOAP actions: `PickUp`, `Place`, `MoveTo`, `Interact`. The `tickWorkers` frontend state machine iterates over these verbs to execute them autonomously. The archetype system assigns personality-driven goals (e.g. "The Mason" focuses on density/compression, "The Hoarder" focuses on stockpiling).
- **HUD subtlety + walk-spawn fallback (Jun 13)**: World-integrity hearts replaced with `MatterReadout` — slim 3px etched bar whose right edge bleeds into a void-purple gradient as world matter drains, with a tiny mono `MATTER 97.4` readout below; held-color warms ivory → amber → void-purple at 70% / 40% thresholds. NieR-instrument aesthetic, no more health-bar literalism. `BotwHearts` left in place (unused). Embody-Worker and Structures buttons gained the missing `className="group"` so hover-state transitions actually trigger. **F-key spawn priority rewritten (3 tiers)**: (1) AT the Tiny Worker — they've already pathfound a valid walkable cell on the actual floor, so anchor the player there; (2) centroid-nearest cell with body clearance (the old behavior, kept as fallback); (3) LOWEST ground cell in the world (almost certainly the room floor — walls/ceilings are higher). The old "highest ground cell" fallback was wrong for indoor scans: cramped lofts have low ceilings that the EYE_HEIGHT body cylinder doesn't fit under, so the ONLY cells passing body-clearance were the rooftop columns, dumping the player onto the roof looking down at the world. Now we anchor to the worker (who's already inside) or to the lowest floor cell (and tolerate a head-clip rather than spawn outside). Also forwarded `nodeLat`/`nodeLon` URL params from `fetchWorlds` to the discovery API so the existing API-tier debug override now also unhides worlds in the picker (helps on VPN/coarse-geo carriers).
- **Save/Load wired (Jun 10)**: `/tinyworld` page now has a SAVE/LOAD panel (world dropdown + Load/Save/Save As) backed by the `world_blocks` table. Layers serialize as compact base64-encoded Int32Array binary (~4.4 bytes/coord vs ~13 for JSON arrays). Save snapshots the LIVE edited scene (slotMaps + hiddenMaps + walkable ground), so moved blocks persist. Load rebuilds the full 3D scene from the DB without re-voxelizing (skips the GLB worker). Saved worlds are also loadable from the idle screen. Round-trip verified via API test.
- **Conservation ledger (Jun 11; stockpile clarified Jun 23; carried pool Jul 6)**: One law for everything: `WORLD + CARRIED + STOCKPILE + BUILT + VOID === baseline`, always. Blocks move between pools, never created/destroyed. **VOID doubles as the world's reservoir** — organic growth draws from it (`spawnBlockInto` auto-logs VOID→WORLD), decay/removal returns to it; this rule is load-bearing, without it the organic lifecycle breaks the invariant within seconds. Fresh scans seed VOID with 2% of scan mass so growth has matter to draw from. Carried blocks (player or worker) live in `CARRIED`, not `STOCKPILE`; failed worker placements keep the block in hand instead of vanishing. **Important design constraint:** STOCKPILE is not a hidden inventory. It must map to visible matter in-world: physical piles, carts/crates, or other explicit storage. A worker's hand is physical matter too, but it is a transit pool until deposited. All construction, including worker construction and onboarding, must physically take from existing visible blocks. Building should spend nearby visible material; if the player cannot see or locate the block, they should not be able to spend it. Every move queues a `block_events` row (kind grow/remove/cycle), aggregated client-side and flushed every 15s / on save / on pagehide (sendBeacon). Pools persist in save `meta.ledger`; WORLD is recounted authoritatively (visible+hidden) on every world build. Audit via `window.__tw.ledger()` → `{pools, sum, balanced, worldDrift, stockpileByLayer}`. Worker pickup/placement now routes through the shared `spawnBlockInto`/`removeBlockFrom` helpers + `syncGroundAfterRemove/Place` (no more inlined mutation logic).
- **Local protection field + void creatures (Jun 12, Chunk 3)**: Density ladder per `docs/mass-and-density.md` §9 (`DENSITY` map: bloom-tier 0.25 → loam 1 → stone 4 → metal 16 → densium 64 → core 256). Protection field = coarse 8×8-column cell grid (`protCells`); cell mass = Σ block density, maintained incrementally via hooks in `spawnBlockInto`/`removeBlockFrom` (the only two mutation paths). `protectionAt(vx,vz)` sums nearby cells with gravity falloff `m/(1+dx²+dz²)`. Catch-up rift now opens **deterministically at the lowest-protection perimeter cell** (was random). Void phase from real local time — passive 06–20h / active 20–23h / aggressive 23–06h — with `?void=aggressive|active|off` URL override for testing. **Void creatures**: hovering wraiths (dark octahedral-shard body, magenta eye strip, purple 0x8a2be2 beacon), no pathfinding — drift toward targets. Spawn at weakest perimeter cells (cap: aggressive 3 / active 1 / passive 0 → despawn at dawn). Eat the top block of their cell via `removeBlockFrom(...,"void_creature")` + `syncGroundAfterRemove` — fully ledger-conserving (WORLD→VOID). Eat rate scales with protection *relative to the perimeter median* (weakest ≈ 6s/bite, median ≈ 24s; raw protection values run in the thousands, so never use them un-normalized). Protection > 2.5× median repels creatures back to weak ground. Season multiplier: winter 1.25 / fall 1.1 / summer 0.9. HUD shows void phase + creature count + eaten total. Test hooks: `__tw.protection(vx,vz)`, `__tw.voidInfo()`, `__tw.spawnCreature()`, `__tw.clearCreatures()`, `__tw.cellInfo(vx,vz)`. Gotchas learned the hard way: (a) creature movement must step inside the 1s tick, never per render frame — headless tabs run at ~1 fps; (b) `removeCol` leaves empty Sets in `colMap` ("ghost columns") — any perimeter scan must skip `size === 0` columns or creatures chase cells with nothing to eat; (c) raw protection values run in the thousands — always normalize against the perimeter median before using them in rates. Verified Jun 12: 3 creatures spawn under `?void=aggressive`, bites log `remove|void_creature` block events, ledger stays balanced (drift 0), passive phase culls all creatures.
- **Scan economy + press (Jun 12, Chunk 4)**: Scanning new land is the ONLY legitimate baseline increase. `__tw.scan()` / HUD SCAN button (charge: 1 start, regen 1/10min, cap 3, `?scancharge=N` debug): picks a random true outer-edge perimeter column (probes 5 cells outward, ≥4 free — interior notches are fallback only), generates an adjacent ~10×12 patch (stone row at h−2, dirt at h−1, grass/dirt top, gentle ±1 height noise), spawns via `spawnBlockInto(...,"scan")` then refunds VOID and raises baseline by the count (`scan` block_event). Material rolls per spec §9.3: 18% pure-metal vein (4–7 blocks, tracked in `blockGrades`), 3% core find. New makeGrowable layers: stone(4)/metal(16)/densium(64)/core(256) — saved by `snapshotLiveLayers` under those names but `buildWorld` ignores unknown layers, so the scan section re-spawns them on load with the same void-refund/baseline-raise compensation (verified: 273-block scan survives save→reload). **Press**: `__tw.press(layer)` compresses 4 stockpiled blocks → 1 next-tier (`dirt→stone→metal→densium→core`); mass conserved exactly (4×d = 1×4d), block count −3, baseline −3, `compress` event. Verified: 16 dirt → 1 metal across 5 presses, baseline −15, balanced. `__tw.stock(layer,n)` debug-moves world→stockpile. Also fixed a Chunk 3 gap: player pickup (`finishCharge`) and `placeAt` mutate inline (NOT via the shared helpers), so they needed their own `protAdd` hooks — remember this when adding any new mutation path. Scan charges were superseded by scan-seconds, and grade enforcement + persistence landed the same day — see the Chunk 4 completion entry below.
- **Keel ships + Return slugs (Jun 12, Chunk 5)**: **Ships**: `__tw.buildShip(vx?,vz?)` binds 1 core + 8 stone from STOCKPILE into BUILT (ledger `ship_build`); `__tw.flyShip(tx,tz,idx)` autopilots at 1.5 cells/tick (1s tick, frame-rate independent like creatures) burning 1 hull block per 6 cells BUILT→VOID (`ship_burn` event — flight feeds the void); fuel<1 mid-flight = stranded where it stops; `__tw.scrapShip(idx)` recovers the core + remaining fuel as stone. Verified: 30-cell flight = exactly 5 burns, scrap returned core 1 + stone 3, balanced throughout. **Return slugs (spec §9.4)**: `__tw.makeSlug(layer)` is press math (4 stockpiled → 1, baseline −3, `compress` event) but the output is an unstable slug tracked as `slug_<tier>` keys in stockpileByLayer (NOT pressable; outside `ledgerMove`'s per-layer bookkeeping — adjust sp manually + aggregate-only ledgerMove). `__tw.placeBomb(vx,vz,size,tier)` arms 1/8/27 slugs stockpile→built; `__tw.detonate(idx)` Returns: each slug cascades a tier down (built→void size, spawn 4×size raw of `DOWN_LADDER` tier, mint `L.void += 3×size; L.baseline += 3×size`, `detonate` event — exactly cancels the slug presses, so a full make→detonate cycle is baseline-neutral; verified delta 0 for both 1-slug and 9-slug tests). Shockwave: force = strain/(1+d²) ≥ density cracks top blocks loose as remove+rescatter (net-zero, disorder not destruction); creatures in radius dissipate, their eaten mass re-spawning void→world; bombs in radius chain (verified: 8-slug bomb chained a 1-slug bomb 2 cells away, 32 raw out, 77 cracked). Armed bombs BAIT void creatures within 14 cells, and a strike (<1.3 cells) detonates — verified end-to-end with a spawned creature. Gotcha: detonation can despawn creatures mid-iteration of `tickVoidCreatures`' for..of loop, so the bite path `break`s out of the creature loop for that tick. Armed bombs are in-memory only (NOT persisted); slugs DO persist since Jun 12 via `meta.chunk4.stockpileByLayer`.
- **Grades + press structure + scan-seconds + purge (Jun 12, Chunk 4 completion)**: Refinement grades (raw/worked/pure, spec §9) enforced end-to-end. Graded stockpile pools per layer (`stockGradesRef`) + world-position grade map (`blockGradesRef`, only non-raw entries stored); all 15 `ledgerMove` call sites stay consistent through ONE choke point — `ledgerMove(from,to,n,source,layer?,grade?)` banks a grade on stockpile-in, drains specified-or-lowest on stockpile-out. Pickup reads a block's grade BEFORE `removeBlockFrom` deletes it (player carry keeps its grade through `carryState.grade`; placement tags the placed position). **Press is a built structure**: `__tw.buildPress(vx?,vz?)` costs 12 stockpiled stone (STOCKPILE→BUILT), spawns a mesh (stone base, metal ram + pillars, amber crystal) and gates compress/refine/makeSlug. `__tw.compress(layer)` — RENAMED from `__tw.press`, which collided with the key-simulator harness entry and silently broke smoke-test walking — compresses 4 cleanly ONLY if all 4 are pure; an impure feed auto-produces an unstable slug instead (entropy law). **Refine queue**: `__tw.refine(layer,n)` upgrades 1 block per 20s one grade step, burning 1 stockpiled fruit per step (bloom feeds the press); starves gracefully at 0 fruit. **Scan-seconds replace scan charges** (`docs/scan-economy.md`): one banked currency, cap 60, start 12, `?scansec=N` debug; bloom feeds it +0.02s per organic grow — accrual is gated to source `"sim"` (lesson: build-population, scans, purges, and placements are also void→world grows and would refund themselves), and ledger-init resets the bank to saved-or-12 to cancel build-time accrual. SCAN costs 10s. `__tw.purge(vx?,vz?)` costs 5s: despawns void creatures within radius 8 and reclaims ≤16 void-pool blocks as raw dirt on owned columns (`purge` event, mass conserved, grades lost). HUD: press line (BUILD PRESS / PRESS / REFINE) + scan line (banked seconds, SCAN / PURGE). **Persistence**: save meta carries `chunk4` {scanSeconds, stockpileByLayer (fixes the old reset-on-load gap, also persists slugs), stockGrades, non-raw blockGrades, press position}; ledger-init restores it and the press mesh rebuilds on load. Verified live on Brooklyn Loft (867k blocks): gate refusal → buildPress (−12 stone) → impure compress → slug; refine 1 raw→worked burning 1 fruit; scan +291 (−10s); purge 16 reclaimed (−5s); ledger balanced / drift 0 throughout; save→reload restored seconds/press/pools/slug exactly.
- **Voice commands + worker self-rescue (Jun 15)**: **Voice (Web Speech API)** — Mic toggle in the HUD next to Capture; `webkitSpeechRecognition` listens continuously (auto-restarts on `end` while listening), each utterance is sent to `window.__tw.setGoal(transcript)` which overwrites every worker's `(w as any).goal`, nulls `w.plan`, and clears `lastPlanFailMs` / `wanderFails` so the next planner tick picks up the spoken instruction. A small amber pill at bottom-left shows the live transcript. Silent no-op (with `setVoiceError`) on browsers without SR support. Gemini still validates/sanitizes the goal text in `/api/tinyworld-plan`. **Worker float bug fix** — `tickWorkers` now runs a per-worker resync at the top of the loop: when a worker is idle (and not mid pickup/place/build), it re-derives `w.vy = colTop(w.vx,w.vz) + 1`. The bug: when a void creature ate the column block beneath a worker, `syncGroundAfterRemove` updated `groundRef.current.map` but the worker's stored `w.vy` was stale, so it rendered at `(vy * voxel + 1.45*voxel)` — visibly floating one block above the new top. **Worker escape route** — same resync path: if the worker's column is empty (no `colTop`), step to a 4-conn neighbor with ground; if no neighbor has ground either, spawn 1 stockpile block (`worker-escape`, dirt/grass/dryGrass/stone/snow/leaves priority) into `(vx, vy-1, vz)` via `spawnBlockInto` + `syncGroundAfterPlace` + ledger `stockpile→void` (mirrors `worker-build`). **Beacon removed** — the cyan cylinder above the worker's head (debug aid for the spawn-zero bug) was deleted. Critical lesson: `edit_space_route` interprets `// ... existing code ...` greedily — removing just the beacon ALSO dropped the `WorkerState` construction + `workers.push(w)` + `return w` lines that followed it. Worlds loaded with 0 workers until the WorkerState construction was restored in a follow-up edit. Always re-grep for surviving identifiers after a deletion-style merge.
- **Organic lifecycle (Jun 10)**: Organic blocks (fruit, seed, sapling, plus growth-trunks/leaves) flow through a lifecycle (transacting against the VOID reservoir, see ledger above): leaf → fruit (buds on sunlit leaves, season-gated) → drops → rots after ~22s → seed → germinates on soil → sapling → matures into trunk + leaf above (~90s total cycle). Tick cadence 750ms; rate × seasonGrowth (1.7 summer / 1.2 spring / 0.55 fall / 0.15 winter). Fruit is pickupable (80ms hold) and consumable: holding fruit + `E` deletes the block and increments a `satiation` HUD counter. `tickOrganic` self-heals — newly placed organic blocks get registered, stale entries pruned each tick. State persists via `snapshotLiveLayers()` (walks all mesh slotMaps including hiddenMaps before save) + `playerState.satiation` in `meta`.

- **Animation layer — anime.js on ticks (Jun 22)**: BotW scripted moments run on anime.js (self-contained bundle at `/lib/anime-three.bundle.mjs`; do NOT `bun add` it — esm.sh's split chunks break the three adapter registry, and zo.space package installs break on restart). Continuous sim stays on the tick loop. Hard rule: never let anime tween a property a tick writes in the same frame — implemented via a `simFrozen` closure flag in `buildWorld` that gates the continuous sim ticks + walk-physics block in `loop()`; render + cinematic ticks (`tickOrb`/`tickOpening`/`tickPilot`) keep running. First conversion: the first-load opening cinematic's camera timing/easing is driven by `animeAnimate(cineK, {k:1, duration:5500, ease:"out(3)"})`; `tickOpening` maps `cineK` onto the arc, with a manual-easing fallback if the bundle fails to load. Edits live in `src/tinyworld-route.tsx`. NOTE: the LIVE `/tinyworld` route is the older no-orb version (deployed via `.route-backups/...MERGED...tsx` → `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld.tsx` + `bun run build` + restart), so the orb+anime build is NOT yet deployed — pending KJ call: promote to live `/tinyworld` vs create a private `/tinyworld-orb` preview route (new routes need a `.routes.json` entry, not just a file drop).
- **Physical building + graphics pass (Jun 23)**: Implemented in `src/tinyworld-route.tsx` and promoted to live `/tinyworld`: per-instance terrain color variation (`setSlotTone` / addLayer tone jitter), placement settle animation (`spawnSettleFx` + `tickPhysicalFx`), dust puff, contact-shadow disc, and source-to-target relocation tether. Historical note: this pass was visual-only over the then-current ledger where carried blocks moved WORLD -> STOCKPILE -> WORLD; **superseded Jul 6 on staging by the explicit `CARRIED` ledger pool**. Debug hooks: `__tw.settleTest(layer?)` replays settle FX on an existing visible block without ledger mutation, `__tw.fxInfo()` reports active settle/dust/tether counts. Reboot-recovery backup refreshed at `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260623-physical-fx-live.tsx`; `reapply-framing.sh` now points to this backup and verifies render + physical-FX markers.
- **Sentinel + drone animation pass (Jun 23)**: Implemented in `src/tinyworld-route.tsx` and promoted to live `/tinyworld`. `/sentinel.glb` is served correctly (825KB GLB); the Sentinel GLB path now has robust clip fallback if a named clip is missing, and drone mode no longer counts as "walk the Sentinel" — LARGE mode wakes/walks the body, DRONE mode parks/sits/freezes it at the last large-body anchor. Added a visible camera-attached drone rig (cyan core, halo, winglets, light) that appears only in drone mode, pulses/banks/glows with movement, and hides the old tool rig while active. Fixed the mobile Bulk/Drone toggle so touch does not double-toggle via the subsequent click event. Debug hooks: `__tw.sentinelInfo()`, `__tw.droneInfo()`, `__tw.setSentinelMode("large"|"drone")`. Reboot-recovery backup refreshed at `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260623-sentinel-drone-live.tsx`.
- **Third-person Sentinel/drone view (Jun 24)**: The live `/tinyworld` Sentinel and drone modes now render as third-person scene avatars instead of camera-attached first-person bodies. The controller/raycast camera remains the stable input target, but LARGE mode places the Sentinel body at a yaw-only forward offset (`thirdPerson.forward` in `__tw.sentinelInfo()`), and DRONE mode places the drone rig as a scene object ahead/below the camera (`thirdPerson` in `__tw.droneInfo()`). Sentinel full-body framing was widened from `0.68` to `2.08` world units (`voxel * 26`) so the camera is not tight on the body. Verified with disposable local debug worlds: no loop errors, drone rig visible in walk+drone mode, and screenshots saved under `debug-shots/third-person-runtime-20260624T0315-yawfix/` and `debug-shots/sentinel-fullbody-20260624T0321/`. Reboot-recovery backup refreshed at `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260624T0321-sentinel-fullbody-camera-live.tsx`.
- **Luanti-aligned rebuild — Slices A/B/C (Jun 24)**: Fixed the architectural inversion (avatar derived FROM camera each frame; Luanti's `LocalPlayer` is truth, `Camera` derives) in three reversible slices, all promoted to live `/tinyworld`. **Slice A (Sentinel grounding + yaw damping):** `renderSentinel` now samples real ground via `sampleGround(...)` for `feetY` (decoupled from camera bob/lift, so the body no longer sinks with the camera's vertical drift) and lerps a persistent `sentinelYaw` toward the camera yaw instead of hard-snapping `rotation.y = camera.rotation.y + Math.PI` — kills the ~26-block swing arc when the camera turns. **Slice B (drone single-rotation):** `tickDroneRig` no longer compounds a `quaternion.slerp(camera.quaternion)` with separate Euler `.z`/`.x` accumulation (double-applied yaw). Now accumulates `droneYaw`/`droneBank`/`dronePitch` state and applies them in ONE `droneRig.rotation.set(pitch, yaw, bank)` with `rotation.order = "YXZ"` (yaw world-up first, then local pitch/bank — standard aircraft convention, matches Luanti's yaw-then-local-rotation model). **Slice C (block-anim frame-delta clock + free-slot guard):** `tickPhysicalFx` now carries its own `_fxPrev` frame-delta clock (was raw `now`-based, frame-rate dependent — broke on throttled/headless tabs running ~1 fps) and the settle/dust/tether fx advance by `dt/duration` into a persistent `age` field (types `SettleFx`/`DustFx`/`RelocationTetherFx` migrated `t0: number` → `age: number`); `takeFx` lost its `|| pool[0]` fallback so it returns `null` when no invisible (free) mesh exists instead of stealing an animating mesh — spawn functions now null-check and skip. **Deploy:** `src/tinyworld-route.tsx` → `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld.tsx` + `bun run build` + `supervisorctl restart zo-space`. Reboot-recovery backup refreshed at `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260624T0522-slices-ABC-live.tsx`; `reapply-framing.sh` repointed at it (verifies Slice A feet-grounding + Slice B YXZ + Slice C takeFx-guard markers). **Verification:** build clean (no syntax/type errors), served bundle `tinyworld-CGe46mK7.js` contains the `YXZ` literal + `.find((m)=>!m.visible)` guard; landing page + debug panel render healthy. Runtime walk/sentinel/drone behavior NOT verified headless — headless Chromium cannot reach esm.sh to load the Sentinel/drone GLB, so the third-person avatar paths must be confirmed on a physical device (open https://kj.zo.space/tinyworld, enter walk mode, turn the camera, confirm the Sentinel body stays grounded and rotates smoothly; flip to drone, move, confirm banking).
- **Sentinel grounding + camera-inside fix (Jun 24)**: Fixed two walk-mode bugs reported via screenshot. (1) Sentinel sunk into cliffs: `renderSentinel` was grounding the avatar's feet using a ground sample taken at the CAMERA's (x,z), but the body renders ~sentinelForward voxels ahead — so on cliff terrain the feet landed on the camera's (lower) column while the body stood on higher ground ahead, sinking the figure. Now samples ground at the Sentinel's own `(bodyX, bodyZ)` and publishes it to a shared `sentinelFeetY`. (2) Camera clipping inside the Sentinel torso: the eye-clamp used only the camera's own ground (`groundY + EYE_HEIGHT + WALK_CAMERA_LIFT`), which on uphill terrain lands inside the Sentinel's vertical span. Now `eye = max(camGroundY, sentinelFeetY) + EYE_HEIGHT + WALK_CAMERA_LIFT` — the camera rides the avatar's ground when uphill (never drops below its own, so holes under the lens still drop the player). Also widened `sentinelForward` from `voxel*16` → `voxel*22` for a proper over-the-shoulder read (the 0321 fullbody pass had set `voxel*26`, later reduced to 16 which was too tight). GLB origin confirmed at feet (min.y=0, native h=5.4) so no model-origin offset needed. Deployed via the custom pipeline (src → `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld.tsx` + `bun run build` + restart); build clean, landing healthy, `loopError:false`. Runtime avatar behavior NOT verifiable headless (GLB can't load in headless Chromium) — confirm on device. Reboot-recovery backup `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260624T1204-sentinel-grounding-cam-inside-fix-live.tsx`; `reapply-framing.sh` repointed to it and its marker list refreshed to current live truths (the 0630 normandy refactor had removed the metaOf/grew/Reframe scan-framing markers, leaving the script stale — now 9 current markers, all passing). **Forward direction (KJ, Jun 24):** BotW-inspired UX — build snapping + flying/glider feel — is the next phase; this grounding fix is the foundation for a proper behind-and-above camera.
- **Cockpit default view + drone visibility fix (Jun 24)**: Added a view-mode toggle for large-mode Sentinel piloting: `cockpit` defaults to a HUD-style first-person frame (based on KJ's reference shot) and `third` keeps a closer, slightly higher over-the-shoulder view. In cockpit mode, the Sentinel body is hidden while walking so the user can see out and place/snap blocks more easily; the cockpit HUD adds corner brackets, reticle, and telemetry text. Drone mode now has a clearer, brighter visible rig and a stronger light so the drone reads properly in flight. Deployed through the custom pipeline (`src/tinyworld-route.tsx` → `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld.tsx` + `bun run build` + restart); new backup at `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260624T133954Z-cockpit-view-drone-fix-live.tsx`; `reapply-framing.sh` updated to restore this slice after reboot.
- **Voxel Robots Sentinel asset swap (Jun 24)**: Replaced the live `/sentinel.glb` with the uploaded `Voxel Robots - Dev Pack` MechaGolem animation model converted from `Assets/_Packs/VoxelRobots/Game Ready Robots/MechaGolem/MechaGolem_Anim_Model.fbx` to GLB via Blender. The old live GLB is preserved as `assets/sentinel-legacy-sitting.glb` and uploaded to `/sentinel-legacy-sitting.glb` because the new pack has no explicit sitting clip. Unity metadata defines one combined take (`MechaGolem_AllAnimExport`) with clip ranges: `Idle` 0-56, `Run` 57-77, `Attack` 78-85, `Hit` 86-115, `Death` 116-213. `src/tinyworld-route.tsx` now normalizes/grounds the Sentinel from `Box3` bounds, splits the combined take with `THREE.AnimationUtils.subclip`, and aliases `Idle -> Sitting`, `Run -> WalkSlow` so the existing state machine survives missing sitting/transition clips. Deployed to live `/tinyworld`; served `/sentinel.glb` hash verified as `5a0104a94c71f98756ed3727f1c9d3a73ec558400abe1aa2995b85c7b3036e94`; served bundle `tinyworld-C1h4eWw3.js` contains `MechaGolem_AllAnimExport` + `AnimationUtils` markers. Reboot-recovery backup refreshed at `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260624T162545Z-voxel-robots-sentinel-live.tsx`; `.route-backups/reapply-framing.sh` points to it and verifies the Voxel Robots markers.
- **Capture inference async + phased loading default (Jun 24; superseded Jun 25)**: Fixed the capture flow failure shown as `inference: failed to start` during `interpreting space`. Root cause: `/api/tinyworld-infer` accepted `start_async:true` but immediately entered the synchronous geometric `spawnSync` path before returning the POST response, so the phone waited for the whole worker and surfaced a start failure on timeout. This originally made progressive Depth/RGB-D TSDF the default; that default is superseded by the Jun 25 OpenMVS/GLB core decision. Keep progressive/depth modes available only through explicit debug/fallback flags, not as the canonical saved-world pipeline.
- **Progressive final fuse + floor-plane fix (Jun 25)**: The current camera path does capture and use IMU/DeviceMotion sidecars (`imu_used:true`), but a regression made the final saved progressive payload serialize local column bottoms as the floor. On `w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu` this put the real floor across y=0..18, with only 3 visible columns at y=0, so the loaded world looked like it had no floor. `tools/video_to_voxels.py` now does two important things in `run_progressive` / `decompose_structure`: (1) writes a final coherent `world.json` from one global TSDF fuse for the saved/loaded world instead of relying on concatenated per-window zones; (2) discards below-dominant-floor drift and snaps all grounded floor/wall/interior columns to the dominant floor plane before serialization (`floor_snapped` metadata). Reprocessed `cap_mqsrje10_l2optl` in place into `w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu`: 24,259 blocks, `consolidation:"global-fuse-floor-plane"`, 887 visible columns all lowest at y=0, floor histogram y=0 for 741 floor columns, IMU used, 2 lost transitions. Old world_blocks payload backup: `/tmp/world_blocks_backup_w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu_pre_floor_plane_fix_1782355624.json`.
- **Old-path GLB restoration (Jun 25)**: KJ confirmed the higher-quality reference was the mesh-first path (COLMAP/OpenMVS -> gravity-aligned GLB -> GLB voxelizer), not the current Depth-Anything/RGB-D TSDF path. Replaced `w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu`'s saved `world_blocks` row from the attached aligned GLB using a server-side mirror of the TinyWorld GLB worker at mobile-safe resolution 220: 50,651 blocks, `inferredFrom.source:"aligned-glb-old-path"`, source GLB `scan_mqjrgo0s_aligned-bqacageaaxkbaaes_bxqph0hn3e6sdkswequ_cephxwstwacgwyaaml-4ewx-hicf5gdytwe.glb`. Previous floor-plane TSDF payload backed up at `data/backups/world_blocks_backup_w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu_pre_old_path_glb_1782358561.json`. Going forward, the desired final saved scan should prefer the OpenMVS/aligned-GLB finalizer when available; the RGB-D TSDF path is acceptable only as preview/fallback.
- **Actual old-path rerun from TinyWorld capture (Jun 25)**: Clarification from KJ: the old GLB was produced by a pipeline; the target is the pipeline, not reusing a static GLB. Added `tools/old_path_from_capture.py` plus `tools/glb_to_world_payload.py` to convert a TinyWorld `cap_...` video/IMU capture into the photogrammetry scan layout, run `extract_frames.ts -> run_colmap.ts -> run_openmvs.ts -> align_gravity.py -> GLB voxelizer`, and update `world_blocks`. Ran it on `cap_mqsrje10_l2optl`: 60/60 COLMAP frames registered, 12,199 sparse points, 0.846px reprojection error; OpenMVS produced 429,802 dense points, 108,413 mesh vertices, 216,470 triangles; IMU gravity alignment dispersion 0.00284. Saved a mobile-safe 180-div voxel payload into `w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu`: 59,232 blocks, resolution 184, `inferredFrom.source:"aligned-glb-old-path"`, `scan_id:"scan_from_cap_mqsrje10_l2optl"`. Previous row backup: `data/backups/world_blocks_backup_w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu_pre_actual_openmvs_old_path_1782359742.json`.
- **OpenMVS vegetation upgrade (Jun 25)**: `tools/glb_to_world_payload.py` now includes the `blocky-brooke-v2` vegetation decorator in the canonical GLB finalizer, so OpenMVS-sourced worlds regain the visual biome pass instead of loading as bare dryGrass/dirt/wall/ceiling geometry. The decorator is deterministic and mobile-bounded: edge-biased tiered trees / deciduous trees / palm silhouettes, shrubs, grass pixels, and fruit/flower accents, while preserving the walkable OpenMVS structure. Reprocessed `w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu` from `scan_from_cap_mqsrje10_l2optl/openmvs/scene_aligned.glb` at target divs 180: 60,311 blocks, resolution 184, vegetation metadata `{style:"blocky-brooke-v2", trees:46, shrubs:121, ground:700}`, layers now include `trunks`, `leaves`, `grass`, and `fruit`. Previous OpenMVS payload backup: `data/backups/world_blocks_backup_w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu_pre_brooke_vegetation_1782386079.json`.
- **Camera rectification restored (Jun 24)**: The scattered-world issue came from the camera/RGB-D path bypassing the older GLB worker's rectification pipeline (floor median smoothing, dominant-floor snap, wall/floor grounding). Added `rectify_indoor_grid()` in `tools/video_to_voxels.py`: keeps columns attached to the dominant floor, applies two-pass median floor smoothing, caps impossible indoor height via `--max-room-height-m` (default/live `3.2m`), adds a smoothed floor skin where needed, then continues through solidify/serialize. Reprocessed `cap_mqsaw3ge_zyfa4g` into cleaner world `w_geo_mqsc3d6f_0r0h8j`: 12,243 blocks, span 82, 1,388 columns, bbox height 59 cells; rectifier dropped 4,126 ungrounded columns / 19,582 voxels and capped 2,916 high voxels. Old scattered world `w_geo_mqsb9ate_7ngdic` still exists but should not be the recommended target.
- **Structural decomposition — straight walls + flat planes (Jun 24)**: The rectified grid was still a "blobbed mess" because the camera path classified every voxel by COLOR only (`rgb_to_layer` → dirt/stone/metal/snow), with no `wall`/`ceiling`/`hidden_dirt` layers — unlike the clean legacy Scaniverse/GLB worlds which decompose into explicit structural layers. Added `decompose_structure()` in `tools/video_to_voxels.py` (runs after `rectify_indoor_grid`, anchored to its `floor_y`/`cap_y`): detects the ceiling as the highest well-supported cluster of tall column tops (default `cap_y`), keeps wall columns in connected components ≥6 (drops isolated false walls), fills each wall column as a SOLID straight vertical run `floor→ceiling` tagged `wall`, lays a flat `ceiling` plane over wall columns, and fills the interior of grounded non-wall columns `floor+1→ceiling-1` as `hidden_dirt` (invisible to the renderer, matches the legacy solid-interior convention — the leaky flood-fill `solidify()` no longer needed for interior). `LAYERS` now includes `wall`/`ceiling`/`hidden_dirt`; `serialize_layers`/`serialize_subset` accept a `layer_override` dict so structural voxels keep their tagged layer instead of falling through to color classification. Wired into BOTH single-shot and progressive (`emit_zone`) paths; the progressive final flood-fill zone was removed (per-zone `decompose` already fills interior). Reprocessed `cap_mqsaw3ge_zyfa4g` single-shot into clean world `w_geo_mqsdrixs_hvcxwq`: 62,658 blocks → `wall` 14,325, `ceiling` 310, `hidden_dirt` 46,943, plus floor/accent layers. Progressive smoke test on the same capture: 13 zones, 69,628 blocks, `wall` 5,471 / `ceiling` 423 / `hidden_dirt` 62,031. Note: headless Chromium cannot reach esm.sh to render the 3D view, so the clean look must be confirmed on a physical device via `/tinyworld?world=w_geo_mqsdrixs_hvcxwq` (or a fresh progressive capture).
- **Formal debug mode + screenshot loop (Jun 23)**: `/tinyworld?debug=1` now shows a `TINYWORLD DEBUG` panel before a world loads (phase, block count, saved-world count, node status) and upgrades to an in-scene diagnostics/control panel after `buildWorld()` initializes. The in-scene panel reports FPS, camera, jitter, columns/meshes, ledger pools, active settle/dust/tether FX, Sentinel state, drone state, worker state, outline state, and loop-crash status. Panel buttons trigger repeatable test hooks: settle FX, drone toggle, void scout, opening, pilot ship, frameWorld, raw render, and post-FX restore. `__tw.frameWorld()` is exposed again to reframe the camera around actual visible geometry. Screenshot pipeline lives at `tools/debug-screenshot.sh`; it opens `http://localhost:3099/tinyworld?debug=1`, captures before/after PNGs and JSON state, optionally clicks the first saved world (`TW_LOAD_FIRST=1`) and triggers a hook (`TW_HOOK=settle|drone|void|opening|pilot|frame|none`). Latest proof artifacts are under `debug-shots/latest/`. Reboot-recovery backup refreshed at `.route-backups/tinyworld-20260623-debug-mode-live.tsx`; `reapply-framing.sh` now points to this backup and verifies render + framing + debug markers.

## Live prototype (zo.space routes — edit via space tools, not files)

- `POST/GET /api/tinyworld-worlds` — register/list worlds (+distance)
- `GET/POST /api/tinyworld-access` — access decision (geo|bridge|none); bridge + structure building
- `GET /api/tinyworld-weather` — Open-Meteo → seasonal/void modifiers
- `GET /api/tinyworld-state` — world tick: growth/removal/cycle pressures, Tiny People mode
- `POST/GET /api/tinyworld-infer` — scan finalization; OpenMVS/GLB via `tools/old_path_from_capture.py` is the only real scan finalizer. `mode=geometric`, `mode=progressive`, `geometry=1`, and `progressive=1` are rejected.
- `/tinyworld/capture` — phone video capture page; always starts the OpenMVS/GLB finalizer and never redirects into progressive/Depth streaming.
- `/tinyworld` — page route (visualizer/playground)

These read/write `data/tinyworld.db` in this folder.

### Staging route caveat (Jun 26, 2026)

`/tinyworld-staging` is currently served by the custom disk/build route pipeline (`/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx` + route index + Vite build), while the Zo Space route API still reports that path as a tiny stub. Treat staging edits as session/disk-pipeline edits unless they are explicitly promoted into the durable Space route store; always build/restart and verify the served bundle after edits.

Jun 26 staging regression fix: Patched `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx` after KJ reported black screens in DRN/3RD, a misaligned cockpit HUD, and lost Sentinel animations. Fixes: cockpit multi-panel renderer now matches the HUD panes (left 2-32%, center 34-66% unskewed, right 68-98%) and resets WebGL render target/viewport/scissor/autoClear after every path; mobile walk/3RD/DRN bypasses the EffectComposer and direct-renders after a clean state reset; Sentinel loader restores the MechaGolem combined-take split via `THREE.AnimationUtils.subclip`, aliases `Idle -> Sitting` and `Run -> WalkSlow`, normalizes GLB bounds, and falls back if transition clips are missing; staging also received the drone full-collision axis-revert block (`oldX/oldZ/oldY` + `bodyBlockedAtY`) so DRN cannot pass through terrain while staying gravity-free in open air. Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-BK_sO2RV.js`), `zo-space` restarted, `/tinyworld-staging` served 200. Verified in mobile viewport by loading first saved world, entering CPIT/3RD/DRN: `window.__twLoopError === false`, screenshots nonblank by pixel stats (means ~0.42/0.43/0.46). Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging-20260626T0208-pre-hud-animation-fix.tsx` and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging-20260626T0212-hud-drone-animation-fix.tsx`.

Jun 26 staging follow-up: Per KJ, first-person HUD is on hold; staged only the size/flight regressions. `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx` now scales the Sentinel from the fixed known GLB native height (`SENTINEL_NATIVE_H = 5.4`) instead of animated bounds and reduces `SENTINEL_TARGET_H` to `7.5 * voxel` so 3RD view no longer fills the camera. Drone flight reverted closer to the older working feel: restored smoothed mobile look lerp (`dt * 18`) and the old small `droneBlockedAt` probe with whole-frame rollback on terrain hit, replacing the newer per-axis body-cylinder sliding. Added staging debug hook `window.__tw.setPilotMode("cockpit"|"third"|"drone")` for direct mode verification. Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-BsN6drOZ.js`), `zo-space` restarted, `/tinyworld-staging` served 200. Verified on smaller saved world `w_prog_mqtlf00g_i5he3x`: CPIT/3RD/DRN all nonblank by pixel stats, `window.__twLoopError === false`. Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging-20260626T030100Z-pre-size-flight-revert.tsx` and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging-20260626T031845Z-size-flight-revert.tsx`.

Jun 26 Sentinel staging follow-up: Addressed KJ's remaining Sentinel issues from screenshot: heavy/grounded gait + clipping in 3RD. `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx` now filters root-motion position tracks from Sentinel clips (`Hips`, `Armature`, `MechaGolem`) before binding actions, which removes animated root bob/slide that made feet feel floaty; `WalkSlow` timeScale reduced from `0.4` to `0.22` while moving, still frozen at `0` when idle. Added `sentinelBodyBlockedAt()` with a wider body radius and 3RD movement guard, so camera movement reverts if the visible Sentinel body would intersect terrain. 3RD placement now tries forward distances 18/16/14/12/10/8/6 voxels and uses the first clear body position instead of always placing at 14 voxels, reducing wall/foliage clipping. 3RD cam lift raised to `voxel * 12`. Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-DWiCofEP.js`), `zo-space` restarted, `/tinyworld-staging` served 200. Verified on `w_prog_mqtlf00g_i5he3x`: forced `__tw.setPilotMode("third")`, simulated a short walk input, `window.__twLoopError === false`, screenshots nonblank by pixel stats. Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging-20260626T033056Z-pre-sentinel-heavystep-clipfix.tsx` and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging-20260626T033355Z-sentinel-heavystep-clipfix.tsx`.

Jun 26 Sentinel staging rollback: KJ's screen recording showed the previous "heavystep/clipfix" made 3RD worse: the visible body/terrain relationship became unstable and oversized. Rolled back the risky pieces in `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx`: removed root-position track filtering, removed `sentinelBodyBlockedAt()` / forward-distance search / 3RD movement guard, restored fixed 3RD placement (`fwd = voxel * 14`, `lift = voxel * 11`), and reduced Sentinel scale to native target height (`SENTINEL_TARGET_H = 5.4 * voxel`).
