Jun 26 staging walk-feel + walkability pass: Three targeted edits to `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx` per KJ. (1) Walk slowed 50%: movement loop walk branch now applies `speed * 0.5` (drone unchanged at `speed * 1.8`); Sentinel `WalkSlow` moving timeScale halved `0.32 -> 0.16` so foot-plant stays in sync with the slower ground speed. (2) Zelda-style camera/drift fix: added `moveYawRef`; in walk mode the move vector is built from `applyAxisAngle(Y, moveYawRef)` instead of live `camera.quaternion`, and the yaw is latched (frozen) while the mobile look-stick is active, so sweeping the camera left/right no longer curves the walk path. When not looking, `moveYawRef` tracks `camera.rotation.y` (old camera-relative feel preserved); drone still uses full `camera.quaternion`; dt*18 look smoothing kept. (3) Ground vegetation removed: before the `addLayer` calls, `layers.grass`/`layers.leaves`/`layers.trunks` are nulled so addLayer skips them (no mesh, no colMap/collision) — kills the green grass caps + bushes (and trees, which share the leaves/trunks layers) that made terrain an obstacle course; structural floor (dirt/dryGrass) intact, no holes. Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-8djSaid6.js`), `zo-space` restarted, `/tinyworld-staging` served 200. Verified headless on `w_prog_mqtlf00g_i5he3x`: harness built, `window.__twLoopError === false` across landing/teleport/gotoWorker/simWalk; terrain screenshot solid + walkable, no green grass/bushes, no holes; Zo Space errors empty (only known Gemini 503 worker-brain noise). NOT verifiable headless (GLB never loads via esm.sh; mobile latch needs touch): the actual slower-walk/heavier-anim feel, the drift fix, and Sentinel render in CPIT/3RD/DRN — must be confirmed on KJ's device. Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-050950.pre-zelda-veg.tsx` and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-051540.post-zelda-veg.tsx`.

Jun 26 third-person chase camera (3RD rework): The decouple-v2 fix latched the mech FACING but not its POSITION — because `camera.position` IS the player anchor (collision/gravity/movement all act on it) and the mech was rendered as a rigid offset from it, running still dragged the mech ("camera still pull sentinel while running"). KJ approved a proper chase camera for 3RD only (cockpit/drone unchanged). Implemented in `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx`: the mech now OWNS its world position. `camera.position` is reused untouched as the body/collision anchor (so no holes/fall-through reopened); a new `bodyPosRef` persists it across the render override. Flow per chase frame: (1) top of loop restores `camera.position` from `bodyPosRef` BEFORE any consumer reads it, so renderSentinel/physics/gravity keep acting on the mech; (2) movement is camera-relative to the ORBIT yaw (`targetLookRef.y`) and the mech turns to FACE its travel direction (`moveYawRef` eased toward `atan2(-moveDir.x,-moveDir.z)` at `dt*10`); (3) after gravity, the body anchor is saved to `bodyPosRef` and the RENDER camera is moved to a smoothed trailing orbit behind+above the mech (`followDist = voxel*30`, `followHeight = voxel*14`, offset along orbitYaw/pitch, lerp `dt*8`) then `lookAt(body + voxel*2)`. renderSentinel 3RD now places the body AT the anchor (`fwd = 0`) since the camera trails behind it. Refs added near `moveYawRef`: `bodyPosRef`, `chaseCamRef`, `chaseWasActiveRef` (rising-edge re-seeds anchor on enter-walk-in-3rd or CPIT->3RD toggle). Desktop keeps a yaw-only capture from `camera.rotation.y` so mouse-look still orbits (pitch holds a fixed angle). Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-CXFXrloH.js`), `zo-space` restarted, served 200. Headless-verified on the 23,711-block progressive scan (temporary `window.__chaseDbg` probe, since removed): entering 3RD gives `loopError === false`; debug confirmed `cam = body + offset` with `|offX,offZ| == followDist (2.27)` and `offY == followHeight (1.06)`; holding KeyW moved the BODY in the orbit-relative forward dir (Δ direction `(-0.852, 0.524)` == forward rotated by orbitYaw 2.12) with the camera trailing. NOT verifiable headless (GLB never loads via esm.sh): the actual mech facing/framing + follow distance/height/smoothing FEEL must be eyeballed by KJ — expect 1-2 tuning rounds on `followDist`/`followHeight`/lerp. Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-053015.veg-revert-decouple.tsx` (prior good state) and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-061229.chasecam-deployed.tsx` (this).

Jun 26 3RD gait + size pass (builds on the chase camera): KJ asked for connected run->walk->stand animation, a 2x-bigger Sentinel, ~2x speed, and travel speed coupled to gait. Edits in `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx`: (1) **Size doubled** — `SENTINEL_TARGET_H` `5.4 -> 10.8 * voxel`. (2) **Gait coupling** — there is only ONE locomotion clip (Run, aliased `WalkSlow`; no separate walk/transition clips), so motion feel is expressed by blending the clip's *playback rate*, not by cross-fading clips. The movement block now computes an analog `travelMult` from input deflection (`defl = min(1, inLen)` smoothstepped 0.15..1) between `GAIT_WALK_MULT = 0.55` and `GAIT_RUN_MULT = 1.2` (× `MOVE_SPEED`); mobile stick deflection drives walk->run, keyboard is binary so it always runs (1.2x = 2.4x the old flat 0.5 walk, < drone's 1.8x). `moveSpeed = speed * travelMult` for both chase and cockpit (drone keeps `1.8`). The matching clip rate is `sentinelGaitTS = travelMult * GAIT_SYNC` (`GAIT_SYNC = 0.32`, preserving the old good 0.5-speed/0.16-rate ratio so feet stay planted at every speed); set to 0 each frame when not moving (non-drone). (3) **Smooth transitions** — `tickSentinel` standing branch now eases the live rate `sentinelCurTS += (sentinelGaitTS - sentinelCurTS) * min(1, delta*7)` and applies it, instead of snapping `setEffectiveTimeScale(moving?0.16:0)`; gives connected run<->walk<->stand and freezes (rate 0) at standstill. (4) **Chase reframe for the taller mech** — the chase override now re-bases on the mech's FEET (`feetY = anchor.y - EYE_HEIGHT - voxel*11`) and scales with `SENTINEL_TARGET_H`: `followDist = H*3.2`, `followHeight = H*1.5` (above feet), `lookAt` at `feetY + H*0.55` (mid-torso), so doubling the size re-frames automatically. New closure state near the sentinel setup: `sentinelGaitTS`, `sentinelCurTS`, and the three gait constants. Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-B4UwI0px.js`), `zo-space` restarted, `/tinyworld-staging` served 200. Headless-verified on the 23,711-block progressive scan: harness built, `setPilotMode("third")` -> walking, `window.__twLoopError === false` while holding KeyW, and a screenshot shows the mech rendered ~2x larger and framed in the chase view. NOT verifiable headless (1fps): the gait-blend FEEL, the actual run/walk speeds, and final framing must be eyeballed by KJ — expect a tuning round on `GAIT_WALK_MULT`/`GAIT_RUN_MULT`/`GAIT_SYNC` and `followDist`/`followHeight`. Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-061229.chasecam-deployed.tsx` (prior) and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-063701.gait-size-v1.tsx` (this).

Jun 26 gait skate fix: KJ reported the forward movement still looked skate-like (foot glide). Added `GAIT_DISTANCE_TRIM = 0.8` and applied it to the walk/run `moveSpeed` ONLY (`moveSpeed = speed * travelMult * 0.8`), NOT to `sentinelGaitTS` — so the body advances 20% less per animation cycle while the clip plays at the same rate, changing the distance:animation ratio (trimming both would leave the skate unchanged). Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-CiuNEYsE.js`), `zo-space` restarted, served 200; headless walk `loopError===false`. Skate FEEL is not headless-verifiable (1fps) — KJ to confirm direction/amount on device (flip toward >1.0, or change the 0.8, if it's the other way / not enough). Backup: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-065757.gait-distance-trim.tsx`.

Jun 26 floor hole-fill + bush removal: KJ asked to make the floor have fewer holes / "fill all of these holes" and "remove bushes". Added one self-contained pass in `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx` right before the `addLayer(layers.dirt...)` calls (~line 2494), so it runs for BOTH fresh mesh imports and saved scans (saved scans bake a sparse floor and previously got no hole-fill — the `filledFloor` rectification at ~line 331 only runs on the mesh-import path). **Hole-fill** (adds floor blocks only, never deletes — deleting reopens holes): builds a per-column surface-top map from dirt/dryGrass/grass; (a) flood-fills the void from the bbox border so empty cells the flood never reaches are *interior holes* (any size) → filled; (b) a 6-pass concave/crack fill adds empty cells touching floor on >=3 of 4 sides OR on two OPPOSITE sides (1-wide cracks/channels) — both close gaps without inflating straight outer edges. Fill height = inward relaxation from real floor (fallback dominant level); fill surface = majority neighbour cap (grass/dryGrass/dirt). Fills are appended to the matching surface layer (so they're collidable via addLayer's addCol) and to `layers.ground` ONLY if the voxelizer already supplied one (appending to an empty ground buffer would make the column-rebuild at ~line 3288 derive groundMap from just the fills; saved scans keep ground empty so the rebuild picks fills up from colMap). **Bush removal** (KJ's documented next-step from the veg work): cull leaf voxels that are low (`leafY - groundTop <= 3`) AND have no trunk within a 2-column radius — i.e. ground-level leaf clumps with no trunk = bushes; tree canopies (trunk nearby) and elevated leaves are kept. Console logs `[tinyworld] hole-fill: enclosed=N dents=M floorCols=K` and `[tinyworld] bush-cull: removed N of M`. Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-BuDiCdvF.js`), `zo-space` restarted, served 200. Headless-verified: 23,711 scan → enclosed=45 dents=5, bush-cull 166/540, groundMap 4533->4575, `loopError===false`; 55,255 OpenMVS scan → enclosed=14 dents=7 (floorCols=15095), bush-cull 582/3627, `loopError===false`; third-person screenshot of the 55k scan shows a solid continuous grass floor with tree canopy intact. **Note for tuning:** these scans have FEW enclosed floor holes (the floor is mostly solid; the visible "gaps" are the void between separate floating fragments, which is deliberately NOT bridged — that would merge distinct islands and alter the world). If KJ wants those fragment-gaps bridged, that's a separate, more aggressive change needing explicit sign-off. Bush removal is the larger visible effect. Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-063701.gait-size-v1.tsx` (prior) and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-065542.holefill-bushcull-deployed.tsx` (this).

Jun 26 bush-cull + hole-fill STRENGTHEN (follow-up to the entry above): KJ sent a staging screenshot ("not sure if i see either fix") still showing green-blanketed ground and floor gaps. The prior pass WAS deployed and compiled, but was too conservative for dense photogrammetry scans (only ~16% of leaves culled, ~7 dents filled). Two edits in `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx`. (1) **Bush-cull rewritten to key off real trunk HEIGHT** (~line 2638): build `trunkTop` (column → max trunk y); a column is a genuine tree only if its tallest trunk rises `>= TREE_MIN (4)` voxels above ground `H`. Keep a leaf only if it is very high (`above >= FORCE_HIGH (7)`) OR (`above >= CANOPY_MIN (3)` AND within `NEAR (2)` columns of a real tree). This keeps canopy on tall trunks and culls the low ground-level green that blankets terrain, regardless of stray short/decorative trunks. (2) **Hole-fill dent pass loosened** (~line 2601): now fills any empty cell touching floor on `>= 2` of 4 sides (was `>=3` or two-opposite), capped at 5 passes. **Island-bridging safety:** cnt>=2 fill can only close 1-wide cracks — a gap 2+ cells wide always has a frontier cell with <2 floor neighbours, so distinct floating islands never merge (verified mathematically, since headless can't force a clean top-down — teleport y is clamped to ground in walk/drone). Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-Bbfs8J-r.js`), `zo-space` restarted; live chain verified `dist/index.html → index-BwwWNgQ0.js → tinyworld-staging-Bbfs8J-r.js` with both `bush-cull`/`hole-fill` strings present. Headless-verified across worlds, `loopError===false` on all: 55,255 OpenMVS scan → bush-cull 1707/2289 (was 582), dents 924 (was 7), trees preserved + ground clumps gone in ground screenshot; 114,288 OpenMVS scan → enclosed=37 dents=1062 floorCols=18752, bush-cull 1453/3088. NOT headless-verifiable (1fps, GLB never loads via esm.sh, no clean overhead): the actual on-device look/feel — confirm on KJ's device at https://kj.zo.space/tinyworld-staging. If still too green/too gappy, tune `TREE_MIN`/`CANOPY_MIN`/`FORCE_HIGH`/`NEAR` (cull) and the dent `cnt>=2`→ lower bound (fill). Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-115628.pre-bushfix.tsx` (prior) and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-120548.post-bushfix.tsx` (this).

Jun 26 veg-removal regression fix + camera decouple v2: The first walk-feel pass nulled the `grass`/`leaves`/`trunks` layer buffers, which KJ reported punched holes in the floor (grass/trunks are load-bearing surface in decorated worlds) and removed ALL trees. Reverted that approach in `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx`: restored all three layers (geometry + trees back, holes gone) and instead added a `collidable = true` param to `addLayer` that guards the `addCol` call; `leaves` is now passed `collidable=false` so bushes + canopy still render but you walk through them (grass/trunks stay solid floor). Note: green grass floor + bushes are still *visible* (removing them = holes) — if KJ wants them gone visually, next step is height-based culling of ground-level leaf instances (skip leaf blocks within ~2 voxels of column ground top) while keeping elevated canopy. Camera decouple: the v1 latch only checked `lookJoystickRef.active`, but mobile look is usually **drag-to-look** (`touchLookRef`), so the latch never engaged → "camera still follows sentinel." Fixed the latch condition to `lookJoystickRef.active || touchLookRef.current.active`. Root cause of the mech swinging on look: `renderSentinel` bolted the body to the LIVE camera yaw (`bodyX/Z = camera.pos + camForward*fwd`, `rotation.y = camera.rotation.y + PI`). Now the body anchors to the latched travel heading (`moveYawRef`) for both position-forward and facing, so sweeping the view orbits the gaze around a stationary mech instead of swinging it. `moveYawRef` seeded to `camera.rotation.y` on walk entry (line ~3473) to avoid a yaw=0 anchor before first step; it syncs to camera yaw each moving+not-looking frame and freezes while looking. Walk speed `*0.5` and gait timeScale `0.16` from the prior pass kept. Build clean (`bun run build`, bundle `tinyworld-staging-DzTGGtVK.js`), `zo-space` restarted, served 200. Headless on `w_prog_mqtlf00g_i5he3x`: harness built, `window.__twLoopError === false`. NOT verifiable headless (GLB never loads via esm.sh; mech swing + walk-through bushes + holes need a decorated world on KJ's device): must be eyeballed by KJ. Backups: `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-051540.post-zelda-veg.tsx` (broken veg-null version) and `.route-backups/tinyworld-staging.20260626-053015.veg-revert-decouple.tsx` (this fix).

Jun 26 headless viewing/testing workflow correction: The old assumption that Sentinel/GLB cannot be tested headlessly was wrong. Use `agent-browser` against local Space (`http://localhost:3099/tinyworld-staging?...`) to actually view and test the page. Best path: load a specific saved world with `?world=<world_id>&debug=1`, wait for `LOADED ... BLOCKS FROM SAVED WORLD`, then inspect `window.__tw.state()`, `window.__twLoopError`, browser console, and screenshots. Useful examples: `agent-browser open 'http://localhost:3099/tinyworld-staging?world=w_glb_mqtq2d9a_q8rkfh&debug=1'`; `agent-browser console --clear`; `agent-browser errors --clear`; `agent-browser eval 'JSON.stringify({ready:!!window.__tw, loopError:!!window.__twLoopError, state:window.__tw?.state?.()})'`; `agent-browser screenshot <path>.png`. `__tw.setPilotMode("cockpit"|"third"|"drone")` can force modes after load, and keyboard events can exercise movement; `simWalk` is not reliable for the current walking state. Use screenshots and pixel/nonblank checks for framing/rendering, and console counters for terrain passes. Headless runs at ~1 fps, so it is good for load correctness, render/framing, mode crashes, GLB visibility, terrain pass counters, and obvious camera placement; KJ's device is still needed for high-fidelity motion feel.

Jun 26 Depth/TSDF removal: KJ identified a new `Progressive Scan 2026-06-26` (`w_prog_mquz7l2f_lwosk3`) as accidentally using the old Depth/RGB-D TSDF path (`video_to_voxels.py:progressive`, `depth-anything-v2-metric-indoor-small + open3d-tsdf`). Jun 26 OpenMVS floor-flatten v2: KJ's screenshot of latest scan `w_glb_mqv04joc_x8upei` showed the correct OpenMVS/GLB pipeline was active, but quality looked lower because `tools/glb_to_world_payload.py` was choosing the *lowest* up-facing mesh sample per `(x,z)` column, which often selected undersides/sloped scan noise and defeated floor flattening. Patched the converter to collect all up-facing floor samples per column, find the dominant walkable plane, choose the candidate nearest that plane, then snap a wider band flat (`floor_snap_band=18`, final snap `<=8`). Regenerated only the latest capture payload from `project-photogrammetry/scans/scan_from_cap_mqv04jhu_rcdqi6/openmvs/scene_aligned.glb` and updated `world_blocks`/`worlds` for `w_glb_mqv04joc_x8upei`; old payload backed up at `debug-shots/20260626T-floor-flatten-v2/w_glb_mqv04joc_x8upei.pre-floor-flatten-v2.json`. Verification: dryGrass floor went from 18.1% at `y=0` to 99.1% at `y=0`; grass is now 100% at `y=1`; staging load via `http://localhost:3099/tinyworld-staging?world=w_glb_mqv04joc_x8upei&debug=1` reports `LOADED 51,788 BLOCKS FROM SAVED WORLD`, `window.__twLoopError === false`, hole-fill `enclosed=4 dents=759`, bush-cull `542/1111`, screenshot saved at `debug-shots/20260626T-floor-flatten-v2/w_glb_mqv04joc_x8upei-after-drone-mid.png`. Jun 26 capture duration + filming guidance: After proving the current COLMAP/OpenMVS pipeline reproduces good 06-25 results, KJ asked to increase capture length and guide filming for parallax. `/tinyworld/capture` now starts with a 40-second scan bank (`SCAN_BANK_START_S = 40`) and auto-stops at that target. UI guidance explicitly tells users to walk sideways or in a slow arc, keep the same subject in view, avoid spinning/pivoting in place, and move slowly with overlap. Recording state shows elapsed seconds, a `40s target` progress bar, and repeats the lateral/arc cue. `/api/tinyworld-infer` scene prompt updated from 30-second to 40-second walkthrough. The OpenMVS finalizer still extracts at `--fps 2`, so a full 40s capture yields ~80 frames instead of ~60; quality still depends on translated viewpoints, not duration alone. Build clean (`bun run build`, capture bundle `tinyworld-capture-DCkZlswj.js`), `zo-space` restarted, browser snapshot verified `SCAN BANK 40.0s` and the new guidance on `http://localhost:3099/tinyworld/capture`.

Jun 26 live sensor capture coach: `/tinyworld/capture` now uses the already-recorded IMU stream during recording to show a live guidance card. `analyzeCaptureCoach()` reads the last ~1.6s of `DeviceMotionEvent` samples and classifies motion as `GOOD ARC MOTION`, `TOO MUCH PIVOT`, `TURN SLOWER`, `TOO SHAKY`, `MOVE SIDEWAYS MORE`, or sensor unavailable/warming up. Heuristic is intentionally simple: high gyro rotation with low lateral acceleration = pivoting in place (the capture pattern that starves COLMAP parallax); high vertical shake warns to slow down; low motion warns to add sidestep/arc; otherwise it rewards steady sideways/arc motion. The card shows the instruction, a score bar, and raw `ROT` / `MOTION` readouts when IMU is supported. Build clean (`bun run build`, capture bundle `tinyworld-capture-DBxIsyCu.js`), `zo-space` restarted, compiled bundle contains all coach states, and `http://localhost:3099/tinyworld/capture` serves 200 + loads in the browser harness. Camera/IMU permission and live thresholds must be checked on a real iPhone; headless cannot supply DeviceMotion data.

Jun 26 cockpit / first-person sunset (Player/Sentinel/Drone zone): KJ asked to remove/sunset first-person, so it's disabled reversibly in **both** `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld.tsx` and `tinyworld-staging.tsx`. `viewModeRef`/`viewMode` now default to `"third"` (was `"cockpit"`), and a co-located `const COCKPIT_ENABLED = false;` gates the CPIT/3RD toggle button (`{COCKPIT_ENABLED && sentinelMode === "large" && (…)}`). All cockpit render code (the staging multi-panel windshield ~7790, the `viewMode === "cockpit"` HUD block ~8061, `inCockpit` branches) is left intact but unreachable. To revive: flip `COCKPIT_ENABLED` to `true` in both files (and re-default if desired). Staging keeps its debug `__tw.setPilotMode("cockpit")` hook so cockpit can still be tested headless. Verified: `bun run build` clean (prod `tinyworld-CLhcj-7M.js`, staging `tinyworld-staging-DGA1_3iv.js`), `zo-space` restarted, both routes serve 200, minifier dead-code-eliminated the toggle (`grep -c CPIT` = 0 in both bundles), and headless staging load of `w_prog_mqtlf00g_i5he3x` enters walk as `viewMode:"third"` with `__twLoopError` false (screenshot shows the mech in chase view, no windshield/HUD).

Jun 26 chase-camera occlusion (Player/Sentinel/Drone zone, staging only): KJ asked the 3rd-person chase camera to avoid "bumping into or obscuring" — i.e. the lens must not clip into terrain behind the mech, nor let geometry sit between camera and mech. Implemented as a voxel-raymarch occlusion pass in the chase override block of `tinyworld-staging.tsx` (replacing the old simple `lerp k=dt*8`). After computing the ideal trailing position (`tx/ty/tz`, `followDist=SENTINEL_TARGET_H*3.2`, `followHeight=SENTINEL_TARGET_H*1.5`), it casts a ray from the mid-torso pivot (`feetY+SENTINEL_TARGET_H*0.55`) toward the ideal position and marches `solidWorld()` (wraps existing `solidAt` via `Math.round(w/voxel)+cxRound/czRound`) every `voxel*0.5`. On first solid hit it pulls the lens to `d - camMargin` (`camMargin=voxel*0.8`), floored at `minChaseDist=SENTINEL_TARGET_H*0.55`. Smoothing is asymmetric — fast pull-in (`dt*24`) so the camera never stays buried, slow ease-out (`dt*8`) so it doesn't pop when obstacles clear — plus a hard clamp re-projecting the smoothed point onto the clear segment, and a floor guard (`sampleGround+voxel*0.6`) for downhill dips. Note: non-collidable leaves are excluded from `colMap`, so tree canopy is intentionally ignored (only trunks/terrain occlude). Cockpit/drone paths untouched. Verified: `bun run build` clean (`tinyworld-staging-B7xuBNa5.js`, 190.94 kB), `zo-space` restarted, route 200, headless `w_prog_mqtlf00g_i5he3x` and `w_glb_mqtok9l1_9ea9n1` both `__twLoopError` false (incl. while driving 'w'), camera demonstrably hugging terrain (horizontal offset collapsing toward 0 in boxed-in scan geometry). Backups in `.route-backups/` (`tinyworld-staging.pre-camocclusion-20260626-201021.tsx`, post-edit twin). Subjective feel/framing in open terrain needs KJ's device — `gotoWorker` parks the camera inside dense scan geometry, so clean open-field framing isn't reproducible headless. Likely tuning knobs: `camMargin`, `minChaseDist`, lerp rates 24/8.

Jun 27 3RD camera framing + gait tune (Player/Sentinel/Drone zone, staging only): KJ reported the post-occlusion chase camera was better but skewed to one side, with Sentinel too high in frame, and asked to slow animation another 10%. In `/__substrate/space/routes/pages/tinyworld-staging.tsx`, changed `GAIT_SYNC` from `0.32` to `0.288` (travel speed unchanged), raised the chase look-at pivot from `feetY + H*0.55` to `feetY + H*0.88` so the taller mech sits lower/closer to center, increased close-occlusion minimum distance from `H*0.55` to `H*0.9` to reduce extreme side crowding when boxed in, and explicitly reset camera up/roll in the chase render (`camera.up.set(0,1,0)`, `camera.rotation.z=0`). Jun 28 phase-matched stop / stride-synced settle (Player/Sentinel/Drone zone, staging only): KJ's idea after the StopPlant revert — no new clip, reuse the existing Run/WalkSlow locomotion clip. On movement release while standing, the clip is NOT frozen on a random mid-stride frame; `tickSentinel` keeps advancing `walkAction.time` along an ease-out to the nearest "passing pose" (legs under the body), then freezes there (timeScale 0). Since there is no separate Standing clip, the neutral stance IS a chosen frame of the Run clip. Implemented in the `sentinelAnimState === "standing"` branch of `tickSentinel` in `/__substrate/space/src/tw-staging-app.tsx` (~line 6348): a live-tunable `SENT_STOP` config (`enabled`, `phases:[0.25,0.75]` passing-pose targets in [0..1), `settleDur:0.42`, `minSpan:0.08`), settle state vars, and a release-edge detector (`sentinelPrevGaitTS > 0.0001 && sentinelGaitTS <= 0.0001`) that picks the nearest forward phase target ≥ `minSpan`, scrubs the clip over `settleDur*(span/0.5)` with an ease-out (`1-(1-k)^2`), then sets `sentinelStopped`. Re-acceleration (`gaitTS>0`) cancels the settle so the mech runs straight back out of a planted stance. Debug/tuning hooks on `window.__tw`: `sentinelFreezeAt(p)` (freeze WalkSlow at phase p to eyeball the stance and find the real passing frame), `sentStop` (mutate the config live), `sentinelAnim()` (state/phase/curTS/gaitTS/settling/stopped/actions readout). Build clean (`tinyworld-staging` chunk 192.20 kB), `zo-space` restarted, `get_space_errors` empty. Headless-verified end-to-end on the 169,174-block OpenMVS world (embodied 3RD via `__tw.setPilotMode('third')`): mid-run `gaitTS:0.384, curTS:0.269`; on KeyW release `settling:true` → phase eases 0.79→0.245 → lands EXACTLY on passing-pose `phase:0.25, stopped:true` and holds; `sentinelFreezeAt(0.25)`/`(0.75)` scrub `walkAction.time` to 0.158s/0.475s of the 0.633s clip; `window.__twLoopError === false` throughout. Smoke scripts: `tools/sentinel-stop-smoke.sh` + `tools/sentinel-settle-edge.sh`. Backup: `.route-backups/tw-staging-app-20260628T145632Z-sentinel-phasematched-stop.tsx`. NOT device-verified (state machine is proven but headless runs ~1 fps, so the deceleration FEEL is not observable): the actual passing-pose frames + the slow-down feel must be dialed on KJ's iPhone — sweep `__tw.sentinelFreezeAt(p)` over p∈[0,1], find where the legs sit cleanly under the body, then set `SENT_STOP.phases[]`. Known v1 limitation: the body is stationary during the brief settle while the legs finish the stride → minor residual foot-slide; add a short forward coast if it reads as sliding on device.

Jun 28 settle → standing Idle-loop crossfade (Player/Sentinel/Drone zone, staging only): Follow-on to the phase-matched stop. Previously, once the legs settled to the passing pose the clip was FROZEN there (timeScale 0) — a dead, posed Run frame. The Sentinel GLB actually ships an authored standing Idle (subclip frames 0-56, also aliased as `Sitting`/used only in overview), which was never played during walk-mode standstill. Now, after the settle completes (or whenever the mech is at rest in `state:"standing"`), it crossfades into that Idle loop so the standstill is a living stance instead of a frozen frame. Implemented in the `"standing"` branch of `tickSentinel` in `/__substrate/space/src/tw-staging-app.tsx` (~line 6357): `idleAction = sentinelActions["Idle"]`, two idempotent edge-triggered closures `fadeToIdle()` / `fadeToWalk()` (guarded by a new `sentinelIdling` flag), and a restructured released-branch that calls `fadeToIdle()` after `!sentinelSettling`. Movement resume (`gaitTS>0`) calls `fadeToWalk()` to crossfade Idle→WalkSlow. Two new `SENT_STOP` keys: `idleOnStop:true` (A/B toggle — set `__tw.sentStop.idleOnStop=false` to revert to the old freeze) and `idleFade:0.3` (crossfade seconds). `sentinelAnim()` now also reports `idling`. Build clean (`tinyworld-staging` chunk 192.62 kB), `zo-space` restarted via `supervisorctl -s http://127.0.0.1:29001 restart zo-space`, served bundle confirmed to contain the new code, `get_space_errors` empty. Headless-verified end-to-end on the 169,174-block OpenMVS world (embodied 3RD): at rest `idling:true` (8 actions incl. Idle subclipped from the single GLB take); press W → `fadeToWalk`, `gaitTS:0.384, idling:false`; release → `settling:true`, phase eases 0.94→0.199 → lands on passing-pose `phase:0.25, stopped:true` and **then crossfades to `idling:true`**; `__twLoopError===false` throughout. Debug shot (runtime emissive-orange tint on the 25 Sentinel SkinnedMeshes so the grey mech reads against the grey scan — tint is browser-only, never touches source) sent to KJ's Telegram. Backup: `.route-backups/tw-staging-app-20260628T161153Z-sentinel-idle-crossfade.tsx`. NOT device-verified — headless ~1 fps can't judge whether the Idle stance + fade timing read well; KJ must eyeball on iPhone and tune `idleFade` / toggle `idleOnStop`.

Jun 28 organic noise rolling hills (voxel pipeline, baked-once): KJ asked for the "best looking" rolling hills and confirmed bake-once is fine ("once voxelized once no need to change"). The prior `rolling_hills()` in `tools/glb_to_world_payload.py` was a deterministic tiling sine "egg-carton" — identical in every world. Replaced it with 3-octave value noise (`_value_noise`/`_lattice`/`_smootherstep`; base lattice = `HILL_WAVELENGTH=36` cells, halving spacing+amplitude per octave) plus a two-pass contrast-stretch (raw value noise clusters near its mean, so pass 1 collects per-cell noise + min/max, pass 2 remaps `(raw-lo)/span` to fill `[0, HILL_AMPLITUDE=3]`). Per-world seed = `fnv1a(resolve(source_glb_path))` (Python's built-in `hash()` is randomized across runs, so unusable) wired at the `convert()` call site and recorded in `meta.inferredFrom.hills` with `floor_plane='dominant-low-plane+rolling-hills-noise'`. Displacement is biased non-negative so the dominant plane stays the valley floor and walls sit flush. Walkability preserved: validated max adjacent floor-cell diff = 1 (no >=2 steps → the y-1 dirt underside leaves no gaps), full [0,3] range, deterministic, ~47% of cells differ between two seeds. Regenerated all 4 saved worlds into `data/revoxel-payloads-hills-noise/` and applied to `data/tinyworld.db` (each gets a distinct seed): `w_glb_mqv04joc_x8upei` 168,850 blk seed 4228724101, `w_glb_mqtq2d9a_q8rkfh` 465,755 seed 3610965735, `w_glb_mqtok9l1_9ea9n1` 202,976 seed 1154152962, `w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu` 229,622 seed 1489808653. Block counts ~unchanged vs prior sine hills (geometry-neutral aside from floor undulation); `worlds.base_blocks` updated to match each new count. Pre-apply backups: `data/backups/world_blocks_backup_<world_id>_pre_noise_hills_1782668318.json` (rollback = restore that payload/block_count/resolution + reset base_blocks). NOT device-verified — the actual look of the organic hills must be eyeballed by KJ on a loaded world; the change is numerically validated (walkable, non-tiling, per-world-distinct) only.

Jun 28 light eroded cliff edges (voxel pipeline, baked-once): KJ asked to make the islands' flat void-adjacent faces "a little more interesting" — "only cliff for now, lightly, not too extreme", no grass tufts, no bridges (bridges conflict with the scan-to-connect mechanic). Added `cliff_edges()` to `tools/glb_to_world_payload.py`: for each floor column on a void edge (>=1 of its 4 orthogonal neighbours empty), hang up to `CLIFF_DEPTH=2` extra voxels below the existing y-1 dirt underside by a coherent `_value_noise` amount in `[0, depth]` (`CLIFF_WAVELENGTH=12` cells of coherence → contiguous deeper/shallower runs, not per-cell speckle); first extra voxel is dirt, anything deeper is stone → grass→loam→rock strata on the tallest sections. **Underside-only by construction → walkability is untouched** (the walk surface never changes; validated 0 columns raised). Separate sub-seed `fnv1a(resolve(path)+":cliff")` so cliffs vary independently of hills; recorded in `meta.inferredFrom.cliffs` (`method='value-noise-edge'`). Re-voxelized all 4 worlds into `data/revoxel-payloads-hills-cliffs/` — **hill seeds all MATCH the prior bake (hills preserved exactly)** — and applied to `data/tinyworld.db`: `w_glb_mqv04joc_x8upei` 169,157 blk (+307) cliff seed 4091437273, `w_glb_mqtq2d9a_q8rkfh` 467,073 (+1318) seed 1804352771, `w_glb_mqtok9l1_9ea9n1` 204,065 (+1089) seed 4119446566, `w_prog_mqsrje6j_r9bmnu` 230,778 (+1156) seed 3069274993; `worlds.base_blocks` updated to match. Re-voxelizing causes a tiny (~0.1%) deterministic cascade through `prune_floating`/stone-accent-cap (a few scattered wall/stone fragments reshuffle) — invisible, no surface/hill impact. Backups: full file `data/tinyworld.db.bak-PRE-CLIFFS-1782670490` + per-row `data/backups/world_blocks_backup_<world_id>_pre_cliffs_1782670490.json`. NOT device-verified — the cliff look must be eyeballed by KJ on a loaded world; numerically validated (additive, underside-only, per-world-distinct, hills preserved) only.

Jun 28 Sentinel real cast shadow via shadow-camera follow (Player/Sentinel/Drone zone, staging only): KJ rejected the earlier fake contact-blob shadow ("shadow is on under sentinel - the wrong approach - should follow the same shadows as rest of the geometry - trees as well") and wants the Sentinel to cast a REAL directional shadow in the same shadow-map pass as the trees/terrain. **Root cause of the original "no shadow" (confirmed live via `__tw.shadowDiag()`):** all 25 Sentinel meshes already had `castShadow=true` — the flag was never the problem. The world's single directional shadow map covers the whole island (`frustumHalf ≈ span*1.4 ≈ 463`, mapSize 2048²) → **0.45 world-units/texel**, so the mech footprint (~6.8·voxel ≈ 1.16 u) lands on only **~2.6 shadow texels** = sub-texel = invisible, while large cliffs/terrain resolve fine. Fix = **shadow-camera follow** in `/__substrate/space/src/tw-staging-app.tsx`: when the Sentinel is the hero subject (`walkingRef.current && sentinelModeRef.current==="large" && sentinelGroup.visible`), `updateSunShadow()` (called each frame after `renderSentinel`) re-centers the sun's shadow camera tightly on the mech — `SHADOW_FOLLOW={enabled, half:24, dist:span*1.5}`, near `dist*0.25`/far `dist*2` — giving **0.0234 u/texel → ~49.4 texels** on the mech (19× denser), a crisp resolvable shadow from the SAME pass the trees use; it falls back to the wide world frustum (`half=span*1.4`) in orbit where the mech is tiny and the whole island must self-shadow. Supporting edits: `scene.add(sun.target)` so moving the follow target updates its matrixWorld; the sun direction is now a persistent `sunDir` vector (`forceSun` can pin it via `_sunDirLocked`) and the TOD tint loop only re-derives `sunDir` (no longer sets `sun.position`, which `updateSunShadow` now owns). **Also fixed a TDZ bug introduced alongside this:** the `__tw` diagnostic block read `SHADOW_FOLLOW` 358 lines before its `const` declaration → a `ReferenceError` that threw mid-render-setup and silently killed the `shadowFollow`/`sentinelCast`/`shadowDiag` hooks (only `forceSun`, set just before the throw, survived); moved the `SHADOW_FOLLOW` const above the `__tw` block. Live-tunable debug hooks kept (consistent with the other Sentinel hooks): `__tw.shadowDiag()` (mode/frustumHalf/uPerTexel/mechFootTexels/mechCasting readout), `__tw.shadowFollow` (mutate `half`/`dist`/`enabled` live), `__tw.sentinelCast(bool)` (toggle all 25 meshes' castShadow for A/B), `__tw.forceSun(az,alt|null)` (pin/release the sun angle). Build clean (`tinyworld-staging-BjST0-Tn.js`), `zo-space` restarted; on the 169,157-block OpenMVS world (3RD) `shadowDiag()` flips `wide→follow` on entering third-person (`frustumHalf 463→24`, `uPerTexel 0.45→0.023`, `mechFootTexels 2.6→49.4`, 25/25 casting), `__twLoopError` false. Backup: `.route-backups/tw-staging-app-20260628T193727Z-sentinel-shadow-follow.tsx` (rejected blob archived at `…20260628T191612Z-blob-attempt.tsx`, clean pre-shadow base at `…185245Z-pre-sentinel-shadow.tsx`). NOT device-verified: headless software WebGL at ~1 fps renders the shadow only faintly — a temporal-averaged A/B pixel diff (mech frozen, 5 ON vs 5 OFF frames) shows the mech consistently darkens ~270 px by ~14 luma near its feet/foreground grass that vanishes when `castShadow` is off (residual dither noise only 53 px), i.e. measurable shadow presence, but it does NOT render as a crisp mech-shaped shadow headless. KJ must eyeball on iPhone whether the shadow reads as a proper directional shadow like the trees, and whether the bias (currently the wide-frustum `bias -0.0005`/`normalBias 0.02`) needs retuning for the 19×-tighter follow frustum (watch for shadow acne or peter-panning / a gap between the feet and the shadow); main tuning knob is `__tw.shadowFollow.half` (24).

Jul 2 worker growth/food/comfort/towers implemented (Game Systems zone, staging only): Full implementation of `docs/worker-growth-mechs-and-food.md` in `/__substrate/space/src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. Tiers Speck/Mote/Puff/Elder (1/2/4/8 max energy; sprite scale 0.7/1.0/1.4/1.9; GP thresholds 10/30/80); pace derives from FILL FRACTION so all prior pace math survives. GP sources: work throughput (harvest/place/build-complete hooks), sheltered rest streaks (night cycle awards GP at dawn for a warm enclosed night), food — and food GATES banking (max +3 GP past last meal; `gpAtLastMeal`). Food: fruit staple, grass graze (energy only, no GP), nectar premium (3 fruit → 1 at the press, +8 GP, Elder-gated). Comfort (spec §3.1) is a slow EMA updated at dawn from home-shell warmth (material `MAT_WARMTH`), housemate MBTI compat, meal regularity, and tower-beam coverage; multiplies GP gain 0.5×–1.5×. Materials matter: enclosure flood-fill now reports shell warmth (rest multiplier) and resilience (void-bite resistance on the built structure). Towers: no blueprint — recognized by construction pattern (≤3×3 base, height ≥6, open sky, pure lens cap) via a main-loop scan + player-place candidate hook; beams give comfort coverage. Dawn arrivals spawn as tier-1 Specks joining homes with spare enclosed capacity (SHELTER_QUOTA); workers spawn at FULL energy; growth persists across reloads via the worker save match. `__tw` levers: `growthReport()`, `feed(id)`, `setTier(t,id)`, `setEnergy(e,id)`, `nectar(n)`, `towerReport()`, `arrive()`. **TDZ bug found in final verification (world load failed: "Cannot access 'fs' before initialization"):** the spawn-full-energy edit put `W_TIER_CAP[2]` inside `createWorker`, which the synchronous open-ground spawn block calls ~170 lines BEFORE the growth consts initialized — same failure class as the Jun 28 `SHADOW_FOLLOW` TDZ; moved the `W_TIER_*` const block above `createWorker`. (Rule: any const read by `createWorker`/the spawn path must be declared above it — that whole region executes synchronously during world load.) Build clean (`tinyworld-staging-DW6GOvCM.js`), `zo-space` restarted. Headless-verified on `w_glb_mr0rdeik_ae66ed`: world loads (levers attach ~20s), `growthReport()` shows worker-1 as full-energy Mote (1.97/2 after 19s work) with clean gp/comfort/bank fields, `feed`/`setTier`/`setEnergy`/`towerReport` respond, `nectar` correctly gated on a built press, `arrive()` correctly null with no settled homes, no loop error. Backup: `.route-backups/tw-staging-app-20260702T200101Z-worker-growth-spec.tsx`. NOT device-verified: GP pacing feel, tier-up molt moment, sprite scale read, comfort-driven eye expressions, tower ignition in real play — KJ must eyeball; dawn-arrival Speck spawn needs a settled colony (long-run or device).

Jul 3 void v1 seek-arrival freeze fixed + full carrier loop verified live (Game Systems zone, staging only): Post-build verification of void v1 (Jul 2 build: dark motes, void ships, island accretion per `docs/void-motes-ship-and-moon.md`; supermoon deferred) found every dark mote frozen forever in `seek`: the seek-arrival check in `/__substrate/space/src/tw-staging-app.tsx` was a **3D** `Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz) < 0.8`, but the surface-clamp holds motes at `columnTop + 1.2` while seek targets are set to the block's own y (`pick.vy` = column top) — a guaranteed permanent 1.2 vertical gap, so "arrived" never fired and the pry branch was unreachable (the pre-fix island grass block came from the load-time catch-up haul, not the live loop). Fix: horizontal-only arrival `Math.hypot(dx,dz) < 0.8`, matching the flee/carry/deliver checks which were already horizontal — only this one was 3D. (Rule: any proximity check against a mote/ship position must be horizontal-only; the surface-clamp owns y.) Build clean (`tinyworld-staging-2Ml6uSG-.js`), `zo-space` restarted (0 captures inferring). Verified end-to-end headless on `w_glb_mr0rdeik_ae66ed` (saved-world entry; NOTE: on `localhost:3099` routes have NO `/pages/` prefix — `/tinyworld-staging`; the `/pages/` form 404s client-side; `agent-browser set geo <lat> <lon>` exists but headless still reports geolocation permission denied, the saved-world button bypasses the gate): night ship spawned naturally → inbound ~800 units (several real minutes each way) → landed at weak frontier → mote seek→pry→carry→deliver ×2 (hold 0→2, both natural) → `__tw.forceLiftoff()` → voyage → island accreted 1→3 in REAL materials (`grass/trunks/dryGrass`), HUD shows "ISLE 3"; ledger `balanced:true` `worldDrift:0` throughout; `__twLoopError` false; "While you were away" recap correctly reported the void raid ("void ship landed 1 time — 1 block ferried to the island"). Note: the ledger `void` bucket also absorbs vegetation-sim grow/remove flux (goes negative when the world grows organically) — not a bug. Dawn-triggered liftoff not observed live (forced via lever; same code path as hold-full). NOT device-verified: dark-mote/ship/island visuals — the 3-block island is a sub-pixel speck headless; KJ must eyeball on device.

Jul 3 void spec v10 — KJ ruled the ENTIRE remaining open-call board (design only, no code): `docs/void-motes-ship-and-moon.md` bumped v9→v10 with every §15 open call resolved and the rulings folded into body sections. Headline rulings: #2 no tap-to-drop but the player CAN build in real time mid-raid (§3); #7 dawn = drop-and-flee; #9 ships protection-gated + NEW mechanic: workers deliver a raid forecast when the player enters the world (§4, `raidForecast()` lever added to §14); #10 loaded carriers walk, only the ship flies; #4 rare tiers covet value and may strike BUILT settlements (KJ's "maybe" — tunable); #3 un-grading only on the moon (island grade holds indefinitely — the "N nights" clock is gone); #5 island visible from far away; #6 island architecture = mix of void-world + normal rebuilt structures; #11 bridge or raft both valid but the void tries to destroy bridges; #12 escalation = bigger slower moon ships, maybe followed by re-seeded islands (cyclical chapters); #13 ferry overflow mechanism ok (tuning); #16 moon visible during day + supermoon-tier attacks can come by day (answers KJ's "are raids always at night?" — night-only is chapter 1 only; curve shape sublinear/soft-cap stands as adopted); #14 griefing = all users the same, geo presence grants the view WITHOUT the visitor's sentinel, sentinels relocate only over built bridges (§9); #17 individual dark motes also grow (tier on stolen matter, crew void mechs); #18 conversion requires stranding (no mid-raid befriending); #19 no relapse ever; #20 conversion yields the mote only (no substance mass; dowry #31 is the separate mech mass); #8 dark motes eyeless AT FIRST (eyes = conversion progress); #21 suggestion boundary as stated; #22 free LLM dialogue Elder-only; #23 attention DOES feed the loop (chat grants comfort AND is the command/suggestion channel, still refusable per #21); #26 corruption seed yes — large raw stockpile is an anti-tower drawing landings; #27 recognizer: cockpit-only statue is valid-but-useless, mass/density law self-balances; #36a every grove-tree is a grave + ALL trees glow at night (world-wide night visual). ONLY REMAINING OPEN CALL: #36b — do memorial groves radiate a comfort aura. Implementation of the v10 rulings (void v1 extensions) NOT yet requested.

## Open aesthetic question (Jun 22, needs KJ's call)

The current aesthetic pillars (`docs/aesthetic-pillars.md`) and Tiny People doc (`docs/tiny-people.md`) are locked to a **melancholic survival-horror** identity — Hades / NieR: Automata / Transistor / Twilight Princess, Ghibli-style pixel billboards, scavenged-rust + glowing-tech contrast. KJ has signaled a strong UI preference for **Breath of the Wild / Tears of the Kingdom**: 3D cel-shaded, physics-based **building + snapping + flying**, and "phased loading as part of the game." These two directions are in tension (pixel-billboard sprites vs. 3D cel-shaded; melancholic survival-horror vs. emergent physics sandbox). This is a creative-direction call, not a factual fix — do **not** rewrite the pillars unilaterally. Surface it; let KJ decide: full pivot to BotW/TotK, a hybrid, or keep current. The phased-loading-as-gameplay decision (#3/#11) already leans BotW/TotK regardless of the character-render call.

## Conventions

- Design specs live in `docs/` as markdown
- DB is committed for now (prototype data); move to Supabase before any real users
- Biome specs named `biome-<region>-<climate>.md`
- Heavy/long-running Zo jobs (voxelizer, GLB conversion, render, Space build, DSM/EPT fetch — anything blocking >~30s): background as a detached job that writes to a log, then poll the log instead of blocking the turn; chunk long ops so each step returns quickly with progress; keep context lean (`/clear` between big tasks). The Zo watchdog reclaims a child after ~919s of silence, so never let a single tool call sit silent for minutes.

Jul 3 Void v10 rulings implemented + verified (staging): built KJ's v10 void-spec rulings into `src/tw-staging-app.tsx` in two passes, autonomous loop while KJ rested. Pass 1 — (#9) worker-spoken raid forecast on world entry (8s after init) and again at dusk: `computeRaidForecast()` picks the weakest open (non-beam-covered) frontier cell, reports an 8-point compass octant with ±1 octant fuzz at worker tier ≤2 and an exact cell at tier ≥4, goes quiet when median protection is high or no frontier is open; delivered as a speech-bubble sprite (`moteSay`, 12s fade) over the worker nearest the camera — no UI, just a voice in the world. (#17) dark motes grow: tier by lifetime `stolen` (≥3 → 1.25x scale/1.4x pry, ≥8 → 1.5x/1.8x). (#18) stranding: at dawn a landed ship waits `DAWN_FLEE_MS` 45s for its motes; at the deadline any mote >8 cells (`STRAND_BOARD_RADIUS`) is STRANDED instead of boarded — dawn-only by design ("the sun strands them"; hold-full/forced liftoffs still board all). (#8) stranded motes are eyeless at first: eyes sprite at opacity 0.05, opening to 1.0 with conversion progress; each dawn a *warm* stranded mote (under a beam or within 6 cells of a worker home) gains +0.25, plus +0.25 if the colony can spare a fruit (`ledgerMove stockpile→void "mote_feed"` — fed with the same food the workers eat); cold-and-alone motes make no progress, rules do the storytelling. (#19) no relapse: `voidConverted` count persists and permanently shrinks `darkAllotment`. (#20) conversion yields a mote only: a tier-1 Speck worker with dark hull tint (closure param into `buildWorkerVisual`, not userData — GLTF callback races the userData reassignment) that works the night shift (`wNight = converted ? !night : night`). (#4) rare value-hunter: 20% roll once per aggressive night, gold gem crest, targets the nearest *graded* block (fallback: worker homes), ignores protection repel. No rescue of stranded motes by later ships — conversion is their only arc. Pass 2 — (#36a) all trees glow at night: `leafGlowMats` registry (base Lambert layer mat + grown-canopy Standard mats), `applyLeafGlow` flips emissive 0x2f5d3a @0.55 on the `isNightNow()` edge every 120 frames (tod-override aware). (#6) island mixed architecture: ~35% of accretion columns assemble as crooked huts (9-block stories: 2x2 floor, 2x2 walls, offset cap; hashed yaw) — block accounting stays 1:1 with the ledger. (#5) island far visibility: faint additive violet beacon shaft (voxel*70 cylinder, opacity 0.14) rises from the island once it has any blocks. Verification (headless, `?world=w_glb_mr0rdeik_ae66ed&tod=night&void=aggressive`): build clean; ledger balanced drift 0 at load, after liftoff, and after conversion; forecast lever returned a correct tier-2 fuzzed line; full live arc observed — ship inbound→landed, mote spawned, dawn-deadline liftoff stranded a mote 15.9 cells out at (26,-78) (an earlier run confirmed a mote 0.8 cells away was boarded, radius works both ways), `openEyes(0.5)` x2 converted it: `converted-mr4kyzi9-1` spawned at the mote's exact spot, workers 1→2, original worker `dormant` at night while the convert sat `idle` (night shift live), console logged the whole arc. Hut math verified offline (200/200 block parity, 38% hut columns, no NaN). Persistence symmetric: `syncVoidPersist.stranded {vx,vy,vz,progress,stolen}` ↔ init restore (clamped, eyes reattached at saved opacity). New debug levers: `raidForecast()`, `voidPhase(p)` (runtime dawn/dusk override), `strandedReport()`, `say(text)`, `forceLiftoff(strandFar)`, `openEyes(n)`. NOT headless-verifiable (renderer barely draws this world): leaf-glow look, beacon/hut silhouette, dark hull tint, speech-bubble rendering — KJ device checklist. Source backed up at `.route-backups/tw-staging-app.20260703T065821Z.void-v10-rulings.tsx`.

Jul 3 GLB upload merge — ICP mesh registration + non-destructive block union (VALIDATED, LIVE ON STAGING): KJ ruled "mesh-level registration (ICP align new mesh to old mesh/blocks) then the same block union", replacing the joint-COLMAP merge path for direct GLB uploads (that path never passed its 0.75 registration guard; it remains for video captures). One Scaniverse GLB per scan is all that's needed — the existing world IS the merge target; PLY/LAS point-cloud exports add nothing (the tool samples its own cloud off the mesh). Mechanism (`tools/merge_glb_into_world.py`, runs under `.venv-oldpath` which now has open3d 0.19): (1) old world's structural block centers x meta.voxel form the target cloud in grid-metric frame; (2) new GLB surface-sampled 120k pts; (3) registration = 24-step yaw sweep (4-DOF init: horizontal centroids + 5th-pct floor levels) + FPFH/RANSAC candidate, each ICP-refined, best fitness wins, fine pass at 1.25 voxel; (4) GATE: fitness ≥0.25 AND rmse ≤0.8 voxel, else `declined_registration` exit 0 DB-untouched; (5) aligned mesh voxelized on the OLD grid (no rotate_to_cardinal/rebase — alignment already placed it), floor blends via the old world's rolling-hills seed from meta; (6) UNION: old layers verbatim (0 loss, verified programmatically), new blocks only in unoccupied cells, floor+vegetation only outside the old structural footprint, and "double-skin" suppression drops new structural blocks within 2 cells of old mass inside the footprint (quantization jitter otherwise re-states walls: 9k dropped in self-merge test); (7) write via `update_world_from_payload` (auto-backup) or `--dry-write`. Controls: self-merge of w_glb_mr0rdeik's own GLB → fitness 0.82/rmse 0.59vox, +1,475 blocks all genuinely-new coverage, render visually identical; wrong-room GLB vs Greene St → fitness 0.056 DECLINED (13x separation). Big-world (467k) attempts take ~10 min; small worlds ~25 s. Plumbing: new store-registered route `/api/tinyworld-upload-glb` — POST raw GLB body `?worldId=&mergeCandidate=` persists mesh to `data/uploads/<worldId>.glb` + `glb_uploads` table and spawns the merge when GPS nominated a candidate (30-min SIGKILL cap); GET `?worldId=` returns status/decision for polling. Staging upload flow now: build+save new world (unchanged, reversible) → POST GLB server-side → note shows "checking geometry vs X — merges only if ICP confirms shared space" → 8s poll updates to merged/declined/error. On success the standalone upload world record is left in place (nothing auto-deleted); KJ decides. E2E verified on scratch worlds via curl (merged +1,369 into a copy-target, backup written, DB updated, scratch cleaned). Caveats: assumes gravity-aligned Y-up GLB (Scaniverse ok); .gltf with external buffers will fail trimesh load; interior novel objects <2 cells from old mass inside the footprint get suppressed (extension coverage is the design target).

Jul 5 Void warship — void ship body is now KJ's Warship model in void voxels: `makeVoidShipBody` (tw-staging-app.tsx) renders `src/tw-warship-vox.ts`, a GENERATED module holding a 1,135-block surface shell packed as base64 (L38xH10xW17 block grid, `WARSHIP_DIMS`/`warshipBlocks()`), built from KJ's MagicaVoxel `Warship.vox` (34x75x20, 16.6k voxels; upload `Warship-929621ffde02.rar`, extracted via node-unrar-js WASM — p7zip in this env can't do RAR5). Pipeline (regenerate with `bun tools/warship/voxgen.ts` — point it at `tools/warship/Warship.vox`): parse vox → classify palette into 5 classes (0 hull-light, 1 hull-dark, 2 engine glow blue→0xa855ff, 3 running light red→0x8a2be2, 4 white light→0xe9d5ff) → 2x downsample (accents win over hull majority, <2-of-8 filled corners dropped) → keep only 6-neighbor-exposed shell → orient to ship frame (voxY→X length with PROW at +X since engines sat at low voxY, voxZ→Y up, voxX→Z beam). In-app: 5 InstancedMeshes over one shared BoxGeometry, block size `bs = 8*voxel/38` (~8 world voxels stem to stern), hull mats Phong 0x1a0f2e/0x0d0617, accents MeshBasicMaterial (self-lit at night). Gameplay contract UNTOUCHED: group origin = hover point, heading rotation prow +X, keel gem (now under the hull at `-(cy*bs)+lift-0.7*voxel`), 30-voxel beacon, and the 12 `group.userData.holdCubes` now sit on the midship deck (deckTop = max block y within the middle 40% of length). Headless-verified in debug world via `__tw` levers (spawnVoidShip said "already inbound"; derived world coords via `teleport(0,0)`/`teleport(100,0)` to recover cxRound/voxel, then `camGoto`): hull + purple accents + keel + beacon all visible, shot sent to KJ's Telegram. Device look pending KJ.

Jul 5 Day/night cycle + offline worker progression (SHIPPED to staging, headless-verified on debug world): two fixes KJ asked for ("workers should make real progress while you're gone — building, eating, continuing goals"; "day/night cycle not fully working"). All in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. **(A) Day/night.** The TOD palette+sun run off an ACCELERATED virtual epoch: `worldNow()` (line ~1348) = `_clockBaseVirtual + (realElapsed)*_timeScale`, `_timeScale=90` so a full day passes in `DAY_MINUTES=16` real minutes; clock persists across reloads (`meta.clock`), `_reanchorClock(virtualMs)` moves it without a jump. Four bugs fixed: (1) ROOT CAUSE frozen clock — `_solarTimes` used a CONTINUOUS day count (`n=(t-J2K)/86400000`) so solarNoon slid 1:1 with `t` and `_solarVirtualHour` returned a constant hour forever; fixed to an integer-day snap `const Jstar = Math.round((t-J2K)/86400000 - lon/360)` (line ~1231). (2) midnight boundary could yield a negative virtualHour → `KEYFRAMES[-1]`; `_solarVirtualHour` now assigns `let v` in each branch and returns `((v%24)+24)%24`, and `paletteAt` normalizes its input `((hour24%24)+24)%24`. (3) `__tw.setTime(hour)` rewritten as a 44-iter bisection over `worldNow()..+24h` so it lands accurately on the requested virtual hour. (4) THE VISUAL FIX — the live render loop applied the palette behind a `frameCount % 60` gate, which starves when RAF is slow; replaced with a WALL-CLOCK gate `if (!_todOv && performance.now() - _lastTodApplyMs >= 1000)` (line ~12507, `_lastTodApplyMs` declared at ~12488) that re-derives `paletteAt(_solarVirtualHour(worldNow(),lat,lon))` and applies scene.background/sun.intensity+color/amb/hemi/rim/sunDir once per real second regardless of framerate. Verified via new `__tw.skyState()` lever (palette-wants vs actually-applied): noon sceneBg #87b6e6≈paletteBg #87b5e5 (sunI 2.99), midnight sceneBg #101424≈paletteBg #111525 (sunI 1.22); clock advances ~0.26h/4s; `setTime(23)` no longer negative, no crash advancing 00:29→00:45. **(B) Offline worker catch-up.** Closed-form GOAP progression applied at load so returning feels like time passed. Refactored the inline block into `const runWorkerCatchup = (elapsedMs: number)` (line ~8004; guarded to `workers.length>0 && elapsedMs>2min`), invoked from `meta.savedAt` elapsed (with `?catchup=<hours>` override). Body: `deepestBuildStock` (excludes slug_/fruit/nectar/seed) → Phase 1 building (homeless worker → hut sets `w.home`, then homestead expansions: cap/wall/tower/ring) via `placeStructureNow` = `spawnBlockInto("worker-build")` + `syncGroundAfterPlace` + `ledgerMove("stockpile","void",…)`; Phase 2 meals round-robin (nectar→`gpAward 8`, fruit→`gpAward 2`), energy restore, tier-up recap, `setLedger`. **Every move goes through the conservation ledger** (`WORLD+STOCKPILE+BUILT+VOID===baseline`). Verified byte-exact: build path (huts:1, blocksBuilt:29, stockpile 80→51, world +29, sum===baseline 70696, balanced, drift 0) and meal path (fruit 10→0, meals:10, balanced, drift 0). New keeper lever `__tw.runCatchup(hours=8)` re-runs it live. **Device-check checklist for KJ (headless RAF is throttled ~1.5fps → cannot confirm smooth animation or heavy worlds):** (1) leave staging open ~1 min, watch the sky visibly cycle dawn→noon→dusk→night (16-min day); (2) `__tw.setTime(21)` should darken to night immediately; (3) load a real (heavy) saved world and confirm the palette still tracks — heavy worlds wedge the headless renderer so this is device-only; (4) close the tab a while, reopen, confirm the return recap shows worker builds/meals and the world gained structures. Deployed bundle `tinyworld-staging-CyiE5yoh.js` (served-verified). Kept debug levers: `skyState()`, `timeInfo()`, `setTime()`, `setDayLength()`, `runCatchup()`, `workerCatchup()`.

Jul 5 Day/night REVERSED to real local time + two-light sun/moon model (SUPERSEDES the accelerated-clock entry above; SHIPPED to staging, code+bundle-verified, device-check pending): KJ corrected the accelerated design — "the day night cycle does not need to be accelerated — needs to follow our real day night (or the user's location's day night); the problem was that there was no night." So the 16-min virtual day is GONE. All in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. **(A) Real-time clock.** `worldNow = () => Date.now()` and `_timeScale = 1` — the sky now tracks the wall clock at the world's anchor location (`anchorRef.current.lat/lon`, per KJ: "solar times are based on the location's longitude, not the user, though they should be the same"). `setDayLength`/`setTime` are now honest no-ops (real time can't be scrubbed); `timeInfo` reports real local time. Offline worker catch-up (entry above) preserved untouched. **(B) Two `_solarTimes` phase/boundary fixes** (the original "no night" was accelerated-clock-masked; under real time the palette must line up with the real sun). (1) TRANSIT PHASE: added `- lon/360` to the transit term — `const Jt = Jstar + 0.0053*sin(M) - 0.0069*sin(2*lam) - lon/360` (line ~1241). Before, longitude only picked the DAY not the time-of-day, so solar noon sat at ~12:00 UTC for every world (NYC noon rendered ~08:04, Tokyo ~21:04); after, NYC noon→13:00 EDT, Tokyo→11:45 JST (matches real). (2) DAY-BOUNDARY SIGN: flipped the Jstar day-select to standard NOAA `const Jstar = Math.round((t-J2K)/86400000 + lon/360)` (line ~1231). With the old `- lon/360`, the once-per-day integer-day rounding step landed mid-afternoon → the sky SNAPPED ~2.70h at NYC ~15:10 / ~5.83h at Tokyo ~18:20 every day; `+lon/360` moves that discontinuity to LOCAL solar midnight where it's invisible (max non-wrap jump → ~0.2h). `_solarPosition` (sun direction/shadows) was already real-LMST-correct and untouched; these fixes only realign the palette path (`_solarTimes`/`_solarVirtualHour`) with it. **(C) Two-light sun+moon model** (KJ: "the light source rotates across the sky but never below the horizon — two light sources, one for day the sun, one for night the moon; the materials all react to the different light sources"; KJ chose option A = real moon shadows over shadowless). New `_moonDirection(t,lat,lon)` (after `_solarDirection`, ~line 1311) = anti-solar arc (`az+π`, altitude `= max(-sunAlt, 8°)` so the moon rides opposite the sun and never dips below ~8°). New `moon` DirectionalLight (after `scene.add(sun.target)`, ~line 1550): cool silver `MOON_COLOR=0xbcccff`, `MOON_PEAK=1.35`, own 3072² shadow map + follow-box (mirrors the sun's shadow-camera-follow from the Jun 28 entry, keyed on `moonDir`). Crossfade in the wall-clock TOD loop (~line 12557): `daylight = _smoothstep(-0.10, 0.10, sunAlt)`, `sun.intensity = liveTp.sunI * daylight`, `moon.intensity = MOON_PEAK * (1-daylight)`. **VRAM-safety property (why option A is iOS-safe):** exactly ONE directional light casts shadows at any instant — the shadow flag is handed off (`sun.castShadow = !glowOn; moon.castShadow = glowOn`) at the day/night edge inside the existing leaf-glow flip (`frameCount % 120 === 1`, ~line 12587), so the shader's active-shadow count never changes → no runtime recompile hitch, and only sun(7168²) OR moon(3072²) is resident, staying under the iOS GPU baseline. New/updated levers: `skyState()` gains `sunCastsShadow/moonI/moonPeak/moonCastsShadow/keyLight`; new `setMoon({intensity,color})`. `?tod=day|night|dawn|dusk` still force-pins TOD (live loop gated on `!_todOv`) and now also seeds the initial sun/moon crossfade + shadow-caster. Deployed bundle `tinyworld-staging-B2bJBkGR.js` (served-verified to contain the real-time clock, `moonCastsShadow`/`setMoon`, and the `+lon/360` boundary sign). **Device-check for KJ (headless can't judge shadow VRAM or crossfade smoothness):** open `?tod=day` (warm sun key + warm shadows), `?tod=night` (cool moon key + cool moon-shadows, all materials lit by the silver moon), `?tod=dawn`/`?tod=dusk` (mid-crossfade) — watch the dawn/dusk shadow HANDOFF for any pop, confirm the live sky tracks real local time, and confirm no iOS "a problem repeatedly occurred" crash from the added 3072² moon map. Tune with `__tw.setMoon({intensity})`.

Jul 5 Sentinel + worker position persistence (autosave) (SHIPPED to staging, code+bundle-verified, device-check pending): KJ's two pillars — "1) sentinel state — where it was left should always stay the same upon user[-]level world; 2) same is true of the worker (though the worker moves offline by itself / simulated movement / simulated working) — should not load up into the same location every time." All in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. **ROOT CAUSE:** there was no autosave. `saveCurrentWorld` (the only primitive that writes the snapshot carrying `meta.sentinelAnchor` + `playerState.workers`) fired ONLY on manual Save / Save As. The 15s interval and `pagehide` handlers only flushed `block_events` (an append-only audit log that is NEVER replayed on load). So walking away without pressing Save discarded all positional progress → reload restored the last manual save (mech + workers back at their old spot / the spawn centroid). This single gap explained BOTH pillars. **(A) Silent dirty-gated autosave** (the effect formerly at ~line 652, now expanded). A full snapshot is written when a cheap state *signature* changes — `round(bodyPos xyz) + sentinel yaw + satiation + ledger(void/built/stockpile) + blockCount + every worker's rounded vx.vz`. Interval writes throttled to ≥45s; **backgrounding forces an immediate write** via `pagehide` AND `visibilitychange→hidden` (the realistic "user leaves" trigger on mobile PWAs, and unlike pagehide it can still run async). Guards: only when a world is loaded (`savedWorldRef.current.id` + `worldDataRef.current`), only in `phase==="ready"` (new `phaseRef` mirror — never save mid-build/scan), and a `autoSaveBusyRef` mutex so saves never overlap. Idle worlds (nothing changed) never write. `saveCurrentWorld` gained an `opts.silent` flag that suppresses the "saved N blocks" `loadNote` for autosaves. To avoid a temporal-dead-zone reference (the effect is set up above `saveCurrentWorld`'s declaration), the effect calls through a late-bound `saveCurrentWorldRef` assigned right after the `useCallback`. **(B) Worker position persist+restore** (the mech's `sentinelAnchor` restore at ~line 8035 already worked once *saved*; workers needed three fixes). (1) SAVE: the per-worker record in `saveCurrentWorld` (~line 1044) now also stores `vx/vy/vz` (rounded) — the worker's last logical cell. (2) RESTORE: `createWorker`'s `if (saved)` block (~line 6133) now overrides the centroid spawn with `saved.vx/vz` (and `vy` if present) and sets `targetV*` to match (no phantom beeline); before this every saved worker respawned stacked on the spawn tile `best`. (3) OFFLINE MOVEMENT: `runWorkerCatchup` (~line 8262) ends with a *simulated relocation* — a closed-form catch-up at LOAD (like the void/meal/build steps, so one-step placement is legal; it does NOT violate the live one-cell/no-teleport rule): settled motes drift to their homestead (`w.home.vx/vz ± id-hash jitter`), the still-homeless fan out deterministically by id-hash onto a 2–4 cell ring, then `colSurfaceNear` reseats `vy` so nobody floats/sinks. Catch-up only runs when `elapsedMs > 2min`, so quick reloads restore exact saved cells with no drift. Net: leave → come back later and the mech is exactly where you parked it, and the colony has visibly moved/worked rather than reloading in a stack. Deployed bundle `tinyworld-staging-Cj_sycb7.js` (served-verified: `/tinyworld-staging` → `index-BDYbIgzc.js` → `tinyworld-staging-Cj_sycb7.js`, contains the autosave/`silent` path). **Device-check for KJ (headless can't drive the mech or age the colony reliably):** (1) load a world, drive the Sentinel to a new spot, wait ~45s (or just switch tabs / background the PWA), reload → mech should be exactly where you left it, NOT reset; (2) with workers present, note their positions, background+reload within 2 min → they should reload where they were (no stacking on spawn); (3) leave >2 min (or `__tw.runCatchup(hours)`) then reload → workers should have relocated (homesteaders near their huts, others dispersed) and be sitting on the surface, not floating. No new debug levers; autosave is silent by design (watch the network tab for periodic `saveBlocks` POSTs to confirm it's firing).

Jul 5 Build-mode highlight follows a camera cursor (SHIPPED to staging, code+bundle-verified, device-check pending): KJ — "in the building mode, highlighting the blocks should follow the camera's angle and camera cursor. Highlighting currently works but doesn't follow the cursor — I think it's because of the changing camera positions." All in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. **ROOT CAUSE (not a lag bug):** the build hover (`buildHoverMesh`/`updateBuildHover`) only ever aimed at `buildHoverScreen`, which is set exclusively by a mouse `pointermove` (`if (e.pointerType !== "mouse") return`, ~line 12193). On touch — KJ's device — it stayed `null`, so `updateBuildHover` early-returned and hid the highlight entirely; there was no camera-locked cursor, so nothing tracked the camera as it orbited/soft-followed. (Verified the desktop path was NOT lagging: in build mode the orbit `camera` is not moved between the hover compute in `tickBuildMode`→`updateBuildHover` (~line 12535, called after the soft-follow at ~12497) and the render (~13474) — the walk/gravity/WASD block (~12853+) is all guarded by `walkingRef`/`isDrone`/`inLen>0`, so on desktop the mouse hover was already glued to the cursor pixel.) **FIX — a "camera cursor" (screen-centre reticle) the highlight tracks every frame.** (1) New `buildCursorScreen()` (~line 12129): returns the live mouse `buildHoverScreen` if present (desktop), else the canvas centre in client coords. `updateBuildHover` now aims through it and no longer bails when `buildHoverScreen` is null — so on touch the highlight casts from screen-centre through the LIVE camera each build tick and follows whatever the camera points at (orbit to aim; the soft-follow keeps the mech ≈centred so the default target sits near the Sentinel's feet). (2) Placement consistency: the build-tap `pointerup` now routes TOUCH taps through `buildCursorScreen()` (centre) instead of the finger's `clientX/clientY` (mouse taps still use the pointer) — so on touch "what the reticle highlights is exactly what a tap places/marks/erases." Mouse/desktop behaviour is unchanged (hover + click both at the pointer). (3) UI: a small centre reticle (ring + dot, `pointer-events-none`) is drawn only in `phase==="ready" && buildMode`, and the top hint copy changed to "aim · tap to plan / mark / clear …". Deployed bundle `tinyworld-staging-DBK_Pj3N.js` (served-verified: `/tinyworld-staging` → `index-Be_NFbVO.js` → `tinyworld-staging-DBK_Pj3N.js`, contains the "aim · tap to …" strings). **Device-check for KJ (headless can't drive touch taps or orbit reliably):** enter build mode on the phone → a centre reticle should appear with a live block highlight under it; ORBIT the camera (one-finger drag) and confirm the highlight tracks the reticle onto whatever the camera points at (place=green/amber, pick=red/amber, erase=white); tap (short, low-travel) to plan/mark/erase AT the reticle and confirm the Sentinel acts on that exact block; on desktop confirm the mouse still drives the highlight and click places at the pointer.

Jul 5 Deterministic homeless-worker hut building (SHIPPED to staging, part of the energy/drive wiring): KJ — "let's give the worker a clear goal — should at minimum be building dirt or grass huts (that it can house itself in)." **Symptom:** after the always-persistent worker landed (persistence entry above), the ledger showed motes gathering but never BUILDING — a homeless worker with a full stockpile kept re-planning "gather dirt" forever. **ROOT CAUSE:** the shelter goal was routed through the LLM goal-setter, which dithered — it returned "gather dirt / gather grass" every plan even at full inventory instead of committing to place a hut, so `w.home` never got set and the build blocks never left the stockpile. **FIX (`src/tw-staging-app.tsx`, `makeMotivationPlan`):** homeless workers (`!w.home`) now bypass the LLM entirely and run a deterministic shelter drive — gather dirt/grass until stockpile ≥ 24 build blocks, then place a hut (the existing `placeStructureNow`/hut assembly) and set `w.home`. Survival is non-negotiable so this drive stays UNGATED by work-ethic/energy (unlike the growth/homestead drives, which the energy pass DID gate). This is what makes the conservation ledger actually show `stockpile→built` moves for a returning colony (the catch-up path at load already did this offline; this closes the LIVE-tick gap so an online homeless worker also commits to a hut). Verified conceptually against the ledger contract (every place is a `ledgerMove` world/stockpile→built, drift 0); the *feel* of a worker deciding to build and housing itself is a KJ device check (233K worlds wedge headless).

Jul 5 Reflection → belief → cultural transmission (DESIGN LOGGED, NOT built): KJ — "so we can add a reflection loop — [worker] start[s to] write its own instructions — share them?" and "exactly, writing rules here is reflection on how to better achieve one's goals, and elder motes have the ability [to] influence the instructions of other motes." Locked the agreed spec into `/home/workspace/TINYWORLD_ENERGY_DRIVE_DESIGN.md` (new "Next arc" section) for a FUTURE implementation pass — **no code was written this pass**, this is design only. The arc, in three stages on top of the now-implemented energy/drive/personality layer: **(1) Farming gradient** — forage (instant, low yield) vs farm (delayed, higher yield) become competing self-scored actions in `makeMotivationPlan` so the worker actually has a choice to measure; where-to-plant is the one genuinely contextual call (natural home for a mid-tier LLM later). **(2) Reflection loop** — each worker keeps a running `fruit-gained ÷ energy-spent` tally per strategy and, at rare growth-tier checkpoints, compares its own tallies and reweights its OWN drive priors toward what it measured as more efficient for it, in its spot. This is genuine deterministic discovery (the division IS the insight — no LLM needed to reach the verdict). **Belief records are DATA, never code** — `{strategy, efficiency, samples, tier}` structs only; the worker never writes executable logic or mints new tools/actions; **self-rewriting agents (AgentEvolver-style) explicitly banned** — the action set is fixed, only the weights over it are learned. **(3) Cultural transmission** — a social drive lets workers exchange belief records on contact; the receiver runs a deterministic **reviewer gate** (is this strategy applicable to MY environment/personality?) before adopting. **Elder motes** (high samples/tier) carry records with higher Bayesian weight, with three non-negotiable guardrails: (a) weight, don't override — elder prestige raises weight but still passes the reviewer gate; (b) don't ossify — a younger worker's fresh measurement can out-vote stale elder tradition, the ledger is the final arbiter of truth, not seniority; (c) protect diversity — the personality-filtered gate prevents a single elder strategy collapsing the population into monoculture. **Model tiering (PIANO-style, cost-shaped):** reflexes deterministic/frame; goal selection tiny(Haiku)/none; contextual placement mid(Sonnet); reflective + elder discovery large(Opus) at rare checkpoints only. Rationale anchored in 2025–26 SOTA (Project Sid/PIANO → Fundamental Research Labs concluded full autonomy is an anti-product; La Serenissima's economic constraints → 90.9% identity consistency; HeRoN's RL+LLM-reviewer +81%). See the design doc's "Build order when this arc is picked up" for the implementation sequence.

Jul 5 Worker naming + per-worker map layer v1 (SHIPPED to staging, code+bundle-verified, device-check pending): KJ — "add a feature that allows the user to name the workers" and asked whether workers should "build their own version of the map." Both specced into `/home/workspace/TINYWORLD_ENERGY_DRIVE_DESIGN.md` (new "Spatial memory — per-worker maps" section + updated 6-step build order + "Naming workers" near-term section) and both implemented in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. This is the SPATIAL SUBSTRATE for the reflection/belief arc above — buildable now, independent of the not-yet-built farming/reflection loop. **(A) Worker naming.** New optional `WorkerState.name?: string`. `defaultWorkerName(id)` (after `mbtiFromTraits`, ~line 5308) derives a whimsical two-syllable name deterministically from the worker id (FNV-1a + xorshift hash → `NAME_SYL_A[h%20] + NAME_SYL_B[(h>>>8)%20]`, e.g. "Moko", "Pichi") so no worker ever shows as a bare id — blank names read as a database, defaults read as a cast. `createWorker` sets `name: defaultWorkerName(id)` on the literal (~6373) and, in the `if (saved)` restore block (~6392), overrides with the saved trimmed name (`slice(0,24)`). SAVE: `saveCurrentWorld`'s per-worker record (~1088) stores `name: w.name || undefined`. AUTOSAVE: the dirty-gated signature (~685) now folds in `w.name` so a rename triggers a persist. UI: a rename button (✎ glyph, amber) sits next to the Compass "Call worker" button (~14638) → `window.prompt("Name this worker", cur)` → `__tw.nameWorker(nn)`; the renamed worker speaks its new name via `moteSay(w, "I'm ${nn}!", 4000)`. Existing HUD already read `w0.name || "Worker"` and rendered `workerUi.name`, so the name shows in the worker label immediately. **(B) Per-worker map layer v1 (coarse chunk semantic overlay — NOT a second voxel grid).** New `WorkerState.map?: Map<string, MapCell>` where `MapCell = {s,f,g,d}` (seen-count + fruit/grass/dirt sighting tallies). `MAP_CHUNK=8` voxels/chunk (reuses grass-chunk-style bucketing), `MAP_MAX_CHUNKS=96` (a per-voxel map at N workers is memory we don't want; a per-chunk overlay is cheap). Helpers after `mbtiFromTraits`: `mapChunkKey(vx,vz)`, `mapCellScore`, `pruneWorkerMap` (drops lowest-scored chunks past the cap), `recordDiscovery(w,vx,vz,kind)` where kind ∈ visit|fruit|grass|dirt, `serializeWorkerMap`/`deserializeWorkerMap` (top-96 chunks → `{key:[s,f,g,d]}` and back). **Discovery WRITES (not omniscient world state):** the A* movement hook (~7176) stamps `recordDiscovery(w, vx, vz, "visit")` as the worker steps, and the harvest hook (~7225) stamps the layer it just picked (`fruit`/`grass`/`dirt`). So two workers end up with DIFFERENT maps because they went different places — divergent maps = personality (a cautious marathoner maps a small area deeply; a sprinter ranges wide and sparse). Stale-is-a-feature by design: a worker's map can be wrong once the world changes; fresh measurement corrects it later (the "don't ossify" guardrail in spatial form). PERSIST: `saveCurrentWorld` stores `map: serializeWorkerMap(w.map)`; `createWorker` restore rebuilds via `deserializeWorkerMap(saved.map)`. This is the `where` substrate future belief records anchor to. **Deferred (flagged to KJ):** worker tap-to-select so ANY worker can be renamed (v1 renames `workers[0]`, the focused worker); persistent floating nameplates; threat/void-raid direction memory in the map (fields reserved, not yet written). New `__tw` levers: `nameWorker(name,id?)`, `workerName(id?)`, `nameList()`, `workerMap(id?)` (top-12 chunks), `mapReport()`. Deployed bundle `tinyworld-staging-V2b2IdQ4.js` (served-verified: `/tinyworld-staging` → `index-CTA8XVAD.js` → `tinyworld-staging-V2b2IdQ4.js`, contains "Name this worker" + the name/map levers). **Device-check for KJ (headless can't drive rename taps or age a colony's exploration):** (1) open a world, tap the ✎ button by the compass → prompt appears prefilled with the current (default) name → rename → the worker's label updates and it says "I'm <name>!"; (2) reload → the custom name persists; (3) let workers wander/harvest a while then `__tw.mapReport()` / `__tw.workerMap()` in the console → each worker should have a non-empty, DIFFERENT chunk map that grows as they explore; reload → maps persist.

Jul 6 Autosave was DOA — `serializeWorkerMap is not defined` fix (SHIPPED to staging, code+bundle-verified, device-check pending): KJ — "bug - upon reloading world - the sentinel and drone are not loaded into old position - is something in auto saving not working?" **This is the reason the Jul 5 "Sentinel + worker position persistence (autosave)" entry never actually worked on device.** All in `src/tw-staging-app.tsx`. **ROOT CAUSE:** the Jul 5 worker-map work (entry above) added `map: serializeWorkerMap(w.map, mapMemCap(w.tier))` into `saveCurrentWorld`'s per-worker record (component scope, ~line 1129), but `serializeWorkerMap` — along with `MapCell`/`mapCellScore`/`deserializeWorkerMap` — was defined INSIDE the scene `useEffect`, unreachable from the component-scope save path. So EVERY call to `saveCurrentWorld` (manual Save AND the new silent autosave) threw `ReferenceError: serializeWorkerMap is not defined` before it built `meta`. The autosave's `try/catch { /* transient */ }` swallowed the throw silently, so it looked like it was firing but NOTHING enriched ever persisted — `meta.sentinelAnchor`, worker `vx/vz`, clock, ledger were never written.
