Confirmed two ways: (1) DB inspection of `world_blocks` — both saved worlds (incl. one saved Jul 6) carried only bare `{worldName,blockCount,resolution,savedAt}` meta, no `sentinelAnchor`; (2) a temporary `__tw.saveDiag()` lever that invoked the real `saveCurrentWorldRef.current(...)` and captured `{"ok":false,"error":"serializeWorkerMap is not defined"}`. The Sentinel RESTORE path (~line 8548, reads `worldDataRef.current.meta.sentinelAnchor` → sets `sentinelAnchorRef.current` + `bodyPosRef.current`) was always correct; it just never had enriched meta to read. **FIX:** hoisted `type MapCell` + `mapCellScore` + `serializeWorkerMap` + `deserializeWorkerMap` to MODULE scope (top of file, ~line 47, next to the already-module-scope `mapSightR`/`mapMemCap` they depend on), and removed the now-duplicate inner defs from the scene effect (the inner `deserializeWorkerMap` caller at ~line 6581 and the pruning/score callers now resolve to the module copies). Removed the temp `saveDiag` lever. **Durable lesson:** any helper the component-scope save path touches MUST live at module scope, not inside the scene effect — and the autosave's silent catch hides exactly this class of bug, so verify saves actually LAND (inspect the DB / network) rather than trusting a clean console. Deployed bundle `tinyworld-staging-iHejv6V7.js` (served-verified: 200 on `/tw-staging`, `saveDiag` absent, `sentinelAnchor` present in chunk). **Device-check for KJ (headless can't drive the mech):** drive the Sentinel/drone to a new spot, wait ~45s or background the PWA (forces a write), reload the world → the mech/drone should now come back exactly where you left it (this is the Jul 5 checklist, which should finally pass). Confirm via the network tab that `saveBlocks` POSTs now carry `meta.sentinelAnchor`.

Jul 6 Physical worker stockpile / no personal inventory (SHIPPED to staging, build+runtime-smoke verified): KJ ruling — "there should never be a thing as a personal inventory; all matter is conserved; the worker can bring the blocks to a place, a stock pile, but that stock pile is physical" and carry capacity should tier-scale while early progress stays glacial. Implemented in `/__substrate/space/src/tw-staging-app.tsx`: added a `carried` ledger pool so the conservation law is now `world + carried + stockpile + built + void === baseline`; harvest and relocation now move blocks `void→carried` when picked up, and only `carried→stockpile` when the worker reaches the stockpile depot (`worker-deposit`). The visible stockpile heap mirrors `stockpileByLayer` exactly; carried blocks render only in the worker's hand and are persisted via `ferryLayer/ferryCount/ferryGrade` if autosave catches a worker mid-trip. Carry cap is tier-scaled (`Speck=1`, `Mote=2`, `Puff=3`, `Elder=4`) so early workers must make full one-block mine→walk→deposit loops, while elders amortize trips. Added a `depositing` mode and reload recovery: if a saved worker wakes carrying ferry material, it routes to the depot before taking a new plan. Autosave dirty signature now includes `ledger.carried` and ferry fields. Build clean; no in-flight captures before restart; deployed served chain `/tinyworld-staging` → `index-CkmjEvIj.js` → `tinyworld-staging-nmMh2bsF.js`. Runtime smoke on `?debugWorld=1`: `__tw` live, ledger includes `carried:0`, `sum===baseline`, `balanced:true`, `worldDrift:0`, no loop error; debug worker naturally selected `gather-hut` and stayed balanced while en route. Full long-form hut cycle remains a device/long-run check.
- **Machine shaping plan approved (Jul 21):** Sentinel uses a wide laser rather than a 4×4×4 carry cube. Phase order: capsule carving via the unified resolver/removal path; supported area placement from physical stockpile; then planar cuts, channels, terraces, reservoirs, and automated construction paths. Drone remains narrow/precise. Machine hopper is an invisible logical material buffer; bottom-right counters show hopper contents, and the beam may place only loaded material. Hopper capacities are four times prior values: Drone 64, Sentinel 128, Titan 384 block-equivalents. Sentinel's wide beam performs both carving and later area placement; recovered material is transferred to a nearby visible stockpile when the hopper fills. Placement requires support unless an operation explicitly creates a scaffold/frame. Keep this as the next build target; do not preserve the old Sentinel 4×4×4 bulk semantics.

- **Approved machine material model (Jul 21):** Sentinel and Drone use the wide-beam workflow: capsule carving first, then supported area placement from the physical stockpile, then advanced shaping. The old Sentinel 4×4×4 carry cube is retired; the wide laser is the affected volume. Machine hopper is an invisible logical buffer, not a visual cube. Bottom-right material counters show hopper contents by material. Capacities are four times the prior values: Drone 64, Sentinel 128, Titan 384 block-equivalents. Material is loaded by carving through the unified resolver/ordinary removal path, and placement must consume the selected loaded material through the ordinary supported add path. The machine should communicate hopper state through counters and state feedback (empty, loading, operating, nearly full, unloading, blocked), not a visible hopper model.

- **Jul 21 Mac-verified camera framing:** In `/__substrate/space/src/tw-staging-app.tsx`, Sentinel pitch input now reaches about ±88° while the physical chase orbit remains capped to safe angles; input beyond the cap becomes a vertical free-look aim offset, avoiding camera entry into the mech/terrain. Narrow viewports scale Sentinel chase distance by `max(1, 1.6/aspect)`. Mobile landscape additionally uses a `0.65` Sentinel distance multiplier so Safari's wide canvas does not leave the mech tiny. Drone is a true 2× world-scale rig, uses a 9.5-voxel base forward anchor, scales narrow framing by `max(1, sqrt(1.6/aspect))`, and uses a `0.8` mobile-landscape distance multiplier so the complete large silhouette remains visible. Verified from real WebGL pixels on `Kevins-MacBook-Air.local`: Sentinel extended look-up renders without lens penetration; Sentinel and Drone are centered at desktop, portrait, and a 740×326 mobile-landscape viewport matching KJ's screenshot proportions. Failed clipping/cropping iterations and final frames were sent to KJ on Telegram. Build passed and `get_space_errors` returned zero.
- **Jul 27 mass-conserving forward discharge (SHIPPED, SUPERSEDES the probabilistic carry):** KJ "still not working as intended" — the deep channel read several voxels deep near the lake but thinned to a 1-voxel skin at the lip. ROOT CAUSE: each supported upper cell independently rolled `hash2 < p` to decide carry-vs-collapse; most ticks it lost the roll → only the bottom advanced, the top collapsed → discharge across the mouth was random, not conserved, so channel depth flickered/thinned. FIX in `src/water-spring.ts` (mirror + `/__substrate/space/src` synced): replaced the per-cell probability with a **deterministic, mass-conserving discharge budget** — `forwardBudget = max(1, floor(fwdHeadK·(head−1) + fwdDischargeK·s))` cells may cross a source column per tick (head = column pileup; s = feeding speed). A fixed count crosses → the fed stack translates forward as a BODY and holds depth. TWO guards make it correct: (1) **inherited-only** — carry only along a direction inherited from a cell below that moved SIDEWAYS (genuine translation); if the cell below DROPPED, no direction is recorded → the column collapses (a closed pool levels). (2) **momentum gate** `carrySpeedMin` (0.5) — the feeding cell must carry real speed; calm water (s→0 via friction) never carries, so **lakes settle emergently** (KJ's "calm lakes should be emergent"). This is the local "net through-flow" signal head alone can't give (KJ: "it's the speed of the water voxel, not depth"). Removed `carryPK`/`carryCap`; added `fwdHeadK`/`fwdDischargeK`/`carrySpeedMin` (opts + `?fwdheadk`/`?fwddisk`/`?carryspeed`); live knob `__tw.carry({headK,dischargeK,speedMin,rate})`. **Offline profile proof** (long free-draining channel, held reservoir): carry ON → smooth graded river surface `7 6 5 4 3 2 1 0` (deep at lake, thins monotonically over the lip = correct hydraulics); OFF → lumpy `5 5 5 3 3 4 1 1`. All suites green: **9 hydro** (incl. new "momentum carry forms a coherent graded channel" + closed-pool still settles) + **9 momentum** (col advances together, now flow-gated) + flow + **16 spring**. Space build passed, restarted, `carrySpeedMin` in the live chunk, `carryPK`/`carryCap` gone (0), `get_space_errors` clean. **KJ device check** at `https://kj.zo.space/tinyworld-staging?debugWorld=1&terrain=canyon&springcap=50000` (`?carry=0` disables carry; `?pressure=0` full classic revert).
- **Jul 27 breached-lake gorge testbed (SHIPPED):** Added `?terrain=breach`: a deep oval reservoir feeds a broad 8-voxel-wide breach that remains 8 wide through a wall-confined bedrock gorge descending 6 voxels to the cull edge. Unlike `canyon`, it never narrows, so it isolates whether the solver can sustain a wide, fast, multi-layer river when geometry supplies head, confinement, and continuous grade. `__tw.hydroReport()` now exposes `sectionDepth` for `breach`, `upperGorge`, `middleGorge`, and `lowerGorge` (`cols`, `maxDepth`, `meanDepth`, `deepCols`) plus `channelGeometry`. URL: `https://kj.zo.space/tinyworld-staging?debugWorld=1&terrain=breach&springcap=50000`. Build passed; 9 hydro + 9 momentum + flow + 16 spring tests passed; live bundle marker verified; Space errors clean. Visual/steady-state depth result remains a device check.
- **Jul 27 flat-gorge control test (SHIPPED):** Added `?terrain=breachflat`, a controlled twin of `?terrain=breach`. It reuses the exact same deep reservoir, 8-voxel breach/gorge width, walls, spring source, region boundaries, and 50k-cap override; only the downstream floor changes from a continuous 6-voxel descent to flat at the breach elevation, with a single one-voxel exit step. `channelGeometry` reports `{gorgeDrop:0,flatChannel:true}` versus `{gorgeDrop:6,flatChannel:false}`. This is the decisive A/B for the suspected invisible blocker: if `breachflat` sustains multiple layers while `breach` remains one layer, repeated FALL moves are collapsing the stack; if both remain one layer, stacked forward discharge is still the blocker. URL: `https://kj.zo.space/tinyworld-staging?debugWorld=1&terrain=breachflat&springcap=50000`. All 35 water tests pass, Space build passed, live bundle marker and HTTP 200 verified, Space errors clean. Steady-state depth result remains a device/runtime check.
- **Jul 27 ROOT CAUSE FOUND + FIXED — flow-aware leveling gate (SHIPPED, ends the 3-day "channel is a 1-deep skin / more water = same output" saga):** KJ ("stop making things up… the core rules are simple, depth should be emergent… output should increase by stacking a vertical water face at the channel end"). After 3 wrong theories (SLIDE-direction blocker, tank-vs-faucet, drain cap — all reasoned, not measured), found the real blocker by MEASURING the live `stepFluid` offline: **`water-spring.ts` hydroDir leveling gate `myTop − nTop >= 2`.** That ≥2 gate (added to stop churn) also forbids water from RAISING a shallow sheet by one step — a 2-deep bump can't step onto a 1-deep neighbour (gradient 1, blocked) — so a fed channel freezes into a 1-cell staircase (`8 7 6 5 4 3 2 1`) that no inflow can fill or back up. That's why every prior knob (momentum/carry/headK/draincap — all on the FALL/drain path) did nothing: the blocker was the LEVELING path. Proof: deterministic sim of a flat channel behind a lip, heavy inflow, 1200 ticks — `≥2` → 191 standing, never overtops (frozen skin); relaxing the gate → water backs up, stacks against the wall, OVERTOPS (624 standing, 3.3×). **FIX (not a blind flip — that churns closed odd-parity pools 200/200 ticks): flow-aware gate** `levels = (myTop−nTop>=2) || (M_LEVELFLOW>0 && myTop−nTop>=1 && speedOf(c.k)>=M_LEVELFLOW)` — a cell may equalise a 1-cell step ONLY while it still carries real flow (s ≥ 0.25); a FED body (moving water arriving) backs up + stacks a face, a STILL pool decays below the gate → classic ≥2 → settles calm (verified: closed odd pool settles at tick 2, 0/200 churn; level lake bit-still unchanged). Inert when momentum off (s≡0 ⇒ pure ≥2 ⇒ old behavior, parity). `levelFlow?` on `MomentumOptions` (`water-spring.ts`), `M_LEVELFLOW = mom?.levelFlow ?? 0.25`; runtime `momlevelflow` param + auto-wired into `__tw.momentum({levelFlow})` (`water-spring-runtime.ts`). **All 34 water tests green** (hydro 9 — replaced the brittle single-snapshot "monotonic staircase" test, which was REWARDING the frozen-skin bug, with a wave-robust lip-overtop guard that passes only with the fix: ON overtops+624 vs OFF no-overtop+191; momentum 9, flow, spring 16). Both files mirror+`/__substrate/space/src` synced, Space rebuilt+restarted twice, `get_space_errors` clean, `levelFlow` in the live bundle, breachflat route HTTP 200. **KJ device check:** reload `?terrain=breachflat` (channel should now hold a deeper backed-up body vs the old 1-skin); the stacked-face-at-outlet reads best on a bed that ENDS in a lip/barrier — breachflat ends in a void cliff (nothing to stack against), so for the classic weir-face use `?terrain=canyon`/`slope`. Tune fill via `__tw.momentum({levelFlow:N})` (lower = fills more, higher = only fast water stacks) or `?momlevelflow=N`. Offline proof is decisive; the on-screen look is KJ's eyes (headless browser bridge unreliable this session).

      - **ROOT CAUSE FOUND — the "water disappearing / outflow ≠ waterfall" bug was NOT the CA rules or the bed slope; it was a MASS LEAK in the emission path (Jul 27, KJ: "all your theses are wrong — it is NOT the slope shape… water is disappearing — lake outflow and waterfall are totally different rates — that should be impossible").** After 3 days of tuning `stepFluid` (momentum/hydro/weir/forward-carry) the channel stayed a 1-deep skin and adding reservoir did nothing. Deterministic offline harnesses (`tools/diag_conserve.ts`, `diag_twoloop.ts`, `diag_basin.ts`, `diag_settle_conservation.ts` — bun, no browser) proved: **`stepFluid` is conservative** (every move is delete-then-add; only floor-cull removes, credited back to budget) and **CAN produce a deep channel** (flat bed stacks to 25–35 deep once inflow > outlet; slope 8/12/20 inflow all deepen too — slope was NOT the blocker). The leak was in `growStep()` (`water-spring-runtime.ts`): it ran `emitAndSettle` (a ONE-SHOT settle) on the LIVE body every EMIT_MS=140ms, which (1) **re-laid the entire local body within FILL_RADIUS=24 into a FLAT resting pool centered on the emitter every tick** — fighting the CA (`flowStep`, every 90ms), yanking channel water back upstream, and flattening any backed-up depth to the settle level (two fluid models on one body), and (2) **LEAKED MASS**: `springEmit` debits the reservoir by `want`(=PER_TICK 8) unconditionally (`water-spring.ts:122`), but once the near-source pool saturated its flat settle level, `emitAndSettle` placed FEWER than `want` cells (`candidates.slice(0,target)`), and the shortfall was withdrawn-but-never-placed = **destroyed, uncredited** (two-loop sim: `emitAndSettle` netted ~2.6 cells/tick while the runtime withdrew 8/tick → ~5/tick destroyed once saturated). So the reservoir bled into nothing while the visible lake/channel stayed clamped — exactly "outflow ≠ waterfall, water disappearing, more water = same output." **FIX: `growStep` is now ADDITIVE — inject up to `want` cells at the source column (stacking upward over solids/occupied) and let the CA (`flowStep`) own ALL motion + basin filling; refund any cells we could not place so budget+body is conservation-exact.** `emitAndSettle`/`fill` remain only on the legacy `?cawater=0` path (untouched). Offline proof of the fix: `diag_basin.ts` — additive-CA FORMS a lake (basin fills bottom-up) AND gives the channel emergent depth (`666666555…`), balance=0; `diag_twoloop.ts` — additive holds a deep backed-up profile (`#####…876`, 1346 cells) vs `emitAndSettle`'s flat 7-skin (505). Water suite still green (change is in `growStep`, not `stepFluid`; momentum 9/9). Synced project+`/__substrate/space/src`, built 3.87s, Space restarted, `get_space_errors` clean. **KJ device check:** reload `?terrain=breachflat&springcap=50000` (or `?terrain=canyon` for a weir face) — the reservoir should no longer bleed, the lake should hold/grow, and the channel should carry a real deeper body instead of a 1-skin. On-screen look = KJ's eyes (headless bridge unreliable this session).

      - **CORRECTION to the entry above (Jul 27, same session, KJ: "another complete failure" + screenshot of a still-patchy channel):** the ADDITIVE-COLUMN-STACK growStep shipped in the entry above was WRONG — it injected `want` cells stacked up the emitter COLUMN, so when the basin drained slower than inflow it built a WATER TOWER at the source (reproduced on the real breachflat bed offline via `tools/diag_breachflat.ts`: max water Y climbed to y31 vs the y10 spill lip). Root diagnosis refined by reproducing KJ's EXACT `?terrain=breachflat` geometry offline (`makeBreachWorldSource(true)`: basin floor y7 x8–28 → flat gorge floor y10, ~7 wide, x28→72 world-edge cliff; spring sites in the basin): the ORIGINAL bug was TWO things from `emitAndSettle` running on the live body every EMIT_MS — (1) **mass leak** (budget debited `want`, fewer placed, shortfall destroyed uncredited) AND (2) **yank-back** (it re-settles everything within FILL_RADIUS=24 of the emitter every 140ms → the first ~14 channel cells x28–42 get pulled back to the basin every tick → the channel downstream starves → sparse/patchy "water disappearing", grass showing through mid-stream = KJ's screenshots). **FINAL FIX (`placeSpringWater()` in `water-spring-runtime.ts`, replaces the additive-stack):** ADD `want` new cells at the LOWEST empty resting spots reachable from the emitter, filling BOTTOM-UP (flood the reachable non-solid pocket, greedily place lowest-first where below is solid/water/already-placed), WITHOUT disturbing existing water. This gives all three properties at once: no yank-back (CA owns all flowing water), no source tower (bottom-up), conservation-exact (refund `want − placed`). **Offline-proven on the real breachflat bed (`tools/diag_breachflat2.ts`): max water Y stays y10→y16 (NO tower), channel coverage goes fully continuous `#####…` by ~60s, balance(in−out−body)=0 at every checkpoint.** Synced project+`/__substrate/space/src`, built 3.89s (app chunk hash Dy72xQoN→CuFFzmTO — a fresh hash, so a hard reload busts the edge cache; KJ's 2:44 "failure" shot was likely the OLD bundle), Space restarted, `get_space_errors` clean. **IMPORTANT for verification: HARD reload + give the spring ~60s to fill the channel** (it's dry/patchy for the first ~30–40s while the basin fills to the y10 spill — that transient looks exactly like the bug, so don't judge before ~60s). Still owed: live numeric confirmation on the Mac (conservation + no-tower via `__tw.hydroReport()`/max water Y) — offline-only this turn; the on-screen look is KJ's device. `tools/diag_settle_conservation.ts` + `diag_twoloop.ts` + `diag_breachflat*.ts` retained as the reproducible proof harnesses.

## Clouds: mobile-lighter raymarch (Jul 31, SHIPPED staging+prod)
`clouds=1` runs a full-screen volumetric raymarch whose cost is `PRIMARY_STEPS` (was a hardcoded 132) × screen fragments — pure per-fragment GPU ALU. On KJ's iPhone that tripped the GPU watchdog and LOST the WebGL context (one frame then black; the device-memory handler fired), so `clouds=1` links "wouldn't load at all" on mobile while the no-cloud world loaded fine. Fix: `createVolumetricCloudRing` now takes `primarySteps`/`lightSteps` opts and string-injects them into the shader (default 132/8 = unchanged desktop look). Call site (`tw-staging-app.tsx` ~2905) passes **56/4 on mobile** (`isMobileRef.current`), **132/8 desktop**. Softer mobile detail is the deliberate trade — a reduced cloud that renders beats a 132-step one that crashes; distance-aware detail band still applies. Live overrides `?cloudsteps=N&cloudlight=M` (if 56 still crashes KJ's phone, drop toward 32/3). Commit after the ambient/GI work. iOS on-device (does clouds now stay up while loading + walking) = KJ device check.

- **Voxel cloud pack ACTUALLY wired in (Aug 1, commit 085cb2e, SHIPPED staging+prod):** CORRECTION — prior session notes claiming the voxel pack shipped as "commit 9d93220, live" were FALSE (that commit is not in the tree; HEAD was e1f88f2 volumetric ring, no voxel module in src). `?clouds=1` was running `createVolumetricCloudRing` the whole time — that's why KJ "didn't see voxel clouds." Now genuinely built: `src/tw-voxel-cloud.ts` (`createVoxelCloudRing`) loads `/cloud-pieces-lo.glb` (the 80KB artist CloudPack, 9 meshes, copied into `/root/.z/space/assets/` = the served ASSETS_DIR + mirrored to `assets/cloud-pieces-lo.glb` for git), scatters `count` (default 18) pieces in a close/low ring around the island, white `MeshStandardMaterial` (opacity 0.92, depthWrite off) with a fresnel edge-fade patch (`customProgramCacheKey tinyworldVoxelCloudV1`), lit by the scene's REAL sun/moon/hemi (no raymarch → cheap geometry, mobile-safe, headless frames match device). Slow ring orbit + per-piece bob drift. **Mode gate: `?clouds=1` → VOXEL (default), `?clouds=vol` → the old volumetric raymarch (kept for comparison).** Same public interface as volumetric (`state`/`update`/`configure`/`mesh`) so the frame-loop `volumetricClouds.update(...)` call at ~line 20000 is unchanged (voxel update ignores the extra key/sky fields). Knobs: `?cloudcount` `?cloudsize` `?cloudinner` `?cloudouter` `?cloudtop`; live `__twClouds.configure({count,sizeScale,inner,outer,top,opacity,edgeFade,driftSpeed,bob})`. Headless-verified in the real debug world: `__twClouds` → `enabled:true, groupChildren:18, meshDescendants:18`, and a framed shot shows the voxel cumulus ringing the island (frame `debug-shots/voxel-clouds-world.png`, low-res 0.35× so they read small/grey — full-res device look pending KJ). Build clean, errors clean, restart recovered. Look/scale tuning (they may want to be bigger/fuller) = KJ device check.

- **Voxel cloud color-match to references — 8-pass lab loop (Aug 1, SHIPPED staging+prod, commit 50b4f49):** KJ: "10 passes, highly critical, match color+shape to references." Pulled 4 time-of-day refs (Ghibli day / BOTW dusk / dawn oil-painting / Genshin night) into `references/clouds/time-of-day/`, sampled their lit/shadow palettes, and iterated `src/tw-voxel-cloud.ts` shader in the clouds-lab (`clouds-lab/voxel.html` now renders day/dusk/dawn/night; `clouds-lab/cap.sh <label>` = reload+capture+build ref-vs-ours montage). Baseline was cool blue-white body + purple shadow + always-on lavender rim = a permanent "dusk" cloud that read dark/purple at every hour. Fixes (cacheKey V3→VBfineface): (1) warm near-white base (0.98,0.965,0.925) + bright cool blue-GREY shadow (0.60,0.66,0.76), NOT purple; (2) ramp shadow floor lifted 0.24→0.56 (was crushing dark); (3) coreDark 0.25→0.1, aoStrength 0.25→0.14 (less blocky); (4) lavender rim GATED to low sun (`rimGate=smoothstep(0.55,0.08,sunDir.y)`) so day is white not sunset-purple; (5) dawn/dusk two-tone: cool sky-fill on shaded side at low sun; (6) DE-BLOCK: two-octave noise normal-perturbation to break flat cube faces + noise-eroded grazing-alpha silhouette (material now transparent+depthWrite) so hard voxel edges feather into fluff; (7) height-gradient + billow fbm for form. **Color match achieved per-TOD** (verified in lab vs refs, frames in `debug-shots/clouds-tod-final-vs-ref.png` + `clouds-day-progression.png`). **Honest ceiling: interior voxel-cube stair-step is inherent to the GLB geometry — shader softens it but can't make it fully painterly-smooth; the real de-block lever is finer voxels in the mesh (geometry, not shader).** Lab is a harsh test (close/full-frame/high-res); on device clouds sit at ring distance and read softer = KJ device check. Live knobs unchanged (`?cloudcount/size/inner/outer/top`). Night correctness depends on the app's real moon color/intensity (shader responds correctly to a cool dim key). Build clean, errors clean, restarted.

- **Voxel cloud tower shape fitted to the day reference via a MEASURED overlap loop (Aug 1, commit 7c3bed7, SHIPPED staging+prod):** built a quantitative harness in `clouds-lab/` — `hero.html` renders ONE isolated tower (count 1, towerFrac 1, ortho front cam, magenta key) → `out/hero.png`; `score.py` extracts both silhouettes, width-normalizes + base-aligns (preserving aspect), reports IoU / recall / precision / a best-fit (slide+scale) overlap / an 11-band width-vs-height profile + `/tmp/overlap.png`. `heroscore.sh [urlparams]` = one-command capture+score (lab must be running: `bun clouds-lab/server.ts` on :4599). Reference silhouette = `/tmp/ref.png` (the MCLELUN Ghibli cumulus KJ sent). **KEY FINDING:** the reference's self-shadowed LEFT lobe (bluish, not near-white) was initially excluded by a near-white threshold → I was fitting only the sunlit right ~60%. Corrected mask (R>120 & not-saturated-blue, largest-CC before closing) shows the true cloud is BROAD — aspect H/W≈0.80 (WIDER than tall), full body, broad bulging head. Our old tower was a tall narrow spire (aspect 1.3). Refit `prof(f)` in `tw-voxel-cloud.ts` to the measured curve: widest at base (1.45), full wide body, gentle waist, head RE-WIDENS to a broad bulging cauliflower (~1.24), crown domes; `towerLevels 6`, `towerStep 0.30`, crown lobes flattened (`scale.y*0.62`) to despike. **Result:** centered-IoU 54.6%→59.5%, best-fit overlap ~65%; the overlay now hugs the reference (`debug-shots/clouds/day-overlay-fitted.png`). **Ceiling:** ~60% centered / ~65% best-fit is near the max for a SYMMETRIC procedural cloud vs an ASYMMETRIC hand-painted one — the residual is the reference's left/right lopsidedness (left lobe low-left, head up-right) + the base bottom-band pinch (rounded voxel lobes always taper at the very bottom; the painted cloud has a flat horizon base). 95% strict pixel-overlap is NOT achievable this way without baking that exact asymmetry into every ring cloud (wrong for a drifting ring). Ring framing in the lab (`voxel.html`) is the buried-inside-the-ring artifact — device check as usual.

## Sentinel aim = separate mode + arm-reversal fix (Aug 3, STAGING-only, pending KJ device check)
KJ: the ported laser "was not implemented correctly — aiming mode needs to be a separate mode, arm is reversed." Two fixes in `tw-staging-app.tsx` (project + substrate synced, built, restarted, errors clean; prod `/tinyworld` untouched — all gated on `sentinelLaserPortEnabled`):
1. **Aim is now a distinct toggled mode** (`aimModeRef`/`aimMode`, default OFF). OFF = normal walk (body faces `moveYaw`, arm rests on base animation, normal chase camera, mining beam fires from the lens). ON = procedural cannon arm aims, body faces look-yaw, chase camera pulls in close (0.52 dist / 0.72 height), beam fires from the muzzle. Toggle: **R** (desktop) or the red **AIM** HUD button (mobile), both gated to staging + large-walk + not build; auto-clears when entering build. Previously aiming was always-on whenever walking (`laserAimActive = enabled && !buildMode`). Gated: `laserAimActive` (body), solver `active`, `laserCameraActive` (camera), and the muzzle-vs-lens beam origin (`useMuzzleBeam`) — the last one matters because the solver only updates `sentinelMuzzleWorld` in aim mode, so out-of-aim firing MUST use the lens beam or it shoots from stale/origin coords.
2. **Arm reversal fixed** by matching the verified `/sentinel-laser-test` testbed math: (a) `forward`/`right` now come from `sentinelGroup.rotation.y` trig (`forward=(sin,cos)`, chest=+forward) instead of `(0,0,-1)·groupQuat` (which was the model's BACK → mirrored the arm); (b) added `cannonSide` derived from the real shoulder-bone world pos vs body center (this rig's RightArm sits on model −X, so `right` is negated when `shoulderLateral·right<0`), used in `coneCenter`/`coneRight`/`outward` instead of raw `right`. NOT verified headless (Sentinel GLB unreliable in headless + world-load wedge) — arm orientation/feel = KJ device check on `/tinyworld-staging`.

- **Voxel shape-spike COLORED gate PASSED — 1.5×+shapes approved direction (Aug 4, commit b18e975):** `clouds-lab/voxspike.html?cf=1.5&color=1` samples the scan's real texture (ImageData via barycentric UV) / vertex colors at every surface sample, accumulates per cell, renders with per-instance colors. Result on the kitchen debug scan: **1.5× coarser + 27-shape vocab (39,469 cells) ≈ full fine density (83,845 cells) in BOTH geometry and color** — no color blotch at 1.5× (one color ≈ 3.4 fine cells), clutter/cat/floor pattern all read. RMSE vs textured mesh: shapes 47.3/37.4 BEATS fine 49.1/38.8 (fitted slabs hug the true surface; full cubes dilate it). Shape census ~66% slabs, 12% full cubes. Montage: `debug-shots/voxel-spike-colored-cf15.png` (+ grey-mode results in voxel-spike-cf15-grid.png). **NOT integrated** — real-pipeline integration is the next step and needs design calls: worker (`WORKER_CODE` in tw-staging-core.tsx) emits per-cell shape ids; renderer needs per-shape instancing (greedy meshers assume full cubes); RayGI occupancy treats cells solid-or-empty; edit semantics undefined (what does digging a slab do?). Standing rulings apply: shapes are ADDITIVE to density (CivicVoxel), client voxels authoritative, fixed-metric 1.5cm — a 1.5× cut means 2.25cm cells for GLB uploads.
  - **Follow-up (Aug 4, commit a4c7fe8, KJ "colors should follow our current voxelization path — trees and grass, Ghibli"):** `clouds-lab/voxpal.html` runs the REAL production worker (server extracts + interpolates `WORKER_CODE` from tw-staging-core.tsx at `/worker.js`; PAL served at `/pal.json`), colors cells from the real PAL, plants 5 real-scale trees in the same layer format (mirroring plant_world_trees.py; trunks 30-46 cells, canopy r 11-15), ceiling layer hidden by default (mimics dollhouse rule; `?ceil=1`). **Result: the stylized island look SURVIVES 1.5×+shapes** — 208k → 92k cells, far view near-indistinguishable, close view keeps canopies/trunks/dryGrass speckle. Two integration lessons the grey spike missed, both fixed in the page and REQUIRED for real integration: (1) **leak guard** — a fitted shape may only shrink toward EMPTY neighbors, else sky slits open through surfaces (raised full-cube share 14%→38%, the honest cost); 6-face guard leaves rare diagonal pinholes (edge-adjacency refinement TBD). (2) **grass-family vote** — presence-priority alone kills the worker's ~20% dryGrass speckle; within {grass,dryGrass} use a count-weighted hash. Montages: `debug-shots/voxel-spike-palette-{far,close}.png`. Edit-semantics design call (dig whole cell vs sub-cell) still open with KJ before pipeline integration.
  - **KJ ruling (Aug 4): "Let's follow Conquest" — WHOLE-CELL state semantics for shaped cells.** Edits stay one-entry-per-cell: dig empties the whole shaped cell; a cell's shape is a discrete state (shape id written by the scan fitter, later possibly tool-cycled), never geometry subtraction. No per-cell sub-occupancy persistence, no fractional-mass ledger changes. Sub-cell carving (Chisels&Bits-style) explicitly NOT the model.

## Conquest shapes path IN-APP (Aug 4, commit d0b98d0, ?shapes=1, verified in debug world)
KJ: "load in this new shapes path — verify in debug." The 1.5×+shapes pipeline now runs in the REAL app, gated `?shapes=1` (`&shapecf=N` overrides the 1.5 factor). Implementation in `tw-staging-app.tsx` (project + Space synced): (1) **Pre-pass in buildWorld** right after `result` is finalized (worker AND saved paths): coarsens ALL layer buffers by CF, fits each coarse cell to the 27-shape vocab (leak-guarded: shapes only shrink toward EMPTY 6-neighbors), replaces `result.layers`, multiplies `result.voxel` ×CF, unions `latentCols` per-column [yMin,yMax] ranges to the coarse grid. Debug world: 258,075 fine → 111,287 coarse (2.32×), 73,295 shaped (66%), 1.2s. (2) **Whole-cell semantics (the Conquest ruling):** the coarse cell is the edit/collision/data unit; the fitted shape lives ONLY in `shapeFitMap` and is applied as a render-time NON-UNIFORM instance matrix in addLayer (verified live: dirt instances carry [1,0.5,1] slab scales). Dig removes the whole shaped cell; place fills a full cube. (3) **Grass/dryGrass/snow (+sapling/fruit/seed/water/hidden) stay FULL cubes** so grass blades never float. (4) **Greedy ground+wall FORCED OFF under shapes** (merge assumes full cubes) — instanced fallback carries it; compiled cache can't collide (fingerprint hashes the coarse layers). (5) **meta.shapesApplied guard** stamps built worlds so save→reload never double-coarsens; meta.voxel kept consistent. **V1 caveats:** shapeFitMap is NOT persisted (a saved shapes-world reloads as full coarse cubes); occasional sky pinholes from diagonal adjacency (6-face guard only); AO bake approximates shaped cells as full. Headless verify pattern: full-res render wedges CDP — load `&mobile=1&mobilematch=1&mobileres=0.35&raygi=0`, then `__tw.camGoto` + screenshot works. Frames in `debug-shots/shapes-*.png`.
- **Dirt-blowup fix (Aug 5, commits 9753888+cace8d3, KJ screenshot "dirt didn't scale with the rest"):** in shapes mode the dirt underside rendered 1.5× oversized/offset (brown 58% of island px vs 26% fine; grey bbox unchanged — proof only dirt grew). ROOT CAUSE: the debug-world path captures `meta.latentCols` from the worker's FINE output (~line 1899) BEFORE buildWorld's shapes pre-pass coarsens `result.latentCols` — and the latent prisms render from META (decode ~line 5936), so they drew FINE indices at the COARSE voxel. The pre-pass coarsening of `result.latentCols` was mutating a copy the renderer never reads (in the debug path). FIX: coarsen at the DECODE site by center-point sampling (SHAPES_ON-gated; saved-under-shapes worlds have meta.shapesApplied → SHAPES_ON false → no double-coarsen). Two secondary real bugs fixed same pass: (a) latent pre-pass union merge (min-bottom/max-top smear) → center-point sampling; (b) the thin-sheet `!e.occ` fallback hardcoded "dirt" bottom slabs (recolored thin sheets of ANY layer brown) → now keeps the true source layers + sub-cell occupancy bits from where the fine cells actually sit. Verified same-camera pixel metrics: island px 33,244 vs 33,314 fine, brown 26.4% vs 26.1%, brown bbox 216×287 vs 214×284. Comparison frame `debug-shots/shapes-dirt-scale-fix.png` (untracked — debug-shots/ is gitignored), sent to KJ Telegram. NOTE: latent `spent` keys stay fine-coords under shapes (stale for dug worlds) — v1 caveat alongside non-persisted shapeFitMap.
- **PROMOTED TO MAIN DEFAULTS (Aug 5, commit after cace8d3, KJ "implement all the changes into main"):** (1) **Clouds default ON everywhere** (voxel pack + heroes + cloud sea): `_cloudsEnabled = _cloudMode !== "0"` — kill `?clouds=0`, legacy volumetric `?clouds=vol`. NOT verified on iOS with clouds default-on — device check; if the phone stutters/OOMs, `?clouds=0` and dial `cloudcount`. (2) **Shapes default ON for FRESH scans ONLY** (`_freshScanBuild = source instanceof File || meta.debugWorld`): saved worlds stay param-only ON PURPOSE — loading an old fine world under default-on shapes + autosave would irreversibly coarsen its save. `?shapes=1` forces on any world, `?shapes=0` disables. (3) **shapeFitMap now PERSISTS** (supersedes the non-persisted caveat): encoded at the shapesApplied stamp as `meta.shapeFits` (Int16 quads [x,y,z,bits]; off bits 0-2 set→0.5, size bits 3-5 set→0.5 else 1; ~585KB for the 73k-cell debug world) and decoded back into shapeFitMap on shapesApplied reload — saved shapes worlds keep their sub-cell detail. Whole-cell semantics make it stable (digs orphan entries harmlessly, places add full cubes). (4) **Latent coarsening has a SINGLE owner** — the pre-pass block no longer touches result.latentCols (it would double-coarsen the File path, whose meta encodes AFTER the pre-pass); the decode site coarsens (center-point) AND re-persists the coarse Int32-quad encoding into meta.latentCols, so saves carry coarse latent + shapesApplied together. Verified no-param debug world: shapes log fires (258k→111k, 73k shaped), cloud sea + heroes + voxel clouds render, dirt keel proportional. Frame in Telegram. Build clean, errors clean.

## Cloud SEA + longer-distance sky (Aug 4, commit 10d086b, SHIPPED staging+prod, default with ?clouds=1)
KJ reference: floating-palace frames (cloud ocean below the viewer, distant masses, open sky). In `tw-voxel-cloud.ts`: (1) **Cloud sea below the island** — a continuous 2-mesh Lambert DISC (inner solid + outer ring whose vertex colors fade to horizon haze) at `seaLevel −0.42×span`, under 300 y-flattened (0.45) swell clones from seaInner 0.3×span to seaOuter 7×span, pieces growing ×(1+rFrac·1.3) with distance. Knobs: `?seacount=0` disables, `?sealevel=`, `?seaouter=`; configure() re-scatters on sea keys. (2) **Aerial haze in the cloud shader** (`uHaze`, cache key bumped VC): color-only smoothstep fade of far clouds toward `mix(pale-blue, uLight, 0.4)` from 0.5→0.95 of the sea reach — kills the hard far edge, reads as scale. (3) **Sky band re-based ABOVE the sea + longer distances** (the old low-anchored scatter merged with the sea into a 360° whiteout at eye level): non-heroes now float `topY×(0.16+0.45r)`, non-hero towers `topY×(0.08+0.12r)`, inner 2.2→3.4, outer 6→8, count 50→26, heroes pushed to 0.72–1.05 of the band so open-sky gaps exist between them; heroes keep sea-level bases so they RISE OUT of the ocean (the Laputa read). Lab-verified day (sea + hero mass + open sky) and dusk (sea catches the warm low sun, backlit tower) — `debug-shots/cloud-sea/`. NOT yet done: sea uses full-res GLB clones (~300 draws — if device stutters, lo-GLB or InstancedMesh sea is the known optimization); distant island silhouettes on the horizon (reference ingredient #3) not started.

## Clouds not flat white — sea + mass tonal shading (Aug 4, commit fb3557a, SHIPPED staging+prod)
KJ: "Cloud system should also not be flat white." Two additions in `tw-voxel-cloud.ts`: (1) **Sea-disc cloud-ocean shading** — the flat Lambert discs now get an onBeforeCompile patch (`tinyworldSeaDiscV1`, uniforms `uSeaTime`/`uSeaSpan` — uSeaTime ticked in update()): two fbm scales modulate diffuse into bright rounded lobe tops vs blue-grey troughs (crevice `vec3(0.36,0.45,0.68)`), drifting slowly. Texture only — the real sun/hemi still do all lighting through the Lambert path (doctrine-clean). (2) **Low-frequency tonal variation on ALL cloud masses** in the cloud shader (cache key → VDtone): `tone = smoothstep(0.42,0.60, fbm(wpos*0.4/span))` scales brightness 0.70–1.10 and cool-shifts the low end — some masses bright cream, some cooler blue-grey, like varied cloud depths. **KEY LESSON (cost 3 wasted rounds): raw fbm clusters near 0.5 — WIDE smoothstep bands render as near-uniform mush; tighten the band around the mean (e.g. 0.44–0.57) to get visible spatial contrast.** Also: the lab's `until ls out/frame.png` wait races against a STILL-ALIVE previous page re-posting stale frames — always `agent-browser close --all`, rm frame+DONE.txt, then open and wait for DONE.txt. Verified day (rolling blue-grey troughs between lit lobes) + dusk (deep shadow valleys, warm-lit swells) — `debug-shots/cloud-sea/sea-{day,dusk}-toned.png`. If KJ wants the tone stronger/weaker, the levers are the `_crev` color, the `_lit` smoothstep band, and the tone mix range — could be lifted to a `?cloudtone=` knob.

## Fidelity-neutral perf rounds (Aug 5)

Standing rule: perf work must be fidelity-neutral (never lower quality for fps); every round is verified with a same-pose pixel A/B against the animation noise floor (~3.7 RMSE on the debug world — grass wind/workers/HUD) plus structural counters. **`window.__twPerf` is a MEASUREMENT-ONLY probe** in the render loop (`tw-staging-app.tsx`): frame-time EMA + whole-frame `renderer.info` totals (`autoReset=false` + manual reset at loop top so counts accumulate across ALL composer passes, not just the last). `__twPerf.sample()` → `{emaMs, fps, calls, triangles, ...}`. SwiftShader headless frame TIME is not device-representative (fragment-bound, ~4s/frame) — judge structure (draw calls, triangles) headless, feel on device.

- **Round 1 (commit 8614f21): GTAO reuses the shared normal/depth G-buffer** — with RayGI on, GTAO no longer re-renders the scene for its own G-buffer (`setGBuffer(normalTarget)`, prepass guard widened, normalTarget bumped 8-bit→HalfFloat to keep AO byte-identical). One full-scene geometry pass eliminated per GI frame. Verified: base-vs-opt RMSE 3.80 ≈ 3.70 noise floor. Escape hatch `?gtaogbuf=0`.
- **Round 2 (commit 78b1ddd): voxel cloud population INSTANCED** — `tw-voxel-cloud.ts` scatter records sub-pieces as instances (same math, same PRNG order → bit-identical layout) into ≤9 `InstancedMesh` buckets (one per GLB piece geometry) + 2 sea discs, replacing ~700 cloned meshes (each was a draw call + matrixWorld update + transparent-sort entry, ×2 with the GI normal prepass). Per-frame in `update()`: CPU frustum culling (conservative sphere = piece radius + center offset — never over-culls) and BACK-TO-FRONT instance write so feathered-edge alpha keeps the sorted blend; per-cloud bob applied to matrix element [13]; drift stays on group rotation. Shader folds `instanceMatrix` into `vWPos`/`vWN` (`modelMatrix·instanceMatrix` = old per-mesh modelMatrix — bit-identical). **Measured (debug world, fixed pose, bob/drift frozen): 574 → 136 draw calls/frame (cloud share 449 → 11), triangles within 0.5% (culling parity), pixel A/B RMSE 4.11 vs ~3.7 floor with residual diff on animated elements (sea-disc uSeaTime drift, grass, workers) + faint feather-edge fringes only.** Cross-bucket blend order is fixed (9 buckets, three sorts them by bounding-sphere depth) — within-bucket order is correct; verified invisible in the A/B. `configure()` re-scatter rebuilds buckets. NOTE: `__twClouds.mesh.children` is now ~11 (buckets + discs), not per-cloud objects — diagnostics counting children must read `c.count`/`instanceMatrix.count` per bucket instead.
- **Round 3 (commit bcb3a69): beauty + normal prepass merged into ONE MRT geometry pass** — with GI/GTAO/outline on, the scene was rendered twice (beauty, then a full-scene MeshNormalMaterial prepass into `normalTarget`). Now the beauty render writes color + view-normals into an MRT target (`gTarget`, count 2, shared 32-bit depth) in one pass; a fullscreen **recon pass** (`_reconMat`, edge-aware neighbor pick) rebuilds the exact prepass-format normal/depth buffer that RayGI/GTAO/outline consume, so their inputs are format-identical. Gate: `_onePass` active whenever the G-buffer is needed; **`?prepass=1` restores the old two-pass path** (escape hatch). GTAO reads `gTarget.depthTexture` in one-pass mode. **Measured (fixed pose): frame time 4288→3265ms (−24%, SwiftShader — structural signal only), beauty-pass calls/triangles unchanged (136→134, ±0.03% tris — nothing drawn differently), pixel A/B RMSE 4.71 vs ~3.7–4.1 floor with captures 17 real-time min apart (sun drift); diff map residual ONLY on animated content (sea-disc drift blobs + wandering worker), zero structural/terrain/GI-shadow delta.** Also: `src/tw-raygi.ts` is now git-tracked in the project (was Space-mirror-only since Jul 25).
- **Scouted, not built:** GTAO's idle internal G-buffer VRAM (~6MB, fragile to dispose); per-frame allocations in the hot loop measured negligible (~9 small objects/frame).
- **Sim-tick CPU audit (Aug 5, commit 33dd801): `__twPerf.buckets` per-system main-thread timers** — mark-style contiguous segments through the render loop (greedy / blockphys / clouds / spring / irrig / env / workers / sentinel / shadow / fx / anim / render), EMA ms/frame, measurement-only, read via `__twPerf.sample().buckets` in console. **FINDING (synthetic world, live sim + clouds, headless server CPU): the sim ticks are near-free** — workers 0.1–0.6ms, spring ~0 (cadence-bounded), clouds ~0 (uniform writes; voxel ring instanced), blockphys/greedy/fx/shadow all <0.1ms. A Minecraft-style sim-distance ring would save <1ms/frame → NOT worth building for perf. **The one outlier: `irrig` 14–17ms EMA** — `recomputeMoisture` (8s cadence, `?irrigms=`) rebuilds `soilTop`/`soilSlotCache` from scratch over EVERY grass slot (string splits per cell) and runs an O(soil-cols × water-cols) nearest-water scan; on device this is a periodic hitch every ~8s, and it scales with world size (a 400k-block scan world would spike far harder than synthetic). Cheapest fix (not built): skip the recompute when the water-cell set + grass mesh version are unchanged — steady-state water (full lake, no rain) makes the whole recompute redundant; next tier: incremental soilTop maintenance on edit hooks + multi-source BFS from water instead of the S×W scan. NOTE: headless SwiftShader inflates ONLY the render bucket (JS submission + soft raster); tick buckets are honest CPU ms. Debug-scan world was too slow to sample headless (~0.4fps load); Mac numbers = KJ console check on staging.

- **Voxy-style neighbor LOD (Aug 6, commit 4910259, SHIPPED staging+prod bundle):** the multi-world neighbor tier no longer renders as a decimated instanced box cloud — `buildLODMesh` downsamples the neighbor's layer buffers into an F³-cell grid (dominant material per cell, 10% occupancy threshold, auto-coarsens F until max grid dim ≤160), then emits ONE face-culled vertex-colored mesh: exact silhouette, real palette (`NLOD_COLORS` → PAL), per-cell deterministic brightness jitter, correct parallax. Default ON; `?nlod=0` restores the legacy box cloud, `?nlodf=N` sets the base factor (default 4; "full" tier halves it). Promote/demote by player distance reuses the same `setTier` rebuild. **Verified live headless (localhost:3099, `?debugWorld=1&raygi=0&nall=1`):** GOLDEN PLAYTEST placed as neighbor — tier full, F=2, **90,896 tris in 1 draw call**, palette/silhouette confirmed by visibility-toggle A/B (`debug-shots/perf/neighborlod-ab.png`, sent to KJ). Known spike artifacts: (1) origin id was empty so a world at distM 0 placed ON TOP of the debug island — production should skip neighbors <~20 m (likely the same site); (2) probe access: scene is `window.__scene` (camera is NOT parented into it), summary at `window.__twNeighbors`. Next (per memory): incremental coarse-mesh update when a neighbor world is edited/merged.

- **Static/dynamic shadow split + sun quantization + targeted invalidation (Aug 6, commit f4a9906, SHIPPED staging+prod bundle):** the per-frame shadow cost rework KJ approved. (1) **Split** — the static world (terrain/greedy/instanced layers) renders into the CACHED sun/moon shadow maps with `light.shadow.autoUpdate=false`; movers (Sentinel/workers/drone GLBs + carried meshes + instanced worker bodies, tagged via `_twDynCaster` + registered in the dyn list) render each frame into a separate small dyn map ("dyn rig") that follows the player. Static map repaints ONLY on: block edits (`__twMarkShadowDirty`, exported + wired into `removeBlockFrom`/`placeAt`), sun-step commits, box recommits (deadband), and a short burst after invalidation. (2) **Sun quantization** — the sun direction commits in coarse steps instead of continuous drift (sun moves ~0.25°/min; steps are visually invisible), so idle worlds stop repainting for solar motion. (3) **Targeted invalidation replaces the 30-frame blanket safety repaint.** Diagnostics: `window.__twShadowStats` = `{staticSun, staticMoon, dynPaints, sunCommits}` (cumulative paint counters). **Verified live headless (localhost:3099 debug world, `?raygi=0`):** steady state = `staticSun` FROZEN across multiple 5–8s windows (zero world shadow repaints while the render loop runs); `dynPaints` ticks only when movers actually move (idle Sentinel ≈ 0.4 paints/s, not per-frame); `__twMarkShadowDirty()` → immediate static repaint burst then re-freeze; `sunCommits` stepped ~2× during warm-up then held flat. **Pixels:** world cast shadows from the CACHED map are correct (cliff shade crisp on grass, no acne/missing shadows — `/tmp/shadow-split-verify.png`, sent to KJ); the walking worker's own dyn shadow could not be cleanly isolated in-frame (blended into cliff shade) — **mover-shadow look + walk feel = KJ device check.** CDP note: close-framing the mech starved eval permanently this session (screenshots still worked); use far framing for stats reads.

- **Dynamic-shadow + cloud-update redundancy caches (Aug 6):** `tw-shadow-split.ts` now fingerprints the quantized light-space matrix plus registered mover matrices and skips the 2048² mover-map clear/render when the result is unchanged (`dynSkips` added to `__tw.shadowSplit().dyn`). Sub-texel translation and sub-0.03° transform noise intentionally hash to the same value; actual poses, caster visibility/count, shadow-box changes, and sun/moon direction changes invalidate automatically. `tw-voxel-cloud.ts` moves the exact per-cloud sinusoidal bob into the vertex shader (`aCloudBob` phase/rate + `uCloudMotion`), so motion remains continuous while CPU frustum culling, transparent sorting, and instance-buffer uploads run only after meaningful camera/bank motion or a 750ms safety refresh. Conservative cull spheres include the full bob amplitude. Build passed; clouds-lab loaded all 11 pieces with the new shader and no loop error. Device FPS/shadow-motion feel remains KJ verification.

- **Hybrid shapes render — greedy full cubes + face-masked instanced fits (Aug 6, commits 9bca201 + 16bb1f0, SHIPPED staging+prod bundle):** fixes the per-block grid lines that reappeared when the conquest shapes path forced everything instanced (root cause: every cell rendered as a full 6-face box; buried near-black-AO side faces between touching cubes leak at shared edges via depth/edge sampling — greedy never had this because it deletes buried faces and merges coplanar tops). (1) **Greedy re-enabled in shapes mode** — `USE_GREEDY_GROUND`/`USE_GREEDY_WALL` no longer check `SHAPES_ON`; `_splitFits` partitions dirt + wall into full-cube cells (→ greedy meshers, unchanged files) vs `shapeFitMap` cells (→ addLayer instancing). Fitted wall cells are EXCLUDED from ground `occluders` so the ground face behind a partial shape stays visible (dig needs no reveal). Compiled cache variant gains `sf<fitCount>`. This also fixes the saved-shapes-world reload path, which previously rendered fits as full cubes inside greedy chunks. (2) **Per-instance face-occlusion mask** (`aFaceMask`, 6-bit float attr next to the AO attrs): a face fully covered by an opaque never-fading neighbor (dirt/grass/dryGrass/snow/wet/trunks/wall/hidden_dirt — NOT water/leaves/ceiling, which are translucent/gappy/fading) collapses to a degenerate triangle in the vertex shader. Fitted cells only cull faces lying exactly on the cell-boundary plane, and a fitted NEIGHBOR must span the full shared plane. Masks clear on dig/settle detach (`_clearFaceMask`) and the 6 neighbors of any removed cell unmask (`_unmaskAround`) — conservative direction: unmasking can only re-draw geometry, never open a hole; recycled freeSlots are always mask-clean. Kills `?facemask=0` (instanced culling), `?greedyground=0&greedy=0` (greedy). **Verified live headless (localhost:3099 debug world, `?raygi=0&grass=0`):** hybrid split logged (debug world: dirt 11,190 fitted / 26,880 full→greedy 54k tris; wall 35,813 fitted / 10,402 full→greedy 44k tris; ceiling 11,991 masked), pixel A/B at identical camera vs the old path (`greedyground=0&greedy=0&facemask=0`): baseline shows the wall-face grid lines, hybrid shows a continuous smooth wall, silhouette steps intact (`debug-shots/shapes/wall-ab.png`, `grass-ab.png` — sent to KJ). `get_space_errors` clean. **Walk feel + edit-path (dig reveal) on device = KJ check.** NOTE: most wall cells in photogrammetry scans get non-full fits (thin shells shrink toward empty), so the instanced+mask path carries the wall majority; the mask is what kills their seams.
